(iru eṉṉum tuṇi viṉiccol)
poruḷ		S	Gk	L	Ger	E
irukkiṟēṉ		asmi	esmi	sum	bin	am
irukkiṟōm		’smas	esmes	’sumus	sind	are
irukkiṟāy		asi	eis	es	bist	art
irukkiṟīr(kaḷ)		stha	este	estis	seid	are
iruk-kiṟāṉ-ḷ,	
kiṉṟatu		asti	esti	est	ist	is
iruk-kiṟār(kaḷ)-
kiṉṟaṉa		santi	eisi	sunt	sind	are
itukāṟum kāṭṭap peṟṟavi eṭuttuk kāṭṭuc coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟu, vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr moḻi evvāṟu mēliyāriya moḻikaṭku iṉamāyirunta teṉpatit teḷi vāyk kaṇṭu koḷḷalām.  (va.va).
potuvāka, mēliyāriya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaṭkuḷ tiyūttāṉi yam muntiya niliyiyum, kirēkkam, cilāvōṉiyam mutaliyavi pintiya niliyiyum, kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, airōppāviṉ vaṭamēṟkōṭiyiliruntu teṉkīḻkkōṭi nōkki āriyamoḻi kaḷ paṭippaṭiyāyt tirintu vantiruppati aṟiyalām.
cila coṟkaḷ ip potuviyalpiṟku māṟākat tiyūttāṉiyattil mikat tirintuḷḷaṉa. itaṟku avi intiyāviṉiṉṟu mutalil airōppā ceṉṟuḷḷamiyē karaṇiyam.
e-ṭu: eṇṇup peyarkaḷum cilavuṟuppup peyarkaḷum.
āriya moḻikaḷuḷ mikat tirintatu kīḻiyāriyam eṉpatu muṉpu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
orucila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ mēliyāriya vaḻiyākavum nēraṭi yākavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukuntuḷḷaṉa.  (va.va.)
e-ṭu: rut (rudh) - mēlivaḻi
rakta (aratta) - nērvaḻi
arattam eṉṉum kurutip peyarē paṇpiyāku peyarāka ataṉ niṟattiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
2. pirākirutac coṟkaḷ
mēliyāriyattilum tamiḻilum teṉtiraviṭattilum illāta vētamoḻic coṟkaḷellām, pirākirutac coṟkaḷē.
v-L.: āti, kirāmam.  (va.va.)
mēḻam
mēḻam - mēṣa (i.vē.)
mēḻakam - mēṣaka.  (va.va:245)
moy
uṟaviṉar moy vikkumpōtu, maṇavīṭṭār tamakku muṉpu ceyta aḷavē ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu karuta vēṇṭuvatilli. tam celva nilikkēṟpak kūṭṭiyō kuṟittō ceyyalām: allākkāl atu vaṭṭiyillāk kaṭaṉpōliruntu taṉ ciṟappiyiḻakkum. maṇa vīṭṭārum uṟaviṉar niliyaṟintu perun taṉmiyāy iruntu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.  (ta.ti.61).
moḻi eṉṟāl eṉṉa?
oru vakuppār allatu nāṭṭār tam karuttip piṟarkkup pulappaṭuttaṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷum olittokutiyē moḻiyām. av voli colluñ coṟṟoṭarumāyirukkum. collum oreḻuttuc col īreḻuttuc col mutaliyavākap palavakittu, 
ōr oli vēṟu; ataṉāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷvēṟu. olikkum poruḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu varivaṭiveḻuttiṟkum olivaṭi veḻuttiṟkum uḷḷatē. kāṟṟu iyaṅkuvatiṉālum iru allatu pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu urāyvataṉālum ōci piṟakkum. av vōcikku iyalpāyp poruḷilli. av vōciyip poruḷuṇarttum aṭiyāḷamākak koṇṭatē moḻiyām. kāṟṟiyakkam veḷḷiṭiyiyaṅku vatum uyiri (pirāṇi)kaḷiṉ vāyvaḻi iyaṅkuvatum eṉa iru vakittu. ivaṟṟuḷ, piṉṉataṉ payaṉāya olikaḷē perum pālum moḻik karuviyām. olikku iyalpāyp poruḷuṇart tum taṉmi yiruppiṉ, ulakameṅkum eṉṟum oru moḻiyā yum atuvum kallāmalē aṟiyappaṭuvatāyu mirukkum. aṅṅaṉam iṉmiyaṟika.
moḻinūliṉ tiṟamaṟiyāta cilar, ōci nilippāṉatu ākavē ōcivaṭivāṉa moḻikaḷum nilippāṉavi eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu, irumpu nilippāṉatu; ākavē, irumpu vaṭivāṉa iyantiraṅkaḷum nilippāṉavi; putitāyuṇṭāyaviyalla eṉṟu kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatē.
ovvoru nāṭṭārum perumpālum vevvēṟu moḻiyip pēci varukiṉṟaṉar. oru nāṭṭār maṟṟoru nāṭṭāriṉ moḻi yik kaṟṟāloḻiyap pēca muṭiyātu. oru nāṭṭāruḷḷum kuḻantikaḷ periyōr vāyilāyk kēṭṭā loḻiyat tam tāy moḻiyip pēcamuṭiyātu. kuḻavip paruvattilēyē tam nāṭṭāriṉiṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭu moḻi vaḻaṅkāta allatu tāymoḻi vaḻaṅkāta iṭattil niliyāy vāḻntavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyip pēca muṭiyātu. muḻuc ceviṭar piṟar pēcu vatik kēṭka iyalāmiyālēyē perumpālum ūmiyarā kiṉṟaṉar. vilaṅkukaḷiyum paṟavikaḷiyum pōṉṟu, mikac cila olikaḷāl mikac cila karuttukaḷit terivittuk koṇṭa tamakkeṉa moḻiyillāta anākarika makkaḷum cila kāṭukaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru nāṭṭilum makkaḷiṉ nākarika vaḷarccikkēṟpa moḻiyum vaḷarntuḷḷatu.
oruvaṉukkup piṟappilēyē tāymoḻi yaṟivilli; atip pēcuntiṟaṉ allatu kaṟkuntiṟaṉ maṭṭumuṇṭu. ōrāṇṭuk kuḻanti ammā, appā, pācci, (pāl), cōcci (cōṟu) mutaliya peyarkaḷiyum, oṉṟariyāṇṭuk kuḻanti avaṟṟoṭu vā, pō mutaliya viṉikaḷiyum, īrāṇṭuk kuḻanti taṉ karuttiṟkuriya coṟkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum collak kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu. allatu kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟatu. mūvāṇṭuk kuḻanti naṉṟāyp pēcukiṟatu; piṉpu vīṭṭiviṭṭu veḷiyēṟip poruḷkaḷiyum ceytikaḷiyum aṟiya aṟiyac coṟ ṟokutiyum peruki varukiṟatu; ataṉpiṉ kalviyiṉāl pala coṟkaḷ putitāy aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam oruvaṉ vaḷara vaḷara, aṟivu perukap peruka, taṉ coṟṟokutiyi mikuttuk koṇṭē pōkiṉṟāṉ. oru kuḻanti taṉ peṟṟōriṭat tiṉiṉṟu pēcak kaṟṟukkoṇṭāṟpōla, oru nāṭṭār tam muṉṉōriṭattiṉiṉṟu pēcak kaṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
mēṟkūṟiya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, māntaṉ oru kālattil moḻi yillātiruntāṉ eṉpatum, moḻiyaminta piṉ vaḻivaḻip piṉṉō rellām muṉṉōriṭattiṉiṉṟu moḻiyik kaṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatum uyttuṇarappaṭum.
ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷellām ōriṭattiṉiṉṟē paravip pōyiṉar eṉpatum, oru tāyvayiṟṟiṉarē eṉpatum ārāycciyāṟ kaṇṭa muṭipukaḷām. māntaṉ mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya iṭattil makkaḷ moḻiyillāmalē cilakālam vāḻntu vantaṉar. moḻiyillāta niliyil cila muṟiyum moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ cila muṟiyumāka makkaḷ palaticikkum pala muṟi paravip pōyirukkiṉṟaṉar. moḻiyillātu pirintupōṉavar tām pōṉa iṭaṅkaḷil putitāyt tattamakku ēṟṟavāṟum iyaṉṟavāṟum iyaṉmoḻi kaḷi ākkikkoṇṭaṉar. moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ pirintu pōṉavar tattam cuṟṟuc cārpiṟkēṟpap putuc coṟkaḷi amittum taṭpaveppa nilikkēṟpap paḻañ coṟkaḷit tirittuṅ koṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, toṭarpuṭiya makkaḷellām toṭarpuṭiya moḻikaḷip pēci varuvatum, makkaḷ toṭar piṉ perumi ciṟumikkēṟpa avarkaḷ moḻikaḷiṉ toṭar pum mikkum kuṟintu miruppatum iyalpām. itaṟku, amerikka nīkirōvip pōla anākarika moḻiyarāyum kōvāp pōrttukkīciyarip pōlac ciṟupāṉmiyarāyu miruntu, ayaṉmoḻikaḷik kaṭippiṭippatu vilakkām.
moḻittōṟṟak koḷkikaḷ
aṟiviyaṟpaṭṭa moḻiyārāycci tōṉṟumuṉ, moḻiyiṉ allatu moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟattippaṟṟi aṟiñariṭip palvēṟu tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ iruntuvantaṉa. aviyāvaṉa:-
1. teyvak koḷki (Divine Theory)
itu iṟivaṉē moḻikaḷippaṭittāṉ eṉpatu. ituvē mutaṉmutal ellā nāṭukaḷilum koḷḷap peṟṟup piṉpu taḷḷappaṭṭatu.
iti maṟukkum ētukkaḷ:
1. 	moḻiyillāmalum cila vilaṅkāṇṭi makkaḷ uḷḷaṉar.
2. 	cilamoḻikaḷ ciṟiyaṉavākavum anākarikamākavum uḷḷaṉa.
3. 	evarum kaṟkāmal iyalpākat tam tāy moḻiyip pēca iyalātu.
4. 	makkaḷ muyaṟciyāl ciṟumoḻikaḷum perumoḻikaḷāka vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa.
5.	tiripiṉālum kalappiṉālum pala putumoḻikaḷ tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa.
6.	makkaḷellārum eṉṟum orēmoḻiyip pēcumāṟu iṟivaṉ ceytirukkalām.
7.	iṟivaṉ paṭittiruntāl moḻikaḷ iṉṟiruppatiṉum mikacciṟappākavum kuṟṟaṅkuṟiyaṟṟum iruntirukkum.
2. oppantak koḷki (Contract Theory)
	itu makkaḷ kūṭi iṉṉa oli iṉṉa poruḷuṇarttuka eṉṟu oppantañ ceytu koṇṭatu moḻi eṉpatu. itu urūcō koḷki.
itu veṇṇey taṭavik kokkup piṭikkuṅ katipōl taṉ maṟuppuk koḷkiyātalāl āyviṟkuriyataṉṟām.
3. tūṇṭuṇarccik koḷki (Instinct Theory)
itu, iyaṟkiyāka māntaṉiṭiyeḻunta tūṇṭuṇarcciyoli kaḷāl moḻitōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu. itu kāṇṭilakku koḷki.
ituvum iṟivaṉ paṭippuk koḷkipōl ēraṇamuṟikku ovvātatē.
4. kuṟippolik koḷki (Pooh-Pooh Theory)
itu māntaṉiṉ iṉpa tuṉpak kuṟippolikaḷāl moḻi tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
kuṟippolikaḷ, orucila karuttukkaḷiyēyaṉṟi ellāk karuttuk kaḷiyum uṇarttum coṟṟokutiyit tōṟṟu vikkum āṟṟalaṟṟavi yākum.
5. oppolik koḷki (Bow-wow Theory)
itu vilaṅku paṟavikaḷālum palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷālum eḻum olikaḷip piṉpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷik koṇṭu moḻi tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
oppolikaḷum ellā karuttukkaḷiyum uṇarttum coṟ kaḷip piṟappikkum āṟṟalaṟṟaviyē.
6. maṇiyolik koḷki (Ding-dong Theory)
itu, maṇiyaṭittavuṭaṉ oliyeḻuvatu pōl karuttut tōṉṟiya vuṭaṉ col veḷippaṭum eṉpatu. itu mākkacu mullar koṇṭu piṉpu viṭṭuviṭṭatu. colliṟkum poruḷiṟkum iyalpāṉa toṭarpuṇṭu eṉṉum vaḻūuk koḷkiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu itu.
7. ayāvuyirppuk koḷki (Yo-he-ho Theory)
itu, makkaḷ kūṭi uṭalivarutti oru vēliyic ceyyum pōtu mūccaṭakki veḷiyiṭum olikaḷāl moḻi tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu. itu nāyrē koḷki.
ituvum uṇarcciyolik koḷki yottatē.
8. cikik koḷki (Gesture Theory)
itu, karuttaṟivikkum iru vāyilkaḷuḷ ciki muntiyatu eṉṟum, olivaṭivāṉa moḻi ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭateṉṟum, kūṟuvatu.
cikiyillāmal oliyum oliyillāmaṟ cikiyum irutiṇi yuyirkaḷiṭattum tōṉṟuvatāl, ivviraṇṭiṟkum oṉṟupaṭṭa uṟavu illiyeṉpatu teḷivu.
oliyoṭu vaṭiya vāycceykiyāl moḻi tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatu (Mouth gesture theory) cikik koḷkiyiṉ maṟṟoru vaki. itik koṇṭavar riccārṭu pēkeṟṟu.
9. toṭarpuk koḷki (Contact Theory)
itu, mutalil piṟaroṭu toṭarpu koḷḷak kuraliṭuvatum, piṉpu potuvākak karivatum ataṉpiṉ oruvaric ciṟappāka viḷippatum, ataṉpiṉ oṉṟic ceyya ēvuvatumāka moḻi tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatu. itu revecu (Revesz) koḷki.
itu, piṟaroṭu toṭarpu koḷvatil nērum uḷanikaḻccikaḷi varicip paṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟatēyaṉṟi, moḻi tōṉṟiya vakiyi viḷakkukiṉṟilatu.
10. pāṭṭolik koḷki (Musical Theory)
itu, muntiyal māntar viṉi muṭivil tam karuttip pāṭ ṭoliyāka allatu iṉṉiciyolit toṭarāka veḷip paṭuttiṉar eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu alakuṭikkum neṭuñ coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum, avi uyarnta iciyilum alakilum olittaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟē piṟkālac coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum, kūṟuvatu.
itu cecupperceṉ koḷki.
vāykku vantapaṭi uḷaṟum iciyolikaḷiṉiṉṟu, oḻuṅkāṉa peyar viṉi yiṭic coṟkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ palvēṟu vaṭivu kaḷum tōṉṟiṉaveṉpatu, muṭceṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu mukkaṉikaḷum tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatiyē okkum.
iṉi, uṇarvolikkoḷki (Emotion Theory) eṉpatum oṉṟuṇṭu.
iṅṅaṉam 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 20-ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari mēli moḻinūlār moḻit tōṟṟatti āyntuvantatum, iṉṉum uṇmi kāṇa iyalātavarāyiruk kiṉṟaṉar. itaṟku muntiyal iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻi aṭippaṭiyākak koḷḷātu pintiyal tirimoḻiyākiya camaṟkirutatti allatu āriyatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatē karaṇiyam.
muḻittalmoḻi, (Inarticulate Speech), iḻittalmoḻi (Articulate Speech) eṉa moḻi iruvakippaṭum ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu uṇarcciyolikaḷ, viḷiyolikaḷ mutaliya eḻuvaki yolikaḷālum, piṉṉatu cuṭṭoli vaḷarcciyālum, āyiṉa.
moḻi tōṉṟiya vaki
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām iyaṉmoḻikaḷ (Primitive Languages) tirimoḻikaḷ (Derivative Languages) eṉa iruvakippaṭum. oṉṟiyum cārātu tāṉē iyalpākat tōṉṟiya moḻi iyaṉmoḻi; oru moḻiyiṉiṉṟu tirinta moḻi tirimoḻi.
moḻikaḷellām orēyaḷavāṉa colvaḷamuṭiyaṉavalla. makkaḷiṉ karuttu allatu aṟivu veḷippāṭṭoliyē col. uṇpatum uṭuppatum tuyilvatumē toḻilākak koṇṭa anākarika malivāṇariṉ allatu kāṭṭu vāṇariṉ karuttukkaḷ mikamikac cila. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ moḻikaḷiluḷḷa coṟkaḷum mikamikac cila. pala toḻiluḷḷa nākarikam vāynta marutavāṇariṉ allatu nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ mikappala. ataṉāl, avarkaḷiṉ moḻiccoṟkaḷum mikappala. ettoḻiliyum anākarikaṉ ceyyumuṟikkum nākarikaṉ ceyyumuṟikkum vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. ivviyalpuppaṭi, anākarikaṉ tiruttamiṉṟiyum nākarikaṉ tiruttamāyum pēcukiṟāṉ. ākiyāl, anākarika nākarika makkaḷiṉ moḻic coṟkaḷ ciṉmi paṉmiyāl maṭṭumaṉṟit tiruntāmiyum tiruttamumpaṟṟiyum vēṟupaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. tiruttamum karuttuttiruttam coṟṟiruttam eṉa iru pāṟpaṭṭatu. ākavē, oru nāṭṭāriṉ nākarikatti yaṟivataṟku avaratu moḻipōṟ ciṟanta karuvi allatu vāyil piṟitoṉ ṟilli.
oru moḻiyār anākarikarāyiṉ, avar piṟa nākarika makkaḷoṭu kūṭumpōtu, tam anākarika nilikku allatu collaḷavukkut takkavāṟu, nākarikariṉ coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tirukkum. iḵtu iyaṉmoḻi tirimoḻi ākiya iruvaki moḻikkum ēṟkum. ēṉeṉiṉ, coṟkuṟivum coṉṉiṟivum avviruvakikkumuṇṭu.
pala moḻikaḷ oṉṟāyk kalappiṉ kalavi moḻiyām. atu irumoḻik kalavi paṉmoḻik kalavi eṉa iruvakippaṭum. oru moḻiyil piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷirunta māttiriyāṉē, atik kalavi yeṉṉamuṭiyātu. uṇmiyil iṉṟiyamiyāta kāṟpaṅkuc coṟkaḷiyāvatu kaṭaṉkoṇṭa moḻiyiyē kalavi moḻiyeṉṉalām.
iyaṉmoḻi vakiyil, tiruntāmoḻikkut teṉkaṇṭa (ātirēliya) teṉ āppirikka moḻikaḷiyum tirunti moḻikkut tamiḻiyum; tirimoḻi vakiyil, tiruntā moḻikkut tuṭākōṭṭā mutaliya moḻikaḷiyum tiruntiya moḻikkut teluṅku karunaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷiyum; kalavi moḻi vakiyil, tiruntāmoḻikkuk kōṇṭi patri mutaliya moḻi kaḷiyum tiruntiya moḻikkut teluṅku āṅkilam ātaliya moḻikaḷiyum; iru moḻikkalavikku marāṭṭi teluṅku mutaliya moḻikaḷiyum paṉmoḻik kalavikku āṅkilam inti mutaliya moḻikaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟalām.
moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟatti ōr iyaṉmoḻiyāl aṟiyamuṭiyumēyaṉṟit tirimoḻiyāl aṟiyamuṭiyātu. moḻi nūṟperumpulavar mākcumullar cīṉamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum teṉ kaṇṭat tīvukaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟumē moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟatti yuṇarntār.
ulakap perumoḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻi tiripu mutirnta tātaliṉ, ataṉik koṇṭu moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟattiyaṟiya virumpuvār, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟavaṉ eṉṟu koḷḷu pavarēyāvar. tamiḻō iyaṉ moḻiyāyum mikat tiruntiyatāyum moḻi vaḷarcciyiṉ pala nilikaḷik kāṭṭak kūṭiyatāyu miruttaliṉ, moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟatti yaṟitaṟkut taliciṟanta vāyilākum.
moḻi mutalāvatu taṉittaṉi olikaḷāka allatu acikaḷākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu. oru karuttu ippōtu pala coṟkaḷuḷḷa vākkiya māka amintālum, atu mutalāvatu vākkiyamākat tōṉṟa villi. ēṉeṉiṉ, vākkiya amippukku vēṇṭum coṟkaḷel lām appōtu illi. maṉitaṉukkuk karuttup perukaperuka olikaḷum allatu coṟkaḷum perukik koṇṭē vantaṉa. meḷḷa meḷḷac ciṟitu ciṟitāyp perukik koṇṭu vanta avvolik kūṭṭamē moḻiyākum.
oruvaṉiṉ uḷḷattileḻum karuttu vēṟu, ataṉip piṟarkkup pulappaṭuttum oli vēṟu. oru karuttip pulappaṭutta oru vākkiya voliyē vēṇṭum eṉṉum yāppuṟavilli. naṉṟāyp pēccuk kaṟṟa piḷḷi kūṭa ammā! eṉakkuc cōṟu vēṇṭum eṉṉum nāṟcoṟṟoṭari ammā! cōṟu eṉṟu iru collil aṭakkiviṭukiṟatu. periyōr pēccilum, ‘nī aṅkē pōṉāyā?’ eṉṉum viṉāviṟkup ‘pōṉēṉ’ allatu ‘ām’ eṉṉum viṭiyum ‘avar eṉṟikku varuvār?  eṉṉum viṉāviṟku nāḷikku eṉṉum viṭiyum ovvoru collāyē niṉṟu ovvoru karuttit terivikkiṉṟaṉa. oru cila coṟkaḷiyē kaṟṟa kuḻanti, eṉakkup pāl vēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ karutti, pā pāci pālu pāl eṉṉuñ col vaṭivaṅ kaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉālēyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu. ataṟkumēṟ colla ataṉiṭam coṟkaḷilli. inniliyi ayaṉmoḻiyi arikuṟiyāyk kaṟṟavariṭamum kāṇalām. āṅkilam naṉṟā yaṟiyāta oruvaṉ ōr āṅkilak kaṭikkāraṉiṭam ceṉṟāl, ‘eṉakku oru katti vēṇṭum’ eṉpati “mankina” eṉṟum, ‘itaṉ vili eṉṉa?  eṉpati “Price?” eṉṟum orē collāl kuṟikka lāmaṉṟō? ivaṉ taṉ moḻikkup periyavaṉāyiṉum ayaṉ moḻikkuk kuḻantiyāyirukkiṉṟāṉ. kuḻantiyum nōyāḷi yum perumpālum ovvoru collālēyē tam karutti yaṟivikkiṉṟaṉar. periyōr tāymoḻiyiṟ pēcum pēccil orucolviṭi toki vākkiyaṅkaḷākak karutap paṭalām. āṉāl, ayaṉmoḻi naṉṟāyaṟiyātāṉ ammoḻiyiṟ pēci ṉāl, tāymoḻiyil tōṉṟum coṟkaḷ avvayaṉ moḻi yil tōṉṟāmiyum, oru kuḻanti pēciṉ tāy moḻiyilum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟāmiyum uṇarka.
moḻi nirampāta muntukāla (Primitive) māntaṉ, karuttu veḷiyīṭṭil kuḻantipōṉṟavaṉ. avaṉ ā ī vā pō kī mē mutaliya cila taṉi yacikaḷālum cila cikikaḷālumē, taṉ karuttip pulappaṭuttiṉāṉ. ivviyalpi iṉṟum cila malivāṇariṭamum teṉkaṇṭattīvāriṭamum kāṇalām. inniliyi oruvāṟu vilaṅku nilikku oppiṭalām.
ōr iyaṉmoḻi 1. acinili 2. coṉṉili eṉa iru nili kaḷiyuṭiyatu. aciniliyāvatu peyar viṉi iṭi eṉa ilakkaṇac coṟṟaṉmippaṭāmal taṉittaṉi yacikaḷ ovvoru poruḷiyuṇarttum nili; coṉṉiliyāvatu ilakkaṇac coṟṟaṉmippaṭṭa nili. atum  1. aciccol (kaṇ, ā) 2. puṇarccol (kaṇṇavaṉ) 3. pakucol (kaṇṇaṉ)  4. toṭarccol eṉa nāṉku nilikaḷiyuṭiyatu. avaṟṟuḷ toṭarccol 1. tokānilittoṭar (taṇṇīr, ceṅkāl nāri) 2. toki nilittoṭar (yāy, cāttanti) eṉa iru vakiyāṉatu.
moḻi vaḷaravaḷara eḻuttukkaliyum vaḷarntuvarum. ulakil mutalāvatu paṭa veḻuttum (Hieroglyph) piṉpu karutteḻuttum (Ideograph) tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉpiṉ aci yeḻuttum (Syllabic Character) oliyeḻuttum (Phonetic Character) muṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉaviyiraṇṭum karuttōṭum piṉṉavi yiraṇ ṭum moḻiyōṭum toṭar puṟṟavi. peruvaḷarcci yaṭinta oru nākarika makkaḷ moḻiyiltāṉ piṉṉīreḻuttuk kaḷik kāṇalām.
oru moḻiyi allatu pēccip pala vākkiyaṅkaḷākavum oru vākkiyattip pala coṟkaḷākavum, oru collip pala eḻuttukkaḷākavum pakuttukkoḷvatu, oru moḻi peru vaḷarcciyaṭintapiṉ ataṉ ilakkaṇatti yeṭuttukkūṟum pōtu nikaḻvatākum. moḻittōṟṟam vēṟu: ilakkaṇat tōṟṟam vēṟu. moḻi aciyolikaḷāyt tōṉṟiṉatē yaṉṟi eḻuttolikaḷāyt tōṉṟavilli. eḻuttē ilakkaṇa vakiyil oru moḻiyiṉ alaku (Unit); vākkiyam alakaṉṟu. ēṉeṉiṉ, vākkiyam oru kuṟitta aḷavuḷḷataṉṟu. mūvaki vākkiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa kalappu (Complex) vākkiyam varampiṟan tōṭuvatu. maṉumuṟikaṇṭa vācakattil pala pakkaṅkaḷ varukiṉṟa atikāraṅkaḷ cila ovvoru vākkiyamāka amin tuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam oru puttakam muḻumiyum orē vākkiyamākkalām. puṇar (Compound)  vākkiyamum varampiṟantatē. iṉi, taṉi (Simple) vākkiyatti eṭuttukkoṇṭālum atuvum pala karuttuk kaḷit taḻuvakkūṭiyatākiṉṟatu. ikkaranti māṇavaṉ iṉiya tamiḻ naṭi eḷitāy eḻutuvāṉ eṉṉum vākkiyattil 1. karanti māṇavaṉ 2. iṉiya tamiḻ 3. eḷitāy eḻutuvāṉ eṉak kuṟintatu mūṉṟu karuttukkaḷiruttal kāṇka. pala karuttuk kaḷik kūṟum kalappu vākkiyaṅkaḷiyum puṇarvākkiyaṅkaḷi yum poruḷ keṭāmal taṉi vākkiyaṅkaḷāka māṟṟavu muṭiyu meṉpati, āṅkila vilakkaṇaṅkaḷil kaṇṭu teḷika. iṉi, mūvaki vākkiyamuṅ kalanta kalavi (Mixed) vākkiyam eṉpatum oṉṟuṇṭeṉavaṟika.
moḻinūṟpaṭi nōkkiṉ, ovvoru pakāc collum ov voru karuttiyum ovvoru paku collum paṟpala karuttuk kaḷiyum kuṟippaṉavākum. ā vā kaṇ eṉṉum pakāc coṟkaḷ ovvoru karuttiyē kuṟittaṉa. ākkam varuvāṉ kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟa pakucoṟkaḷ paṟpala karuttukkaḷik kuṟittaṉa. piṉṉa vaṟṟuḷ, varuvāṉ eṉpatu mūṉṟu karuttukkaḷiyum ēṉiya ivvirukaruttukkaḷiyum kuṟittaṉa. varutaṟṟoḻilik kuṟikkum vā eṉṉum pakutiyum etirkālatti yuṇarttum v eṉṉum iṭiniliyum, āṇpāliyuṇarttum aṉ eṉṉum īṟum cērntē varuvāṉ eṉṉum col uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ākavē, pakuti vikuti iṭinili eṉṉum colluṟuppukkaḷuṅ kūṭa ovvoru poruḷiyuṇarttuvaṉavāyiṉa. iṉi, pakāc colluṅkūṭap pala poruḷkaḷi yuṇartta iṭamuṇṭu. kaṇ (kaḷ >f©) eṉṉum col karumiyeṉṉum kuṇamum ati yuṭiya kaṇ eṉṉum uṟuppum ākiya iruporuḷkaḷi yuṇart tal kāṇka. karumi eṉṉum kuṇattimaṭṭum karutuvatu poruḷum, atu karumi yuṭiyatu eṉakkarutuvatu karuttumākum. Mfnt., ovvoru poruḷum karuttākavum māṟakkūṭiyatu eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
neyyē toṉṉikkātāram eṉpatu pōlat tiripiṟ ciṟanta vaṭamoḻiyi iyaṉmoḻiyākak koḷvārē, iṅṅaṉam vākkiyatti moḻiyalakākak koṇṭu iṭarppaṭuvar. mēlum, vaṭamoḻiyi ulaka mutaṉ mūlamoḻiyākak koṇṭu valippavar moḻi nūṉ māṇavarātaṟkuṅ kūṭat takutiyaṟṟa varāvar.
ā, ī, ū eṉṉum olikaḷi moḻi mūla volikaḷākak kūṟuvatu iṉṟu mali yēṟiviṭṭateṉiṉ, atu kuṉṟiṉ mēliṭṭa viḷakkup pōla viḷaṅkutaṟku maliyēṟi viṭṭateṉka.
moḻinūl
1. moḻi
moḻiyāvatu makkaḷ tam karutti oruvarukkoruvar veḷiyiṭu taṟkuk karuviyākum olittokuti.
2. moḻivaki
ulakamoḻikaḷ mottam 860 eṉṟu palpi (Balbi) eṉpavar tamatu tiṇippaṭattil (Atlas) kuṟikkiṉṟār. mākmullar (Max Muller) avi toḷḷāyirattiṟkuk kuṟiyā eṉkiṟār. (Lectures on the Science of Language, Vol. I, p. 27). ēṟattāḻa āyiram eṉiṉum tavaṟākātu.
moḻikaḷiyellām, palappala muṟiyil, palappala vakiyāka vakukkalām. av vakikaḷāvaṉa:
1. acinili (Monosyllabic or Isolating), puṇarnili (Compounding), pakucoṉṉili (Inflectional or Polysyllabic), tokunili (Synthetic), paltokunili (Polysynthetic) allatu koḷuvunili (Agglutinative), pirinili (Analytical) eṉṟu aṟuvakiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟi. avaṟṟuḷ,
aciniliyāvatu, coṟkaḷellām ōraciyāka niṟpatu.
kāṭṭu: vaḷ, cel, vā, pō, tamiḻ, moḻi,
cīṉamoḻi acinilikkuc ciṟantatākum.
puṇarniliyāvatu, iru taṉiccoṟkaḷ puṇarntu niṟpatu.
kā: tamiḻmoḻi, marumakaṉ.
turēṉiya moḻikaḷiṟ cīṉamoḻintavi (piṉṉiyam (Finnish), haṅkēriyam (Hungarian), turukkiyam (Turkish), tamiḻ mutaliyaṉa) puṉar nilikkuc ciṟantaviyāyc collappaṭum.
pakucoṉṉiliyāvatu, puṇarnilic coṟkaḷil oṉṟu kuṟukit taṉittaṉmiyiḻantu, colluṟuppāy niṟpatu.
kā: marumāṉ, villavaṉ (vil + avaṉ), villāṉ, pakucoṉṉi likku āriyamoḻikaḷ ciṟantaṉavākak kūṟappaṭum.
tokuniliyāvatu, puṇarniliccoṟkaḷiṉ iṭiyil, eḻuttuk kaḷum acikaḷum tokkuniṟpatu.
kā: ātaṉ + tanti = ānti: peru + makaṉ (perumāṉ) - pirāṉ, allatu pemmāṉ.
paltokuniliyāvatu, paṉmoḻit toṭariṉiṭiyē pala acikaḷ allatu coṟkaḷ tokku niṟpatu.
kā: nacceḷḷiyār (nal + ceḷḷi + avar), cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar. amerikkamoḻikaḷ paltokunilikkuc ciṟantaṉa vākak kūṟappaṭum.
mekcika (Mexican) moḻiyil ‘ achichillacachocan’ eṉṉum paltokunilit toṭar, nīr civantiruppatāl makkaḷ aḻum iṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatām . (yaṭava-nīr, uhiuhṭiviu -civanta,  tlactl - māntaṉ, chorea -mG) ( Historical outlines of English Accidence, p. 2.)
piriniliyāvatu, pakucoṉṉilic coṟkaḷ īṟiḻantu niṟpatu.
kā: vantēṉ - vantu (maliyāḷam); brekan (O.E.) - break (Infinitive).
2. mūlamoḻi (Parent Language), kiḷimoḻi (Daughter Language), uṭaṉmoḻi (Sister Language) eṉa mūvakiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟi.
tirāviṭak kuṭumpattil, tamiḻ mūlamoḻi; teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam (karunaṭam), maliyāḷam mutaliyavi tamiḻukkuk kiḷi moḻi yum, tammuḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uṭaṉmoḻiyumākum. nīlamali yiluḷḷa paṭakam (paṭakarmoḻi) kaṉṉaṭattiṉ kiḷimoḻi.
3. iyaṉmoḻi (Primitive Language), tirimoḻi (Derivative Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟi.
ivaṟṟukkut tamiḻiyum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiyum muṟiyē kāṭṭākak koḷka.
4. iyaṟkimoḻi (Natural Language), ceyaṟki moḻi (Artificial Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟi.
tamiḻ ōr iyaṟkimoḻi. uvilkkiṉ kaṇkāṇiyār (Bishop Wilkins) eḻutiya kaṟpaṉi moḻi (‘ A Real Character and a Philosophical Language’) ceyaṟki moḻikkuk kāṭṭākum (L.S.L. Vol.II, p.p. 50-63) kāṅkira talivarkaḷ urutu viṟkum intikkum iṭittaramāka, intutāṉi eṉa oṉṟi amippatākac colvatu aric ceyaṟkik kalavi moḻiyākum.
5. taṉimoḻi (Independent Language), cārmoḻi (Dependent Language),  kalavi moḻi (Composite or Mixed Language)vd mūvakiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟi.
ivi mūṉṟukkum, muṟiyē, tamiḻiyum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷiyum, āṅkilattiyum kāṭṭākak kūṟalām.
6. mutumoḻi (Ancient Language), putumoḻi (New Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟi.
ivaṟṟukkut tamiḻum intiyum kāṭṭākum.
7. ilakkiyamoḻi (Classical Language), vaṟumoḻi (Poor Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟi.
ivaṟṟukkut tamiḻiyum kuṭakiyum kāṭṭākak kūṟalām.
8. cemmoḻi allatu tiruntiyamoḻi (Cultivated Language),  puṉmoḻi allatu tiruntāmoḻi (Uncultivated Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟi.
tirāviṭak kuṭumpattil, tamiḻ, teluṅku mutaliyavi cem moḻikaḷ; tuṭā, kōṭṭā mutaliyavi puṉmoḻikaḷ.
9. alimoḻi (Nomad Language), nilimoḻi (State Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟi.
alintu tiriyum makkaḷ pēcuvatu alimoḻi; ōriṭattil nilitta makkaḷ pēcuvatu nilimoḻi.
10. tāymoḻi (Mother Tongue), ayaṉmoḻi (Foreign Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟi.
11. vaḻaṅkumoḻi (Living Language), vaḻaṅkāmoḻi (Dead Language) eṉa iruvakiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟi.
ivaṟṟukku, muṟiyē, tamiḻiyum vaṭamoḻiyiyum kāṭṭākak koḷka.
oru perumoḻiyil pala vaḻakkukaḷ (Dialect) uṇṭu. avaṟṟi moḻivaḻakkeṉalām. avi, iṭavaḻakku (Local Dialect), tici vaḻakku (Provincial Dialect), kulavaḻakku (Class Dialect) tiṇivaḻakku (Regional Dialect) eṉa nālvakippaṭum.
orumoḻi nīṭittu vaḻaṅkumāyiṉ, muṟkāla (Old) vaḻakku, iṭikkāla (Middle) vaḻakku, taṟkāla (Modern) vaḻakku eṉa mun nilikaḷiyaṭin tirukkum.
orumoḻi uyarntatum tāḻntatumāṉa iruvaḻakkukaḷi yuṭiyatā yiruppiṉ, avi muṟiyē uyar (High), tāḻ (Low) eṉṉum aṭikaḷ peṟṟu, ammoḻip peyarāṟ kuṟikkap paṭum (kā: uyarjermaṉ - High German, tāḻ jermaṉ - Low German) tamiḻil ivi centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
oru moḻiyil, eḻuttil vaḻaṅkum vaḻakku nūlvaḻakku (Literary Dialect) eṉṟum, pēccil vaḻaṅkum vaḻakku ulaka vaḻakku (Colloquial Dialect) eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
3. moḻikkulam
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām, vērccoṟkaḷiṉ uṟavum ilakkaṇa voṟṟumiyum paṟṟi, turēṉiyam (Turanian) āriyam (Aryan), cēmiyam (Semitic) eṉa  mukkulaṅkaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
ovvoru kulamum pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷik koṇṭatu. ovvoru kuṭumpamum cila allatu pala moḻikaḷik koṇṭatu.
tamiḻ turēṉiyak kulattil(tirāviṭak kuṭumpam oru ciṟu kuḻuvā yiruntālum mukkulattiṉiṉṟum vēṟākat taṉittuk kūṟappaṭaṟ kuriyatu tirāviṭak kuṭumpattic cērntatu. turēṉiyattiṟkuc cittiyam (Scythian) eṉṟum āriyat tiṟku intō- airōppiyam (Indo - European), allatu intō- jermāṉiyam (Indo- Germanic) eṉṟum piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu.
moḻi nūl neṟimuṟikaḷ
1. moḻi nūl oru taṉikkali.
palar moḻinūl oru taṉikkali yeṉpati iṉṉum aṟintilar. paṇṭitarkaḷ potuvāyt taṅkaḷiṭam yārēṉum oru collukku mūlaṅkēṭṭāl, ataṟku ētāvatu collā tiruppiṉ iḻiveṉṟeṇṇi, poruntap puḷukal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi ētēṉumoṉṟic colliviṭu kiṉṟaṉar. pāṇṭit tiyam vēṟu, moḻinūṟ pulami vēṟu eṉpati avar aṟintilar.
kālañceṉṟa oru perun tamiḻppaṇṭitar, tiruccirāp paḷḷiyil orumuṟi vaṭi eṉṉum tiṉpaṇṭap peyar, vaṭu eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉṟum, ap paṇṭattiṉ naṭuvil tuḷiyiruppatu ataṟkoru vaṭu (kuṟṟam)  veṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. vaṭi eṉpatu vaḷ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟanta teṉpatiyum, vaṭṭamāṉatu eṉṉum poruḷuṭiya teṉ patiyum, uḻuntu māviṟkuḷ eṇṇey eḷitāyc cellum paṭi tuḷiyiṭuva teṉpatiyum, tuḷiyiṉāl cuviyāvatu maṇamāvatu keṭā teṉpatiyum avar aṟintilar.
ottuppārkka: peḷ + ti = peṭṭi - peṭi: vaḷ+ti = vaṭṭi - vaṭi. peḷ = virumpu, vaḷ = vaḷi.
2. moḻinūl eṉṟum oppiyal taṉmiyuḷḷatu
aṭṭiyāṭal eṉpataṉ poruḷ uṇartaṟkut teluṅ kaṟivum, kaṉṉaṭa aṟivum vēṇṭiyatā yirukkiṉṟatu. aṭṭa allatu aṭṭe = muṇṭam (te.ka.) (vēṅkaṭarājulu reṭṭiyār kaṭṭuri (tamiḻppoḻil).
ampu (vaḷiyal), ampi ( paṭaku, kāḷāṉ), āmpi (kāḷāṉ) eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ vērkkarutti lattīṉiṉuḷḷa ampi eṉṉuñ collum, kirēkkiluḷḷa ambhi eṉṉuñ collum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
amphi = round; amphitheatre eṉṉum tokic colli nōkkuka.
3. moḻinūl piṟakalic cārpuḷḷatu
moḻinūl, kaṇitattippōlap piṟakali cārāta kaliyaṉṟu. moḻinūliṉ ovvoru kūṟṟum, avvak kalikkup poruntiya tāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṅkilattiluḷḷa teak eṉṉuñ col, tēkku eṉṉum tamiḻccollē yeṉpataṟkut tiṇinūl (Geography) cāṉṟākum. intiyāvil, vintiya malikku vaṭakkē tēkku vaḷarvatilli eṉṟu, rākosiṉ (Ragozin) tamatu vētakāla intiyā (Vedic Inida) eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
eḻuttolikaḷi olippatil vayiṟu, nuriyīral, mūccukkuḻāy, toṇṭi, nā, aṇṇam, pal, utaṭu, mūkku ākiya palavuṟup pukaḷ toṭarpuṟu vatāl, uṭalnūlum (Physiology);
uḷḷakkaruttukaḷiṉ veḷippāṭē moḻiyātaliṉ, uḷanūlum (Psychology);
moḻikaḷip pēcum makkaḷiṉ iṭamāṟṟattiyum nākarika vaḷarcciyi yum aṟintirukka vēṇṭiyatāl, carittiramum (History);
taṭpaveppa nilikkum avvan nāṭṭup poruḷkaṭkum takkapaṭi, oliyum collum māṟupaṭuvatāl, tiṇinūlum (Geography);
nākarika makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷ, mataviyalip peritun taḻuviyirup patāl, matanūl (Theology), paḻami nūl (Mythology), paṭṭāṅku nūl (Philosophy) mutaliya kalikaḷum; iṉṉum, curuṅkakkūṟiṉ pala kalikaḷum moḻinūṟ payiṟcikkut terintiruttal vēṇṭum.
4. moḻikaḷellām ōraḷavil toṭarpuṭiyaṉa.
cila coṟkaḷum, avaṟṟiyāḷum neṟimuṟikaḷum, pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kā: tamiḻ - maṉ, E - man, A.S. - mann, Skt. -  manu. tamiḻ - tā, L. - do, Skt. - da.; tamiḻ - nāvāy, L. - navis, Skt. - nu.
taṉikkuṟiliyaṭutta mey uyirvariṉ iraṭṭuvatu pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyirukkiṉṟatu.
kā: tamiḻ - maṇ + ulakam = maṇṇulakam, veḷ + āṭu = veḷḷāṭu.
āṅkilam - thin + er = thinner, sit + ing = sitting.
5. 	moḻikaḷukkuḷ iṉa aḷaviṟkut takkapaṭi oppumiyirukkum.
6.	moḻikaḷi ārāyumpōtu, muṉṉi moḻiyi muṉvittum piṉṉi moḻiyip piṉvittum ārāyavēṇṭum.
cilar tamiḻukku mikap piṟpaṭṭaṉavum, tamiḻiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṉavum, mikat tirintuḷḷaṉa vum, āriyak kalappu mikkuḷḷaṉavumāṉa maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷukkup piṟkālattil vaṭamoḻiyip piṉpaṟṟi eḻutiyuḷḷa kēraḷapāṇiṉīyam, karṉāṭaka saptamaṇi tarppaṇam pōṉṟa nūlkaḷit tuṇikkoṇṭu tamiḻiyalpi ārāyvatu, narivālik koṇṭu kaṭalāḻam pārppatum, tantiyi makaṉ peṟṟatākak koḷvatum pōṉṟatē. an nūlkaḷik kaṟpatāl uṇṭākum payaṉ, malikalli eli piṭittal pōla mikac ciṟiyatākum; mēlum, uṇmi kāṇātapaṭi mayakkattiyum ūṭṭum. moḻi nūl ārāyccikku vēṇṭiya maṉaviriviyaṭivataṟku, āṅkilattil māri (Morris), āṅka (Angus), kīṟṟu (Skeat), vīṟṟu (Sweet), uviṭṉi (Whitney) mutaliyōr eḻutiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷip paṭittal vēṇṭum. nuṇṇaṟiñarum perum pulavarumāṉa vēṅkaṭarājulu reṭṭiyār vaṭamoḻiyiyum piṟkālattilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum piṉpaṟṟiya tāl tām  eḻutiyuḷḷa ilakkaṇak kaṭṭurikaḷ, tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ mūviṭappeyar eṉṉum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ, cila cōrvupaṭṭuḷḷār. aviyāvaṉa:
1. 	tamiḻil ukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ mūṉṟēyeṉpatu.
 	tolkāppiyattil, moḻimarapil,
  	ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē
  	uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa  (41,42)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatu, muṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟiyēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiya lukara vīṟṟiyaṉṟu.
kuṟṟiyalukaram īreḻuttiṟkuk kuṟiyāta colliṉ īṟṟil valliṉa meyyūrntaṉṟit taṉittuvarutal illi eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. maṟuku, cerukku eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟ kaḷiṉiṭiyilum, atu, poṟu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟilum uḷḷa ukaratti (ikkāla viyalpu nōkki)k kuṟṟiya lukaramēyeṉak koḷḷa iṭamiruppiṉum, u,tu eṉat taṉittum, ural, mukam eṉac coṉ mutalilum varum ukarattik kuṟṟiya lukaramākak koḷḷa eḷḷaḷavum iṭamilli. ukaram, kuṟṟu karam, kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṉum coṟkaḷil varum ukaraṅkaḷum muṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷē, ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉa vaṟṟil kuṟu, kuṟṟiyal eṉṉum aṭimoḻikaḷāl, kuṟukiya ukaram poruḷaḷavil kuṟikkappaṭu kiṉṟatē yaṉṟi oliyaḷavilaṉṟu. ākavē uccakāram uppakāram eṉpavi muṟṟiyalukarattik kuṟikkumēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiyalukarattik kuṟiyā eṉpatu mikat tēṟṟam.
mēṟkūṟiya iru nūṟpākkaḷāṉum, muṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ mūṉṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. avi ucu, mucu, tapu eṉpaṉa eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ivi cilavātaliṉ iṅṅaṉam vitantu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
cukku, kuccu, paṭṭu, pattu, kaṟpu, maṟṟu eṉpaṉapōṉṟa kuṟṟiya lukaraccoṟkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉavātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟkut toki kūṟiṟṟilar tolkāppiyar.
pu,tu eṉṉum īṟukaḷikkoṇṭa eṇṇiṟanta toḻiṟ peyar kaḷum, tu,cu eṉṉum īṟukaḷik koṇṭa eṇṇiṟanta piṟa viṉic coṟkaḷum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷātal kāṇka. itaṉālēyē,
uyiru eñciya iṟuti yākum  (36)
eṉṟu iruvaki yukaramumaṭaṅkap potuppaṭak kūṟi, piṉpu uccakāram........ urittē eṉṟum, uppakāram .... moḻipa.... eṉṟum muṟṟukaratti vitantō tiṉār tolkāppiyar.
2. tolkāppiyar kuṟṟiyalukaratti meyyīṟākak koṇṭār eṉpatu.
tolkāppiyap puṇariyalil,
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷi yoṭu niliyal  (2)
kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa  (3)
eṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukaratti meyyīṟṟōṭu māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiyatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaratti meyyīṟākak koṇṭaṉar tolkāppiyar eṉṟār reṭṭiyār.
kuṟṟiyalukarattip puḷḷiyoṭu niliyalākak kūṟiyatāl kuṟṟiyalukarattip puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭum vaḻakkam paṇṭik kālatti liruntateṉpatu peṟappaṭumēyaṉṟi, āciriyar kuṟṟiya lukaratti meyyīṟākak koṇṭār eṉpatu peṟappaṭātu.
paṇṭit tamiḻar nuṇṇiya cevippulaṉ vāyntiruntamiyiṉ, kuṟṟukaram muṟṟukarattiṉum kuṟukiṉateṉpatik kāṭṭum poruṭṭup puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutiṉar.
kuṟṟukarattiṉmēl arimati (allatu kuṟumpiṟi) vitteḻu tuvatu iṉṟum teṉmaliyāḷa nāṭṭil uḷḷatu eṉṟu reṭṭiyārē kūṟukiṉṟār. maliyāḷa nāṭṭil, iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭilillāta pala paṇṭit tamiḻ vaḻakkaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
tolkāppiyar kālattil, kuṟṟiyalukarattip puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻu tiṉārāyiṉ, av vaḻakkam ēṉ piṟkālattililli eṉṟu kēṭkalām. ataṟku viṭi kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
tolkāppiyar kālattil, ekara okara uyirkaḷiyum aviyēṟiṉa meykaḷiyum, mēṟpuḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkamāyirunta teṉpatu,
nūṉmarapiluḷḷa,
meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal  (15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē  (16)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāṉ aṟiyalām. ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷum, aviyēṟiṉa meykaḷum, puḷḷi peṟāmiyālēyē, tattam kuṟilkaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iv vaḻakkam aṟṟuppōyviṭṭa teṉpatu, tamiḻil ekara okara uyirēṟiṉa meykaḷum, ēkāra ōkāra uyirēṟiṉa meykaḷum, vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yeḻutappaṭṭa miyāl, piṉṉavaṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭaṟku vīramā muṉivar kompukaḷi mēṟcuḻitteḻutiṉatākak koṭuntamiḻ ilak kaṇattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉamē kuṟṟukarap puḷḷiyum vaḻakku vīḻntateṉak koḷka.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi  (eḻuttu. 43)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāviṉ uriyil, āṇṭu eṉṟa mikiyāṉē......... kuṟṟukarak kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukku mēṟpuḷḷi koṭup pārum uḷar eṉakkoḷka eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, av vaḻakkuc cila nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭku muṉtāṉ vīḻntateṉa aṟiyalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē kalvikkaḷam pārppaṉar kippaṭṭuviṭṭatiṉālum, tamiḻ vaṟaḷa vaṭamoḻiyi vaḷampaṭuttuvatē avar kaṭippiṭiyātalālum, tamiḻar uyar nilik kalviyi iḻantu viṭṭatiṉālum tamiḻ eḻuttumuṟi kavaṉippāriṉṟic cīrkēṭaṭintatu.
puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatiṉālēyē, kuṟṟukaratti meyyīṟākak koḷḷiṉ, moḻimarapil,
meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal  (15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē  (16)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyiruppatāl, ekara okara vīṟukaḷiyum meyyīṟākak koḷḷavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam koḷḷalākāmi veḷippaṭi.
iṉi, kaṇ+yātu = kaṇṇiyātu eṉpatupōṉṟē, cukku + yātu = cukkiyātu eṉṟu puṇarvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟākak koḷḷap paṭumeṉṟār reṭṭiyār. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, katavu + yātu = kataviyātu eṉṟu muṟṟukaramum puṇarvatāl, atuvum meyyīṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭu, ukaravuyirē tamiḻukkilli yeṉṟākum. ākiyāl, avar kūṟṟu uṇmiyaṉmi yaṟika.
vaṭamoḻiyil, purik, virāṭ, cat eṉṟu valliṉa meyyīṟu vantiruppatu paṟṟit tamiḻilum avvāṟē varumeṉṟum, kuṟṟiya lukaramellām meyyīṟēyeṉṟum koḷvatu tamiḻ muṟikku nērmāṟākum. kuṟṟiyalukaramellām meyyīṟāyiṉ, eṉakku, pāyccu, kaṭṭu (kaṇ+tu), tāṭṭu (tāḷ + tu), vāḻttu, piṭippu, koṭpu, tirumpu, pāṟṟu (pāl+tu), aṟṟu (aṉ+tu) eṉpavaṟ ṟiṉ uṇmiyāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷ, muṟiyē, eṉakk, pāycc, kaṭṭ, tāṭṭ, vāḻtt, piṭipp, koṭp, tirump, pāṟṟ, aṟṟ eṉpaṉa vātalvēṇṭum, aṅṅaṉamākāmi aṟika.
tolkāppiyar kuṟṟiyalukaratti meyyīṟākak koṇṭi ruppiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram..... eḻuttō raṉṉa eṉṟu, ati ōr taṉiyeḻuttākak kūṟiyirār.
kuṟṟiyalukaramāvatu, iṭattiṉālum cārpiṉālum kuṟukiyum ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭum olikkum iyalpāṉa ukaramē. kuṟṟiyalukaram varumiṭattil muṟṟukaramirukka muṭiyātu. valliṉa meykaḷ, melliṉa meyyum iṭiyiṉa meyyum pōlaṉṟic cikkeṉak kāṟṟit taṭuttalāṉ (checks of breath), nīrōṭṭam kallil muṭṭi vēkantaṇikiṟatupōla, ōciyāṉatu taṭippaṭṭu, īṟṟiluḷḷa ukaram kuṉṟi yolikkiṉṟateṉak koḷka.
3. paṇṭit tamiḻccoṟkaḷ valliṉa meyyilum iṟṟaṉa eṉpatu.
ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum
appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti  (45)
eṉṟu, collīṟṟu meykaḷip patiṉoṉṟēyeṉṟu tēṟṟē kāraṅ koṭuttuk kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar. ātalāl, valliṉa mey collīṟāmeṉak koḷḷa eḷḷaḷavum iṭamilli.
uriñ, porun mutaliya coṟkaḷum, maṇ, mīṉ mutaliya coṟ kaḷum ukarampeṟṟu vaḻaṅkuvati, valliṉa meyyīṟukaḷ ukaram peṟṟut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvatākat tām koṇṭa koḷkikkuc cāṉṟāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār reṭṭiyār.
uriñu, porunu, maṇṇu, mīṉu eṉṟu mutalil ukaravīṟā yirunta coṟkaḷ, piṟkālattil ukaravīṟu keṭṭu uriñ, porun, maṇ, mīṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉavēyaṉṟi, mutalil meyyīṟāyirunta coṟkaḷ piṟkālattil ukaravīṟupeṟṟu vaḻaṅkiṉavalla.
tamiḻar ulakil mikap paḻimiyāṉa maṉpatiyarātaliṉ, moḻi yolimuṟiyil kuḻantiyarpōlvar. mutalil, tamiḻiluḷḷa ellāc coṟkaḷum uyirīṟākavē olittaṉa. piṉpu, pulavarāṟ paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟapōtu, melliṉa iṭiyiṉa meykaḷi ṉiṉṟu maṭṭum uyirīṟu nīkkappaṭṭatu. ivvīriṉa meykaḷum valliṉa meyyilum olittaṟ keḷitātal kāṇka.
iṉṟum, cila melliṉa iṭiyiṉa meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ, pakāc coṉṉiliyil aṅṅaṉam meyyīṟāka niṟkumēyaṉṟip pakucoṉṉili yil nillā.
kā: paṇ- paṇṇukiṟāṉ, aḷ - aḷḷukiṟāṉ.
ivi paṇkiṟāṉ, aḷkiṟāṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkāmi kāṇka. itaṉāl, uyirīṟṟunili muntiyatēyaṉṟi, meyyīṟṟu nili muntiyataṉṟu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum.
āriya moḻikaḷ tamiḻukkup pintiṉaviyātalāṉum, tiripu vaḷarcci mikkavi yātalāṉum, avaṟṟil valliṉa meyyum īṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
piṉṉiyattilum (Finnish), vaṭaturēṉiya moḻikaḷuḷ yūraliya (Uralic) vakuppu muḻuvatilum, coṉmutalil iṇi mey kaḷiyum, collīṟṟil oru meyyiyum cērppatilli. kiḷā (glas) eṉṉum jermaṉiyaccol piṉṉiyattil lāci eṉṟeḻutappaṭukiṟatu. mak (smak), ṟṟār (stor), ṟṟirāṉṟu (Stand) eṉṉum cuvīṭiyac (Swedish) coṟkaḷ muṟiyē, makku, cūri, ranta eṉṟeḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu (L.S.L. Vol. II, P. 207) mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṟār.
tirāviṭam turēṉiyak kiḷiyākak koḷḷappaṭuvatiyum, hāpiṟṟal, pāṅk, pvī mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ muṟiyē, āpattiri, pāṅki, pīcu eṉṟu uyirīṟākat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvatiyum nōkkuka.
4. valliṉamey iraṭṭittal illi eṉpatu.
valliṉa mey iraṭṭittal eṉpatē yilliyeṉṟum, āṭṭu, pōkku mutaliya piṟaviṉikaḷ ellām, 
āṭu + tu = āṭ + tu = āṭṭu,
pōku + tu = pōk + tu = pōkku,
eṉṟa muṟiyilēyē amivaṉaveṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār reṭṭiyār.
taṉviṉip pakutiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttil aḻuttam (Stress) viḻuva tāl atu piṟaviṉiyākak kūṭumeṉpatu.
uppa kāra moṉṟeṉa moḻipa
iruvayi ṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē  (tol.moḻi. 43)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. āṭu, pōku mutaliya taṉ viṉikaḷiṉ īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ mēl, aḻuttam viḻuvatālēyē, avi iraṭṭikkiṉṟaṉa. aciyaḻuttattāṟ poruḷ vēṟupaṭu vatu, moḻinūlil oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭa uṇmi. cīṉamoḻi yil in neṟimuṟi mikutiyum payilkiṉṟatu.
aciyaḻuttam mutalāvatu eṭuppaciyāka iruntatēyaṉṟi, valiyiraṭṭalāka illi. nāḷaṭivil aḻuttam mikamika vali yiraṭṭalākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. appōtu ati iraṭṭit teḻuti ṉar. ataṟkumuṉpē tapu eṉṉum viṉi vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉati ṉāl atu paṇṭi muṟippaṭi eṭuppaciyāl piṟaviṉiyāva tākavē kuṟikkappaṭṭuvantatu, tolkāppiyarum muṉṉūl kaḷip piṉpaṟṟi aṅṅaṉamē kuṟittār. iti oṉṟeṉa moḻipa eṉṟu, vaḻinūṉ muṟiyil āciriyar kūṟuvatāl aṟiyalām.
kāṭu, āṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷ, avaṟṟoṭu poruṭ porutta muṟṟa piṟa coṟkaḷuṭaṉ puṇarumpōtu iraṭṭittal, avvap puṇarmoḻikaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷukkuḷḷa neruṅkiya allatu oṉṟiya toṭarpik kāṭṭuvatākum.
kā: kāṭṭuvilaṅku, āṟṟunīr.
naṭṭāṟu, kuṟṟukaram eṉpaṉavum im muṟipaṟṟiyaṉavē. iti, iru poruḷkaḷi iṇippatāl uṇṭākum iṟukkattiṟ koppiṭalām.
puku, cuṭu mutaliya viṉikaḷ pukku, cuṭṭu eṉa iraṭṭittu iṟanta kālam kāṭṭiṉatum, aciyaḻuttattiṉālēyē, pukku, cuṭṭu eṉpaṉa paṇṭik kālattil muṟṟākavum eccamākavum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. piṉpu, pāl kāṭṭum īṟu cērntu pukkāṉ (pukku + āṉ), cuṭṭāṉ (cuṭṭu + āṉ) eṉṟu muṟṟukkut taṉi vaṭivam ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, av vīṟu peṟāta paṇṭi vaṭivam eccamāka maṭṭum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
pōkku, āṭṭu mutaliya toḻiṟpeyarkaḷum aciyaḻuttat tāl īṟu iraṭṭittaviyē.
ciṟṟaṭi, cīṟaṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkum iru vaṭivukaḷuḷ, cīṟaṭi eṉpatē ciṟantateṉṟum, ciṟṟaṭi eṉpatu ciṟupāṉmi vaḻakkēyeṉṟum kūṟiṉār reṭṭiyār.
paccili, pācaṟi; cevvāmpal, cētā; mutiyōr, mūturi eṉap paṇpup peyarkaḷ ciṟupāṉmi nīḷātum nīṇṭum iru vakiyāy varutalāṉum, perumpāṉmi naṭṭāṟu, puttuyir, kuṟṟukaram eṉa nīḷātu valittē varutalāṉum, avar kūṟṟiṟ ciṟanta toṉṟumilliyeṉa viṭukka.
5. aravu eṉṟoru toḻiṟpeyarīṟu illiyeṉpatu.
aravu eṉṟu oru toḻiṟpeyarīṟē yilliyeṉṟu kūṟi, kūṭṭaravu, tēṟṟaravu eṉṉun toḻiṟpeyarkaḷi, muṟiyē,
kūṭu = kūṭ + taravu = kūṭṭaravu
tēṟu = tēṟ + taravu = tēṟṟaravu
eṉṟu pirittuk kāṭṭiṉār reṭṭiyār.
oru poyyi maṟikka oṉpatu poy vēṇṭum eṉṟāṟ pōla, kuṟṟukaratti meyyīṟākakkoṇṭa oru vaḻuvi maṟikka, iṅṅaṉam oṉpatu vaḻukkaḷ nērntirukkiṉṟaṉa.
aram eṉṟoru toḻiṟpeyarīṟu iruppatu, viḷamparam eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarā laṟiyappaṭum.
aram eṉṉum īṟē aravu eṉṟu tiriyum.
aram - (aramu) - aravu.
o.nō. uram - uravu, taṭam - taṭavu, puṟam - puṟavu.
6.	mūviṭappeyar viṉāppeyarkaḷiṉ amippu, uyireḻuttu 	
	varicimuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉpatu.
a,i,u,e eṉṉum nāṉkuyirkaḷum, muṟiyē cēymic cuṭṭiṟkum, aṇmic cuṭṭiṟkum, iṭimic cuṭṭiṟkum, viṉāviṟkum āki, paṭarkkip peyar muṉṉilippeyar iṭimippeyar viṉāppeyar eṉṉum nāṟpāṟ peyarkaḷum amintatu, uyireḻuttu kaḷiṉ varici muṟiyi ētuvākak koṇṭateṉṟu reṭṭiyār kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēṟkūṟiya nāṟpāṟ peyar kaḷum arivarit tōṟṟattiṟku (eḻuttiṟku varivaṭivam tōṉṟu taṟku) muntiṉaveṉpatē.
7. 	moḻinūṟ payiṟcikku moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum akara mutaliyum 
	(akarātiyum) pōtiya karuvikaḷām.
oru colliṉ poruḷiyum mūlattiyum aṟitaṟku, ilak kaṇamum colliyalum (Etymology) terintirukka vēṇṭum. illāviṭṭāl, pōliyoppumiyāl vaḻumuṭipiṟ kiṭamākum.
kaṇṭi, pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, condemn, Parlia-ment eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkaḷuṭaṉ oliyilum poruḷilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, colliyali nōkkiṉ, avi ciṟitum toṭarpaṟṟavi eṉpatu teḷivākum.
Condemn - L. condemno, from con intensive, damno to damn.
Parliament - Lit. ‘a parleying or speaking,’ Fr. parlement - parler. to speak, ment, a Lat. suffix.
pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉpatu pār, āḷum, maṉṟu eṉṟa muccoṟkaḷālāṉatāyum, Parliament eṉpatu orē collāyumiruttal kāṇka.
coṟkaḷ perumpāṉ moḻikaḷiṟ kālantōṟum vēṟupaṭu kiṉṟamiyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ mūlavaṭivi yaṟintē poruḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum.
mūlam vēr (root), taṇṭu (stem) eṉa iruvakippaṭum. vaḷiyam eṉṉum collukku vaḷi eṉpatu taṇṭu; vaḷ eṉpatu vēr. (vērum mutal vēr (primitive root), vaḻivēr (Secondary root) cārpu vēr (Tertiary root) eṉa mūvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, mutalvēr maṭṭum mīṇṭum mūvakippaṭum. iviyellām piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum).taṇṭi aṭiyeṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.
mūlamum pakutiyum tammuḷoru vēṟupāṭuṭiyaṉa. purantāṉ eṉṉuñ collukku, pura eṉpatu mūlamum pakutiyumākum. āṉāl, purantaraṉ eṉṉuñ collukku, pura eṉpatu mūlamum, purantā eṉpatu pakutiyumākum.
moḻikaḷukkiṭiyiluḷḷa toṭarpik kāṇpataṟku, mūviṭap peyar, viṉāppeyar, mukkiyamāṉa muṟippeyar, eṇp peyar, vīṭṭiluḷḷa taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷiṉ peyar, ciṉippeyar, vā,pō eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa mukkiya viṉikaḷ eṉpaṉavē pōtu māyiṉum, pala coṟkaḷum terintiruppiṉ colliyalpum coṟporuḷum viḷakkam peṟutaliṉ, akarātiyiyē tuṇikkoḷvatu mikac ciṟantatākum.
8. oruvar tam iṭattiliruntukoṇṭē moḻinūl payilalām.
oruvar oru moḻiyi aṟiya, atu vaḻaṅkum iṭattiṟkuc celvatu iṉṟiyamiyāta taṉṟu; tam iṭattiliruntukoṇṭē takkōr eḻutiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum akarātikaḷiyun tuṇi koṇṭu ayaṉmoḻikaḷik kaṟkalām.
9. oru moḻiyaṟivu piṟamoḻi yaṟivukkut tuṇiyākum.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷiṉ mūlam piṟa moḻiyilēyē aṟiyak kiṭappatālum, oru moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇikaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkāta, ammoḻikkuriya cila ilakkaṇavuṇmikaḷ, piṟa moḻiyilakkaṇikaḷāl kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭiruppata ṉālum, ulakat tāymoḻiyiṉ iyalpiyum vaḷarcciyiyum aṟiyat takka pala neṟimuṟikaḷ pala moḻikaḷilum paravik kiṭattalā lum, moḻikaḷi aṟiya aṟiya oruvariṉ aṟivu perukuvatā lum, ovvoru moḻiyiṉ aṟivukkum piṟamoḻi yaṟivu ētēṉum oruvakiyil utavuvatēyākum.
10. tāymoḻi muṉṉum ayaṉmoḻi piṉṉum kaṟkappaṭa vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉ kallāmalē taṉ tāymoḻiyil pēcakkūṭumāyiṉum, ataṉ marapiyum ilakkaṇattiyum kaṟṟē yaṟiya vēṇṭiya tirukkiṉṟatu, tāymoḻiyiyalpi yaṟiyu muṉ ayaṉmoḻiyi virivāyk kaṟpavar, ayaṉmoḻi vaḻakkit tāymoḻiyiṟ pukuttiviṭuvar.
tamiḻik kallātu āṅkilattiyē payiṉṟa cila tamiḻar, perum pāluṅ kiṟitavar, naṉṟāy viḷiyāṭiṉārkaḷ eṉpati viḷiyāṭṭil naṉṟāyc ceytārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
11. mukkula moḻikaḷum orē mūlattiṉa. (L.S.L. Vol.I. pp. 35,375, 387)
oru marattil, orē aṭiyiṉiṉṟu pala kavikaḷum, akkavi kaḷiṉiṉṟupala kiḷikaḷum, ak kiḷikaḷiṉiṉṟu pala kompu kaḷum, ak kompukaḷiṉiṉṟu pala kappukaḷum, ak kappu kaḷiṉiṉṟu pala vaḷārkaḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē, ulaka mūla moḻiyiṉiṉṟu, pala kulanilikaḷum, ak kulanilikaḷi ṉiṉṟu, pala kuṭumpa nilikaḷum, ak kuṭumpa nilikaḷiṉiṉṟu pala moḻikaḷum, am moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu pala kiḷi moḻikaḷ allatu moḻi vaḻakkukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ mukkulat tāykaḷum orē mūlattiṉa eṉṟu, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē, mākkacu mullar tēṟṟamāyk kūṟiviṭṭār.
paṇṭikkālattil, āriyar varumuṉ, intiyā muḻutum, pala moḻivaḻakkukaḷāka vēṟupaṭṭa orē moḻi (tamiḻ) vaḻaṅkiṉa tākac cila tolkāppiyak kuṟippukaḷālum moḻinūlārāyc ciyālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. āriyar vanta piṟakuṅkūṭa, uṇmiyāṉa moḻikaḷāka oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭavi, vaṭakkil cama kirutamum teṟkil tamiḻumāka iraṇṭē yeṉpatu, vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyeṉṉum avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷālēyē aṟiyap paṭum. iṉṟum, teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷi yellām tamiḻukkuḷḷum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiyellām camakirutattikuḷḷum aṭakkiviṭalām. tirāviṭak kuṭumpam muḻutum tamiḻukkuḷ aṭaṅkuvatu pōlap piṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷum ovvoru moḻik kuḷ aṭaṅkiviṭum. inta muṟiyiyē toṭarntu piṉpaṟṟi ṉāl, iṟutiyil, turēṉiyam āriyam cēmiyam eṉṉum mukkula nilikaḷē eñci niṟkum. avaṟṟiyum oṉṟāyaṭakkuvataṟkuriya neṟimuṟikaḷ illāmaṟ pōkavilli.
(nōvāvukku muntiya kālattil, ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ) ulaka muḻuvatum orē moḻiyum orē pēccumāy iruntatu eṉṟu, kiṟitava maṟiyil ātiyākamam 11ām atikāram mutaṟ kiḷaviyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉṟum, oru ulaka mūlamoḻiyit tēṟṟañ ceyvataṟkāṉa pala cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aviyāvaṉa:
1.		makkaḷ toki varavara mikutal.
2. 		ellāmoḻikaḷiṉ arivariyum a āvil toṭaṅkal.
3. 	ammā, appā eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, mūviṭappeyar viṉāp peyar vērkaḷum, pala coṟkaḷum pala moḻi kaṭkum potu vāyiruttal.
4. 		eṇkaḷ pattuppattākavē pala nāṭukaḷil eṇṇappaṭal.
5.	neruppu, cūriyaṉ, nākam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaḻipāṭu paṇṭi yulakeṅku miruntami.
6. 	neṭṭiṭiyiṭṭa nāṭṭārkkum karuttorumippu.
kā:	āṅkilam	tamiḻ
	tonic(from tone)	olittal (taḻittal)
	to catch fire	tīppaṟṟu
	to fall in love	vīḻ(tāmvīḻvār....cyF”)
	blacksmith	karumāṉ
mutalāvatu kaṇavaṉum maṉiyiyumāka illaṟan toṭaṅkum iruvar, piṉpu periya kuṭumpamākap perukuvatiyum, nāḷaṭi vil ciṟṟūr nakarum nakar mānakarumāvatiyum, kuṭi matippaṟik kikaḷil varavara makkaṭṭoki mikuntu varuvatiyum kāṇ pārkku, makkaḷ ellām orutāy vayiṟṟiṉar eṉpatu pulaṉā kāmaṟ pōkātu. makkaḷ orutāy vayiṟṟiṉarāyiṉ, avarkaḷ pēcum palvēṟu moḻikaḷum orē mūlattiṉavātal vēṇṭum.
makkaḷtoki vaḷarccikkuk kāṭṭāka, mākkacu mullariṉ moḻinūṟ kaṭṭurikaḷiṉ mutaṉ maṭalattil, 62ām pakka aṭikkuṟippiṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷa oru kuṟippi iṅkuk kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
“oru naṅki patiṉāṟu piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟu, avaruṭ patiṉoruvar maṇantapiṉ iṟantāḷ. avaḷ iṟakkumpōtu, 144 pērappiḷḷikaḷum, 228 pērap pērappiḷḷikaḷum, 900 pērap pērap pērappiḷḷikaḷum avaḷukkiruntaṉar.”
muṇṭāmoḻiyiṉarkkuḷ irupatirupatāy eṇṇum vaḻakkam iruppatu, kiyiyuṅ kāliyum oṉṟupōṟ karutattakka anākarika niliyil, avar muṉṉōr iruntatik kāṭṭum.
makkaḷ perumpālum taṭpaveppa nili vēṟupāṭṭālēyē, pala vakuppārāka vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟum nākarika vāḻkkiyum kalviyumiruppiṉ pala vakuppārum oru niliyarā kavum otta karuttiṉarākavum vāḻak kūṭum.
12. ōr iyaṟkimoḻi tōṉṟum vaḻikaḷ palvakiya.
ulakat tāymoḻi tōṉṟiya vaḻiyippaṟṟip palarum pala vakiyāṉa karuttuk koṇṭaṉar. cilar oppolik koḷki (Onomatopoeic theory)yum, cilar uṇarcci yolikkoḷki (Interjectional theory) yum, cilar cuṭṭaṭik koḷki (Demonstrative theory) yum eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar. ōr iyaṟkimoḻi oru vaḻiyilaṉṟip pala vaḻikaḷil tōṉṟumeṉpatu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
moḻi iyaṟkiyāy tōṉṟiyateṉṉum iyaṟkik koḷki (Natural theory) yum, iṭukuṟikaḷāyt tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟum iṭukuṟik koḷki  (Arbitrary theory) yum, palar kūṭi iṉṉa poruṭ kiṉṉa colleṉṟu muṭivu ceytatākak kūṟum kūṭṭu muṭivuk koḷki (Conventional theory) yum, kaṭavuḷē amittār eṉṉum teyvikak koḷki (Divine theory) yum ikkālattiṟ kēlā.

13. iyaṟkimoḻiyil iṭukuṟiccol irātu.
cila moḻikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷ mūlan teriyātapaṭi mikat tirintu pōyirukkiṉṟaṉa. poruḷaṟiya vārāmiyāl avaṟṟi iṭukuṟi yeṉṟaṉar ilakkaṇikaḷ. vaṭanūlār irākaṅkaḷukkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum peyariṭumpōtu, avaṟṟit tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirittuk kāṭṭavēṇṭi iṭukuṟippeyarkaḷi yiṭṭaṉar. ittakiya nilimikaḷ iyaṟki moḻikku nērā.
ovvoru collum poruḷ kuṟikkavēṇṭumeṉpatu, māntaṉ pēcciṉ mukkiyamāṉa ilakkaṇamākum. oru moḻiyil olittiripu tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉ, iv vilakkaṇatti am moḻi yiḻatuviṭukiṟatu eṉṟu mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṟār. (L.S.L. Vol. I. p. 47).
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (tol. 639)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
14.	ulaka mutaṉmoḻi tōṉṟiya muṟiyi ōr iyaṟki moḻiyiltāṉ  kāṇamuṭiyum.
tiripu vaḷarcciyil mika mutirnta camakirutatti, iṉṟum, cilar tirāviṭat tāyākavum, ataṟkumēlum, ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yākavum mayaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. tiripiṟkuc ciṟanta āriyakkulat tiluṅkūṭa atu mikap pintiyateṉṟu, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē taḷḷiviṭṭaṉar moḻinūl vallār. aṅṅaṉamirukka, mika iyaṟkiyāṉa tamiḻukku atu eṅṅaṉam mūlamāka muṭiyum?.
ulaka mutaṉmoḻikku neruṅkiya moḻi, eḷiya olikaḷi yuṭiyatāy, acinili mutaliya aivaki nilikaḷik koṇṭa tāy, iṭukuṟiyillatāy, taṉimoḻiyā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
15. carittirattāl ēṉi moḻikkuḷ aṭakkappaṭāta muṉṉi moḻikkē 
	mūlamoḻi āyvu ēṟkum.
maliyāḷam, inti, āṅkilam pōṉṟa moḻikaḷuḷ, mūla moḻikkuriya iyalpukaḷ iraṇṭoṉṟu tōṉṟiṉum avi koḷḷappaṭā. ēṉeṉil am moḻikaḷ ovvoṉṟum ēṉiya moḻikkuḷ aṭaṅkiviṭutal carittirattāl aṟiyappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
16. moḻi māntaṉ mik¥ng. (L.S.L. Vol. I, p. 31)
moḻi eṉṟu potuppaṭak kūṟuvatellām ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyiyē, eppikkūra (Epicurus), ariṟṟāṭṭil (Aristotle) mutaliya kirēkka meypporuṇūlār, moḻi aimpūtampōla iyaṟkiyāṉateṉṟu koṇṭār. intiya muṉṉōrukkum ak karuttē yiruntatu.
oli eṉṟumuḷḷatēṉum, atik karuttaṟivikkum karuviyākak koṇṭatu māntaṉ vēliyē. itaṟkuc cāṉṟāka vilaṅku paṟavi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuk kaṭavuḷ peyariṭṭatākak kūṟāmal, ātām peyariṭṭatākak kiṟitava maṟiyiṟ kūṟiyiruppatik kāṭṭiṉar mākkacu mullar.
tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu ulakattil oru moḻiyumilli.
17. moḻi makkaḷ kūṭṭuṟavā lāyatu.
moḻi oruvaṉāl amintataṉṟu. oruvaṉ taṉittu vāḻvāṉā yiṉ avaṉukku moḻiyē vēṇṭiyatilli. makkaḷ oṉṟāyk kūṭi uṟavāṭumpōtē taṅkaḷ karuttip piṟarkkut terivikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. ataṉāl moḻi piṟakkiṟatu.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷil, cila toḻilpaṟṟiyavi; cila tiṇipaṟṟiyavi; cila kulampaṟṟiyavi; cila vaḻipāṭu paṟṟiyavi. iṅṅaṉam palavakiyil pala vakuppārāl tōṟṟappeṟṟa coṟkaḷellām oṉṟucērntatē moḻiyākum.
ikkālattil uvilkkiṉ pōṉṟa oruvar, iṭukuṟikaḷik koṇṭu oru putu moḻiyamikka muṭiyumāyiṉum, iyaṟki yāyp poruḷ kuṟitteḻunta ulaka mutaṉmoḻi oruvarāl taṉippaṭa iyaṟṟappaṭṭatākātu.
18. moḻi acikaḷākavum coṟkaḷākavum tōṉṟiṟṟu.
moḻiyiṉ kaṭicic ciṟṟuṟuppu eḻuttākak kūṟuvatu, eḻuttum ilakkaṇamum ēṟpaṭṭa piṟkālattatākum.
tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭum nālvakic coṟkaḷuḷ, ilakkaṇavakic coṟkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟattakkavi peyar viṉi iṭi eṉṉum mutal mūṉṟumē. iṟutiyiluḷḷa uriccol ilakkaṇavakic collaṉṟeṉpatu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
mūvakic coṟkaḷuḷ muntit tōṉṟiyatu iṭi; ataṉ piṉṉatu viṉi; ataṉ piṉṉatu peyar.
kā:
kū(eṉṟu kattiṉāṉ)	-	iṭiccol
kūvi			-	viṉiccol
kūval			- 	peyarccol
tamiḻukku ilakkaṇam ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, immūvakic coṟ kaḷum tarukkanūṉ muṟippaṭi, kīḻiṟakkap perumi muṟiyil māṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
moḻittōṟṟam mutalāvatu coṟṟoṭar amippit taḻuviyateṉṟu cāy kūṟuvatu, moḻinūṉ muṟikku māṟāṉatu.
19. moḻi orē camayattil uṇṭāṉataṉṟu.
makkaṭku varavarak karuttu vaḷarkiṉṟatu. ataṉāl moḻiyum vaḷarkiṉṟatu. mutal māntaṉ kuḻantipōlvaṉ. avaṉukku ūṇuṭi mutaliya cila poruḷkaḷiruntāl pōtum. nākarikam vaḷara vaḷarattāṉ karuttukaḷ palkum. appōtu coṟkaḷum palkum.
māntaṉ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiyataṟkum, makkaṭkuḷ kūṭṭuṟavuṇ ṭāṉataṟkum iṭiyil, neṭuṅkālam ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭu mātaliṉ, akkālamellām vaḷarcci yiliruntutāṉ piṉṉar moḻi uruvākiyirukkum.
20. moḻikkali ōr ākkak kaliyākum.
kalikaḷ iyaṟkali (Physical Science), ākkakkali (Historical Science) eṉa iruvakippaṭum, pūtanūl ōr iyaṟkali. ōviyam ōr ākkakkali. atupōla moḻikkaliyum ōr ākkak kaliyē.
21. moḻi potumakkaḷālum nūlkaḷ pulavarālum ākkap peṟṟavi.
22. orumoḻi cila kāraṇattāl palavāyp piriyalām.
oru perumoḻi pala ciṟumoḻikaḷākap pirintu pōvataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa:
1. maṉpatipperukkam
oru moḻiyip pēcuvōr, mikappalki oru viyaṉilap parappil paraviviṭuvārāyiṉ, taṭpaveppanili vēṟupāṭu cēymi, mutaliyavaṟṟāl, pala moḻivaḻakkukaḷ tōṉṟi, nāḷaṭivil vēṟu moḻikaḷākap pirintuviṭum.
potuvāy, 100 mil cēymiyil iraṇṭoru coṟkaḷum, 200 mil cēymiyil cila coṟkaḷum, 400 mil cēymiyil moḻivaḻakkum, ataṟkappāl moḻiyum vēṟupaṭuvatiyalpu. ip potuvitikkuc cila tavircci (exception) kaḷumuṇṭu.
2. olittiripu (Phonetic Decay and Phonetic Variation)
1. cōmpalāl.
kā. paḷam (paḻam)
ii. uṟuppu vēṟupāṭṭāl.
taṭpaveppanili, nilacciṟappu, ūṇ, toḻil, paḻakkavaḻakkam, iyaṟki mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, eḻuttolikkuk karuviyākum uṟuppukaḷ, ovvoru nāṭṭāriṭattum nuṭpamāy vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl, olippumuṟiyum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. maliyāḷattil vali meliyātalum (Nasalization) kaṉṉaṭattil pakaram hakaramātalum, teluṅkil ḻakaram ṭakaramātalum kāṇka.
3. coṟṟiripu (Verbal Changes)
i.  īṟukeṭal (Discardance of Inflection)
ii. īṟutirital (Inflectional Changes)
iii.  pōli (Mutation)

v. marūu (Disguise)
vi. citivu (Corruption)
vii. mukkuṟi (Apherosis Syncope and Apocope)
viii. muccērppu (Prosthesis, Epenthesis and Epithesis)
ix. aṟutiripu 
x. etuki (Rhyming)
xi. vaḻūuppakuppu (Metanalysis)
xii. x¥ò( Analogy)
xiii. mēṟpaṭiyamippu (Superstructure) (uṇṭu eṉṉum tamiḻ viṉimuṟṟip pakutiyākakkoṇṭu uṇṭāṭu, uṇṭāti mutaliya teluṅku viṉimuṟṟukaḷi yamippatu, mēṟpaṭi yamippākum.
4. poruṭṭiripu (Semantic Changes).

5. coṟṟerintu kōṭal (Natural Selection)
teluṅku il eṉṉuñ colliyum, kaṉṉaṭam maṉi eṉṉuñ colliyum tamiḻiṉiṉṟum terintukoṇṭatu coṟṟerintu kōṭal.
6. vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkal (Colonial Preservation)(English, past and present by Trench. p. 55)
nōli eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ mūlam teluṅkil nūṉa (eḷney) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkal.
7. putuccollākkam.
tāyvaḻakki lillāta col, tokiccol, īṟu, oṭṭuc coṟkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip putitāy ākkik koḷḷal.
8. tāyvaḻakkoṭu toṭarpiṉmi.
cēranāṭṭut tamiḻ, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉārkkup piṉ, tamiḻoṭu toṭarpiṉmiyālēyē vēṟu moḻiyāyp piriya nērntatu.
9. piṟamoḻik kalappu (Admixture of foreign elements)
mēṟkūṟiya tiripu muṟikaḷellām, cōmpalālum aṟiyāmi yālum nērntu oru payaṉum payavātiruppiṉ, moḻiccitivu (Linguistic Corruption) eṉṉum kēṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇamākum; aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi, ataṟku maṟutaliyāyiruppiṉ, moḻi vaḻakku vaḷarcci (Dialectic Regeneration) eṉṉum ākkattiṟkuk kāraṇa mākum.
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉatu, tirāviṭar tavaṟumaṉṟu, āriyar tavaṟumaṉṟu, moḻi vaḻakku vaḷarcciyēyākum. āṉāl, am moḻikaḷil vaṭacoṟ kaḷik kalantatum, am moḻikaḷit tamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭut tiṉatum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku vaṭamoḻiyit tāyākak kāṭ ṭiyatum, tamiḻit tāḻttiviṭṭu vaṭa moḻiyit talimi yākkiyatum, āriyattāl tirāviṭarkku nērnta tīmikaḷākum, mikap periya tirāviṭa maṉpatiyi āriyar mutalāvatu pala pirivākap pirittatu moḻiyiṉāl eṉpatu, itaṉāl viḷaṅkum. moḻiyiṉāl tirāviṭarip pala pirivākap pirittapiṉ, matattālum varaṇavoḻukkattālum tamiḻarip pala pirivākap pirittaṉar. (itupōtu nāṭṭiyaliyakkattālum tamiḻar pirikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉar.)
teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷ, centamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuc cēyavātalālum, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya āṭcikaṭkappāṟpaṭṭa vātalālum, cila nalla colvaṭivukaḷi yuṭiyavātalālum,  tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintu kiḷimoḻikaḷāy, vēṟāka veṇṇap paṭuvataṟkut takutiyuṭiyaṉavē. āṉāl. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari cēranāṭṭut tamiḻāyirunta maliyāḷa moḻi. tamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟāka eṇṇappaṭuvataṟkuc caṟṟum takutiyuṭiyataṉṟu. maliyāḷa moḻiyik keṭutta āriyaruḷ talimiyāṉavar, tamiḻeḻuttiṉ vakikaḷāṉa vaṭṭeḻuttilum kōleḻut tilumē taṅkaḷ moḻiyi eḻutivanta maliyāḷikaṭku, āriyaveḻutti yamittuk koṭutta tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉ eṉpataṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamēyilli.

mukkiyamāṉa iraṇṭoru moḻikaḷiṟ pēcuntiṟaṉum, piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇavaṟivum collaṟivum, moḻi nūlārkkiruntāṟ pōtum.

pārppaṉarum tamiḻarum orē moḻi pēcuvatāl ōriṉamākār.
32.	makkaḷ nākarikattik kāṭṭuvataṟku moḻiyē ciṟanta aṭiyāḷam.
vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum māntaṉi vēṟupaṭuttuvatu moḻiyoṉṟē. am moḻiyum palatiṟappaṭṭu, avaṟṟip pēcum makkaḷiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷik kāṭṭuvatākum.
33.	moḻinūṟ kalikku naṭuvuniliyum poṟumiyum vēṇṭum.
iv viti ellāk kalikkum potuvāyiṉum, moḻinūṟ kalikkuc ciṟappāy vēṇṭumeṉpataṟku iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. oruvar perumi pārāṭṭikkoḷḷum poruḷkaḷuḷ, tāy moḻiyum oṉṟu. ākiyāl, ataṉ mītuḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl naṭuvu niliyi nekiḻaviṭak kūṭātu.
tamiḻnāṭṭil, naṭuvunili yārāyccikkut taṭiyāka īruṇarccika ḷiruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, āriyattiṟku māṟāka iruppatu uṇmiyāyiṉum kūṟakkūṭāteṉpatu. iṉṉoṉṟu matappaṟṟu, cuvāmināta tēcikar tiruvācakam, periya purāṇam mutaliyavaṟṟip paṭikkalāmeṉṟum, cīvaka cintāmaṇi, cilappati kāram mutaliyavaṟṟip paṭikkakkūṭāteṉṟum kūṟiyatu matap paṟṟākum. ōr ārāycciyāḷaṉ oṉṟi ārāyumpōtu, taṉṉi matamaṟṟavaṉpōlak karutiṉāloḻiya, ataṉ uṇmi kāṇamuṭiyāteṉpatu tēṟṟam.
kāytal uvattal akaṟṟi yoruporuṭkaṇ
āytal aṟivuṭiyār kaṇṇatē - kāyvataṉkaṇ
uṟṟa kuṇantōṉṟā tākum uvappataṉkaṇ
kuṟṟamun tōṉṟāk keṭum.
iṉi, cilar kālṭuvel kūṟiyavāṟu, oppiyalaṟiviṉṟi, oru tuṟi yilēyē amiḻntu, uḷḷam parantu viriyāmal kuvin toṭuṅkap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. nuṇṇiya matiyum ciṟanta ārāycci yiyalpum uḷḷa, kālañceṉṟa pā,vē. māṇikkanāyakar oppiyalaṟi viṉmi yāṟ pala ceytikaḷi uṇmikku māṟākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar.
ōṅkāra vaṭiviṉiṉṟum tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ vantaṉa veṉṟum; ō eṉṉum vaṭivam uma eṉṉum malimakaḷ peyarum liṅkak kuṟiyum cērntateṉṟum, civāyanama eṉṉum tiruvinteḻut til, vakaram aruḷiyum yakaram uyiriyum nakaram tirōtatti yum makaram malattiyuṅ kuṟikkumeṉṟum, āytam mūccoliyeṉ ṟum, ataṉ tuṇikoṇṭu arapi, hepirēyam pōṉṟa moḻi kaḷit tamiḻil eḻutalāmeṉṟum, uṭampaṭu mey eṉpatu uṭampu + aṭum + mey eṉṟu pirintu, piṟaviyik keṭuppa teṉṟu poruḷpaṭumeṉṟum, okarattiṉ mēl paṇṭik kālattiṟ puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutiṉatu, civa perumāṉiṉ takkaṇa vaṭivattik kāṭṭutaṟkeṉṟum, palavāṟu mayaṅkik kūṟiṉar pā,vē. māṇikkanāyakar.
iviyellām palavaki malivukaḷuṭiyaṉa eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar ellārkkum pulaṉākum. āyiṉum, ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟimaṭṭum, ēṉiyōrkkum teriyumpaṭi iṅkuk kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
y, v eṉṉum meykaḷ tamiḻil maṭṭumalla, enta moḻiyil īruyirkaḷip puṇarttālum, iṭiyil uṭampaṭu (uṭaṉ paṭuttum) meykaḷāyt tōṉṟattāṉ ceyyum. itu ellā moḻikkum potuvāṉa olinūl viti.

Drawer, sower mutaliya āṅkilaccoṟkaḷil, vakaravuṭampaṭu meykkup patilāka yakaravuṭampaṭumey varukiṟatēyeṉiṉ, vakarattiṉum yakaram olittaṟkeḷitātalpaṟṟi, piṟkālattil yakaravuṭampaṭumey peruvaḻakkāyiṟṟeṉak koḷka. tamiḻilum iṅṅaṉamē. āyiṭi, kōyil, cummāyiru mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷik kāṇka.
māṇikka nāyakarip piṉpaṟṟuñ cilar, avarip pōṉṟē pala coṟkaṭkut tavaṟāṉa poruḷum, tamatu karuttiṟkiciyāta nūṟpākkaḷiyum colvaṭivaṅkaḷiyum iṭiccerukalum pāṭavēṟupāṭu meṉṟum kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. avar viri nōkkaṭivārāka.
mēṉāṭṭār naṭuniliyum oppiyalaṟivum uṭiyarāyiṉum, tamiḻiṉ uṇmittaṉmiyik kāṇa muṭiyāmikkup piṉ varupavi kāraṇaṅkaḷākum;
1.	tamiḻ taṟpōtu intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil oru ciṟu nilapparappil vaḻaṅkal.
2.	(tamiḻuṭpaṭa) intiya moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum vaṭa coṟkaḷ kalantiruttal.
3.	(kaṭalkōḷālum, āriyattālum, tamiḻar tavaṟṟālum paṇṭit tamiḻnūlkaḷ aḻintupōṉapiṉ) intiyak kali nūlkaḷellām ippōtu vaṭamoḻiyiliruttal.
4. 	itupōtu pala tuṟikaḷilum pārppaṉar uyarvāyum tamiḻar tāḻvāyumiruttal.
5. 	pārppaṉar tamiḻariṉ matattalivarāyiruttal.
6. 	tamiḻippaṟṟit tamiḻarē aṟiyātiruttal.
7. 	airōppiyar tamiḻic cariyāyk kallāmi.
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻit tuṇi vēṇ ṭāta taṉimoḻi yeṉṟaṟintum, tamiḻar uyarnilik kalikaḷi āriyariṭattiṉiṉṟum kaṟṟatākat tavaṟṟeṇṇaṅ koṇṭami yāl, tamiḻil maṉam, āṉmā eṉpavaṟṟik kuṟikkac col lilliyeṉṟum, tiraviṭam, cēraṉ, cōḻaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟum, iṉṉa eṉṉum vaṭiviṟkotta akarac cuṭṭaṭiccol tamiḻililli yeṉṟum, piṟavāṟum piḻipaṭak kūṟiṉar.
uvilliyam (William), kōlpurūk (Colebrooke), mūyr (Muir) mutaliyavarōveṉiṉ, tamiḻic ciṟituṅ kallātu, vaṭa moḻiyilēyē mūḻkik kiṭantu, intiya nākarikamellām āriya vaḻiyākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar.
moḻinūṟkaliyum nūlārāycciyum varavara vaḷarntu varu kiṉṟaṉa. ākiyāl, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya pala moḻi nūṟ karuttukaḷ in nūṟṟāṇṭil aṭipaṭum. pārppaṉar, āriyatti uyarttik kūṟiya mēṉāṭṭār cilariṉ kūṟṟukaḷit taṅkaṭkēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷākap paṟṟikkoṇṭu, avaṟṟi māṟṟu kiṉṟa puttārāycci tōṉṟātapaṭi, pala vakiyil tamiḻari maṭṭantaṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar. mēṉāṭṭil, uṇmi kāṇa vēṇṭumeṉṟu peru muyaṟci naṭantu varukiṉṟatu; āṉāl  kīḻ nāṭṭilō uṇmiyi maṟikkavēṇṭu meṉṟē perumuyaṟci naṭantuvarukiṉṟatu. kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyāriṉ tirāviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṉ mutaliru patippukaḷilum, illāta (tirāviṭa nākarikatti yiḻittuk kūṟum) cila mēṟ koḷkaḷ, mūṉṟām patippiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi yellām moḻi nūṟ kaliyiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattik kuṟiyātu piṟpōkkiyē kuṟikkum.
moḻinūliṉ mutaṉmi
oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu, 1. eḻutappaṭṭa varalāṟu (Written History), 2. eḻutappaṭā varalāṟu (Unwritten History) eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa nāṭāyiṉ, perumpālum eḻutap paṭṭirukkum; muṟpaṭṭatāyiṉ, eḻutappaṭṭō paṭātō irukkum. eḻutappaṭā varalāṟu, 1. aṟiyappaṭṭa varalāṟu (Known History) 2. aṟiyappaṭā varalāṟu (Unknown History) eṉa iru tiṟattatu. varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷum karuvikaḷum cāṉṟu kaḷum pōtiya aḷavu iruppiṉ, aṟiyappaṭum; iṉṟēl illi. iṉi, eḻutappaṭṭa varalāṟum,
mey varalāṟu (True History), poy varalāṟu (False History) eṉa iruvakittu: oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu annāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṟṟum naṭuniliyum uḷḷavarāl eḻutappaṭiṉ perumpālum meyyā yirukkum: vēṟṟiṉap pakivarālum taṉṉiṉak koṇṭāṉ mārālum (Quislings) eḻutappaṭiṉ perumpālum poyyāyiruk kum. tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyaruḷ P.T. cīnivāciyaṅkārum, irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum, T.R. cēca aiyaṅkārum tavirap piṟarellārum perumpālum āriyac cārpāṉavarum naṭunili tiṟampiyavarumātaliṉ, kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa varalāṟṟip perumpālum uḷḷapaṭiyum, muṟpaṭṭa varalāṟṟi muṟṟum tirittum eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuk karaṇiyam, kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭatu perumpālum āriyak kalappu illatāyum, tamiḻarkkup perumi taruvatāyum, piṟpaṭṭatu āriyak kalappu uḷḷatāyum, āriyarkkē perumi taruvatāyum iruppatē.
eḻutappaṭā varalāṟṟi eḻutuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭum cāṉṟukaḷ, ilakkiyam, veṭṭeḻuttu (Inscriptions) paḻamporuḷ nūlkaḷ (Archeology) eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ilakkiyattuḷ tolkāp piyamum iraṇṭoru puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuṭkaḷum tirukkuṟaḷum tavira maṟṟap paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷellām aḻiyuṇṭu pōṉamiyāl kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟi eḻutuvataṟku vēṇṭiya ilakkiyac cāṉṟu illātē pōyiṟṟu; itu pōtuḷḷa veṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ perumpālum ki.pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭaviyātaliṉ, aviyum payaṉpaṭa villi. iṉi, paḻam poruṭ kaliyōveṉiṉ, kaṟkālattu iruppuk kālattup parupporuṭ ceytikaḷit tavira, oḻuṅ kāṉa varalāṟṟiṟkuriya nuṇ kuṟippukkaḷit terivikkātatā yiruppatālum, atiyum ārāytaṟkuriya mutaṟkālat tamiḻaka mākiya paḻam pāṇṭiya nāṭu (kumarikkaṇṭam) muḻutum muḻukip pōṉamiyālum, atuvum payaṉpaṭātatē. ākavē, eñci niṟkum orē cāṉṟu moḻiyē.
tamiḻ, āriyar varukikkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumarik kaṇṭattil tāṉākat tōṉṟi muḻuvaḷarcci tiripu uṟavu mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷakkum moḻinūliṉ tuṇi koṇṭē, tamiḻiṉ iyalpiyum paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟiyum oruvāṟu aṟiyamuṭiyum.
moḻinūliṉ variyaṟi (Definition)
moḻikaḷ tōṉṟiya vaki, avi vaḷarnta muṟi, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟukkuḷḷa toṭarpu mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷakkum nūl moḻinūlām (Philology).
orē moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiyellām ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭu, amippu, piṟappu, poruḷurttumuṟi, tiripu mutaliyavaṟṟi āḷum neṟimuṟikaḷi viḷakkum nūl col liyal nūlām. (Etymology). ākavē, colliyalnūl moḻi nūliṉ uṭkūṟām. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu piṉṉataṉuḷ aṭaṅkum; piṉṉatu muṉṉataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātu.
moḻinūl iṉṉum uruvākāmi
moḻinūlippaṟṟi makkaḷukku iṉṉum cariyāṉa uṇarcciyum nampikkiyum ēṟpaṭavilli. kaṇitam, vāṉūl mutaliya kalikaḷippōla moḻinūlum oru tiṭṭamāṉa neṟimuṟip paṭṭa kaliyē. cilar moḻinūlic colliyal nūlākak koṇṭum coṟkaḷiṉ piṟappu vaḷarccikaḷippaṟṟiya neṟi muṟikaḷi aṟiyātum, oli yoppumiyum poruntap pukalal eṉṉum uttiyumpaṟṟic coṟkaṭkut tāṟumāṟāyp poruṭ kāraṇamum mūlamum kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, moḻinūlē ōr uṉṉa allatu kaṟpaṉik kalitāṉ eṉkiṟa muṭipip palar koṇṭuviṭṭaṉar. palakalikaḷ āyvu (Empirical) nilikaṭantu uruvākiyuḷḷaṉa. moḻinūl iṉṉum āyvu niliyiltā ṉuḷḷatu; āyiṉum, mika aṇukiya kālattil uruvākiviṭum eṉpatu tiṇṇam. mēṉāṭṭu vāṉūṟkali car aicakku niyūṭṭaṉ kālattiltāṉ avarāl uruvāyiṟṟu. ataṟkumuṉ atu vaḻivaram piṉṟip paḻipaṭu niliyiltā ṉiruntatu. atu pōṉṟē iṟṟi moḻi nūlum.
ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷellām ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu pala muṟiyākap paravip pōṉavarkaḷē eṉpataṟku maṟukka muṭiyāta cāṉṟukaḷuḷa. aṅṅaṉam paravicceṉṟavarkaḷ moḻi tōṉṟumuṉ ceṉṟavarum moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ ceṉṟavarum eṉa irutiṟattār. moḻi tōṉṟumuṉ ceṉṟavar tām kuṭiyēṟiya nāṭṭil nāḷaṭivil tattam cuṟṟuc cārpiṟkum karuttiṟkum olikkun tiṟaṉukkum ēṟṟavāṟu ovvōr moḻiyi ākkik koṇṭaṉar. attakiya moḻikaḷiṭiyē cila moḻiyākka neṟimuṟikaḷiyaṉṟic colloppumi kāṇpatu mikavum aritu. teṉ āppirikka moḻikaḷum ciṉam cappāṉ mutaliya kīḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷum, amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷum ivvakik keṭuttuk kāṭṭām. āriyarum tirāviṭarum moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ pirintu pōṉavarātaliṉ, avvirucārār moḻikaṭkum collilum neṟimuṟiyilum periyatōr nerukkamuṇṭām. āyiṉum, avar iṭattil neṭṭiṭiyiṭṭup pirintu kālattil neṭ ṭiṭiyiṭṭuk kūṭiyamiyiṉ, iṭikkālattil olimuṟiyilum collamippilum coṟṟoṭar ilakkaṇattilum mikunta vēṟupāṭu uṇṭāyviṭṭatu.
oru cikkuppaṭṭa nūṟkaṇṭic cikkaleṭuppataṟku mutalāvatu ataṉ iru muṉikaḷil oṉṟik kāṇavēṇṭum. oru poru ḷurimi paṟṟiya vaḻakkit tīrppataṟku mutalāvatu, mutaṉmutal ati vittiruntavaṉ yār eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamē. moḻinūli uruvākkuvataṟku, mutalāvatu, māntaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭam etu veṉṟum toṭarpuḷḷa moḻikaṭkuḷ etu muntiyatu eṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum, potuvāka, muntiya moḻikaḷil colvaṭivam iyalpāyum pintiya moḻikaḷil tirintum iruk kum. tirintamoḻiyi muntiṉatākavum iyalpāṉa moḻiyip pintiṉatākavuṅ koṇṭu ārāyappukiṉ, pēraṉip pāṭṭa ṉākak koṇṭu marapuvaḻi kūṟuvatum ciṟitu citintupōṉa ēṭṭi allatu kalveṭṭit talikīḻ vittut tavaṟākap poruḷ koḷvatum pōṉṟatēyākum. mēṉāṭṭār iṉ ṉum tamiḻic cevviyāy ārāyāta tiṉālēyē, avarkaḷ moḻinūl iyaṉmoḻit tōṟṟatti allatu vērccol tōṟṟatti aṟiyamuṭiyāta colliyal nūlāyum iṉamoḻi nūlāyum niṉṟuviṭṭatu.
moḻinūlukkut tamiḻiṉ mukkiyam
māntaṉ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiyaviṭam kumari nāṭē (Lemuria) yeṉpatu tamiḻaṟiyāmalē cila mēṉāṭṭukkali vallār kaṇṭa muṭipākum. tamiḻc carittiramum tiraviṭa moḻinūlum itaṟkut takka cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. tiraviṭarukkuḷ moḻiyilum ilakkiyat tilum nākarikattilum tūymippaṭṭavar iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭi luḷḷa tamiḻarē. tamiḻnāṭṭuḷḷum moḻit tiruttam teṟku nōkkiyatē. tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr iruntatu teṉperuṅ kaṭalil muḻukiyuḷḷa kumari nāṭu. atu oru kālattil kōṭikkaṇak kāṉa allatu ilakkakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ nili peṟṟirun tatu. paṉimali (imayam) tōṉṟiyataṟku muṉpē atu toṉmutu nāṭāyiruntatu. tamiḻ vaṭakku nōkkic cellac cellac ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tirintu piṉpu tiraviṭamākiṉṟatu. tiraviṭamum vaṭakku nōkkic ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tirintu piṉpu āriya mākiṉṟatu. tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ airōppāvari ceṉṟu ilattīṉ kirēkkam mutaliya mutuperumoḻikaḷilum kalantuḷḷaṉa. intiyāviṉ paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ tiraviṭarē. ivaṟṟi nōkkumpōtu, tamiḻc carittiram intiya carittirattiṟkum tiraviṭamoḻi nūlukkum maṭṭumaṉṟi, ulaka carittirattiṟkum intiya airōppiya oppiyaṉ moḻi nūlukkumē aṭippaṭiyāku meṉpatu pulaṉām.
tamiḻ kumari nāṭṭil tāṉē tōṉṟiya moḻiyātalāṉum, tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyākavum āriyattiṟku mutaṟṟāyākavum iruttalāṉum, colliyal tiṟavukōliyum moḻinūl tiṟavukōliyum taṉṉakattēyē koṇṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ tiraviṭat tāyeṉpatu ippākattiṉ piṟpakutiyilum, tamiḻc collākka muṟikaḷ centamiḻc colliyal neṟi muṟikaḷ eṉṉum nūlilum kūṟappaṭum.
tamiḻiṉ talimiyi aṟitaṟkut taṭikaḷ
ulaka moḻikaḷukkuḷ tamiḻ talimiyāṉatāyiruppiṉum, 1. carittiramaṟiyāmi, 2. colliyalakarātiyiṉmi, 3. mutunūlka ḷiṟantupaṭṭami, 4. moḻipaṟṟiya tavaṟāṉa araciyaṟ kaṭcik koḷki, 5. kalavi moḻinaṭi, 6. tamiḻaṉ aṭimiyuṇarcci, 7. tamiḻp paṟṟillātār kalvi niliyaṅkaḷilum āṭciyiṭaṅ kaḷilumiruttal, 8. ārāycciyiṉmi, 9. matappaṟṟiṉāl piṟamoḻi taḻuval, 10. perumpāṉmit tamiḻariṉ kallāmi mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl tamiḻiṉ perumi tamiḻarālum aṟiyap paṭāmal irukkiṉṟatu. intiya carittirattit teṟkiruntu toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum; intiya nākarikam tiraviṭam eṉpatiyum; tiraviṭa uṭalamippit tiraviṭa maṉṉar, tamiḻa vēḷir, vēḷāḷar mutaliyār mutaliya kulattiṉar eṉṟivariṭit tāṉ kāṇa muṭiyumeṉpatiyum; tiraviṭaṉik kāṭṭu mirāṇṭi yākac carittira nūlkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiyiruppatum kūṟiyiruppatum perun tavaṟeṉpatiyum; āriyar varumuṉpē, aṉṟaṉṟu, paṉi mali tōṉṟumuṉpē, kumari nāṭṭil civamum (cēyōṉ vaḻipāṭu) māliyamum (māyōṉ vaḻipāṭu) muṟiyē kuṟiñci mullit tamiḻar mataṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa eṉpatiyum; ulakiyalum mataviyalum paṟṟiya tiruntiya paḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭi lēyē uḷḷaṉa eṉpatiyum; ennāṭṭilum makkaḷuḷ tāḻntōr uyarntōr eṉa iru cārār iruppatu pōl tamiḻ nāṭṭilum toṉṟu toṭṭu uḷḷaṉareṉpatiyum; avaruḷ uyarntōriṉ mataṅkaḷiyum paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷiyumē āriyar mēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟit tamavākak kāṭṭiṉar eṉpatiyum; moḻikaḷil tēvamoḻiyeṉa oṉṟilli yeṉpatiyum; aṅṅaṉamiruppiṉ, atu iyaṟkiyum eḷimiyum veḷip paṭi yum naṭunili aṉpu piṟappoppu vēḷāṇmi mutaliya karut taṟivippum paṟṟit tamiḻēyām eṉpatiyum; paḻantamiḻar piṟa tuṟikaḷiṟ pōṉṟē moḻi, ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉpavaṟ ṟilum taliciṟantiruntaṉar eṉpatiyum; tām karutiya ellāk karuttukkaḷiyum aṟinta ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum kuṟikkac collamit tiruntaṉar eṉpatiyum aṟital vēṇṭum.
itaṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyi mutaṉmoḻiyāyum vaṭanūli muta ṉūlāyum vittut tamiḻārāyappukiṉ, uṇmikku nēr māṟāṉa muṭipukaḷē tōṉṟu meṉpatiyum, vīra cōḻiyam ilakkaṇakkottu pirayōkavivēkam mutaliyaṉa aḷavi nūl kaḷākā eṉpatiyum uṇartal vēṇṭum.
iyalpāṉa moḻikaḷum coṟkaḷum oru neṟippaṭṭē tōṉṟi iyaṅkukiṉṟamiyiṉ, muṟippaṭiyārāyiṉ avaṟṟiṉ neṟimuṟikaḷi yellām kaṇṭu koḷḷalām eṉpatu, ic cuṭṭu viḷakkatti naṭuniliyāy nuṇuki nōkkuvārkku iṉitu pulaṉām.
eṉatu moḻiyārāycci kuṉṟāvāṟu iṭiyiṭi ūkkivarum eṉ naṇpar tirumāṉ va. cuppiyā piḷḷi avarkaṭkum piṟarkkum yāṉ mikamikak kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ.
ivvārāyccikkum ataṉ veḷiyīṭṭiṟkum tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāyiruntutaviyaruḷum ellā valla iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭi kaḷi neñcāra niṉittut taliyāra vaṇaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
moḻinūṟ kulivu
moḻinūlāṉatu varalāṟṟiyal, vaṇṇaṉiyiyal, oppu nōkkiyal eṉṉum mūviyalkaḷiyum oruṅkēyuṭiyatēṉum, mēlimoḻinūlār iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻi aṭippaṭi yākak koḷḷātu tiripil tiripākiya āriyatti aṭippaṭi yāka vittu āyntatiṉāl, mūlamoḻiyiyum atu tōṉṟiya vakiyiyuṅ kāṇātu kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kurī ippōl iṭarp paṭṭu, varalāṟṟiyali aṭiyōṭu vilakkiviṭṭu vaṇṇaṉi yiyaliyum oppiyaliyumē kiyāṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.
oru kuṭumpattiluḷḷa makkaḷuḷ ovvoruvariyum paṟṟi, ivar iṉṉapāliṉar; iṉṉa niṟattar; iṉṉavaḷarttiyar; iṉṉa tōṟṟattar; iṉṉa iyalpiṉar; iṉṉa tiṟamiyar eṉṟu kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatu vaṇṇaṉi moḻiyiyal (Descriptive Linguistics).
oru kuṭumpattār ellāriyum oppunōkki, ikkuṭumpattil ittaṉiyar āṭavar; ittaṉiyar peṇṭir; ittaṉiyar civappar; ittaṉiyar karuppar; ittaṉiyar neṭiyar; it taṉiyar kuṟiyar; ittaṉiyar ottavar; ittaṉiyar vēṟupaṭṭa var eṉṟurippatu pōṉṟatu oppiyaṉ moḻinūl. (Comparative Linguistics).
oru kuṭumpattāruḷ, iṉṉār tantiyar; iṉṉār tāyār; iṉṉār putalvar; iṉṉār putalviyar; iṉṉār tamiyaṉār; iṉṉār tampimār; iṉṉār tamakkiyār; iṉṉār taṅki mār eṉṟiṅkaṉaṅ kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatu varalāṟṟu moḻinūl (Historical Linguistics.).
varalāṟṟu moḻinūl moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟa vaḷarccikaḷik kūṟuvatāl ēṉiyīriyalkaḷiṉuñ ciṟantatām. oppiyaṉ moḻinūl varalāṟṟi aṟiyat tuṇiceyvatāl, ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākac ciṟantatām. vaṇṇaṉimoḻinūl piṟamoḻit toṭarpu nōkkātu oru moḻiyi vaṇṇippatu maṭṭum ceyvatāl, uṇmiyil ilakkaṇattiṉpāṟ paṭuvatē. iṅṅaṉam im mūṉṟiyalpum muṟiyē taliyiṭi kaṭiyām.
mēliyar mūlamoḻiyiyum moḻi tōṉṟiya vakiyiyum aṟiyāmiyāl, avar varalāṟṟu moḻinūleṉṟu koḷvatu, ilakkiyat tuṇikoṇṭu oru moḻiyiṉ allatu moḻik kuṭumpattiṉ palvēṟu nilikaḷit toṭarcciyākak kāla muṟippaṭi kūṟuvatēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. itu oru moḻiyiṉ muḻuc coṟṟokutiyi maṭṭumaṉṟit taṉic coṟkaḷiyun taḻuvum.
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutivari ulaka mutaṉmoḻi etuveṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭāviṭiṉum, eṉṟēṉum orunāḷ atu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭum eṉṉum nampikkiyō nampāciyō mēṉāṭukaḷil iruntu vantatu, innūṟṟāṇṭilō atu muṟṟuṅ kiviṭappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. ataṉ viḷivē vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl. itic ciṟappāka vaḷarttu varupavar amerikkarē. avar tamiḻic ceṅkoḷkit tamiḻp pulavar vāyilākak kaṟkum vari, ivvaḻuviyal moḻinūl mēṉāṭukaḷil toṭa rattāṉ ceyyum. āyiṉum tamiḻar āriya aṭimit taṉattāṟ piṟanta tam tāḻvuṇarcci nīṅkip pakuttaṟivuk kaṇ koṇṭu pārppārāyiṉ, tamiḻiṉ muṉmiyiyum piṟamoḻi kaḷiṉ vaḻinilittaṉmiyiyum kāṇat tavaṟār eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
moḻi paṟṟiya cila vuṇmikaḷ
1. moḻimāntaṉ amippē.
ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ etuvum iṟivaṉ paṭittataṉṟu. iyaṟki yolikaḷāṉa kiḷavikaḷum ceyaṟki yolikaḷāṉa coṟkaḷu māka, ovvoṉṟāyp piyap piyap pallāyira māṇṭuk kālañ celaviṭṭu, oru makkaṭ kūṭṭattār kūṭṭuṟa vāl ōr oḻuṅkupaṭa amittukkoṇṭa olittokutiyē moḻi.
ulaka moḻikaḷ ēṟattāḻa mūvāyiram mutal nālāyiram vari vevvēṟu tokiyiṉavākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mānta rellārum ōriṇip peṟṟōriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟip palki vēṟu paṭṭiruttal pōṉṟē, moḻikaḷum orē mūla moḻiyiṉiṉṟu tirintu palvēṟu vakiyil vēṟupaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. karuttunikaḻviṟku moḻi tēviyilli.
nilittiṇi tavira maṟṟellā uyiriṉaṅkaṭkum karuttuṇṭu. moḻipēcāta ūmiyarum pēcuvār pōṉṟē karutuvar. ciṟappākak kaṭṭapommaṉ iḷavali nōkkuka.
uṇarcci, viruppu, cūḻvu, tuṇipu, makiḻcci, iraṅkal, niṉippu eṉṉum eḻuvakiyē karuttiṉ iyalkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṟku moḻi tēviyilli.
3. tāymoḻiyuṅ kaṟkappaṭuvatē.
oru kuḻaviyi ariyāṇṭiṟkuḷ tāyiṉiṉṟu pirittu makkaḷ moḻit toṭarpillātu vittu vaḷarttāl, ettaṉiyāṇ ṭāyiṉum emmoḻiyum pēcātu.
oru tamiḻak kuḻaviyip piṟantavuṭaṉ ōr āṅkila mātiṭam vaḷarkkaviṭṭāl, atu nāḷaṭivil āṅkila moḻiyē pēcum; ōr arapi mātiṭam vaḷarkka viṭṭāl, vāyamippu nuṇ vēṟupāṭṭāl olippu muṟiyiṟ caṟṟu iṭarppaṭiṉum, arapi moḻiyē pēcum.
ovvoru piḷḷiyum, kuḻantip paruvattiṟ peṟṟōriṭamum, piḷḷip paruvattiṟ peṟṟōr uṟṟōriṭamum, piyaṟ paruvattiṟ peṟṟōr uṟṟōr maṟṟōriṭamum, ovvoru collāka muyaṟci varuttamum viṉiyuṇarcciyu miṉṟik kaṟṟē taṉ tāy moḻiyip pēcap payilkiṟatu.
tāymoḻiyi naṉku pēcak kaṟṟapiṉpum, atiṟ pēcuvati niṟuttiviṭṭup pallāṇṭu ayaṉmoḻiyilēyē pēcivariṉ, tāymoḻip pēccāṟṟal mikakkuṟiyum.
4. moḻivāḻiṭam nāvē.
moḻiyeṉpatu, oru vilaṅkum pūtamum pōṉṟu makkaṭku appāṟpaṭṭa ōr uruvamaṉṟu. oru makkaḷ vakuppār tam karut tukkaḷip piṟarkkut terivikkum vāyilākap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭum olittokutiyē moḻi. oli nikaḻvatu vāyil. ākavē, moḻivāḻiṭam nāvē. moḻivalar nāvalar. moḻit teyvam nāmakaḷ. oru colli vaḻaṅkāviṭiṉ, atu oliyiḻakkum. ataṉāl iṟantupaṭum. atu ilakkiyattil eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉ, piṇampōṟ kiṭappatumiṉṟi maṟin toḻiyum, pala coṟkaḷ oliyiḻappiṉ oru pakutiyum, ellāccoṟkaḷum oliyiḻappiṉ muḻuvatum, oru moḻi yaḻiyum. orumoḻi āṭcimoḻiyāyiṉ, potumakkaḷ ataṉ coṟkaḷiyē āḷa virumpuvar; ataṉāl, intiyāl tamiḻ keṭātu eṉṟu pērāyattalivar colvatu pitaṟṟalē.
āṅkilam āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu tamiḻil illāta aṟiviyal ilakkiyac ciṟappiṉālēyē. avviyalpu intikkilli. mēlum, āṅkilarkkut tamiḻi yoḻikka vēṇṭum eṉṉum kuṟik kōḷilli. vaṭamoḻiyārkkum intiyārkkum aḵtuṇṭu.
moḻiyeṉpatu uṇmiyil olittokutiyē. makkaḷ aṟivuṅkaruttum cēymiyarkkum piṟkālattārkkum payaṉ paṭu māṟu, moḻiyiṉ oliyuṟuppukkaḷik kaṭpulaṉākiya varivaṭi vil eḻutikkāṭṭuvatē ilakkiyam eṉṉum eḻuttu moḻi. ākavē, makkaḷiṉṟik karuttilli; karuttiṉṟi oliyilli; oli yiṉṟi varivaṭiveṉṉum eḻuttilli; eḻuttiṉṟi eḻutap paṭṭa ilakkiyamilli.
ākavē, āriyavētam māntaṉālum iṟivaṉālum iyaṟṟap paṭātu tāṉē tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟum, marikkāṭu eṉṉum ūrp peyari maṟikkāṭu eṉṟu māṟṟi, vētaṅkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭut tirukkāppucceyta iṭameṉṟum, kūṟi makkaḷi yēmāṟṟuvatu, tuṇiccalāṉa poyyum kaṭun taṇṭaṉikkuriya kuṟṟamumākum.
5. camaṟkirutam ilakkiya moḻiyē.
intiya āriyar moḻi vaḻakkaṟṟapiṉ eḻunta vēta ilakkiya moḻi tamiḻoṭu kalantuṟṟa virivē camaṟkirutam. atu oru kālum pēcappaṭṭataṉṟu. ātalāl, atu piṟantatumilli; iṟantatumilli; ceyaṟkiyākap puṉiyappaṭṭa pāvi pōṉṟatē. iti mēliyar iṉi aṟivar.
6. ellā moḻiyum kaṭaṉ koṇṭu vaḷarcci peṟā.
uyaravuyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākātu. kaṭivāḷamuñ cēṇamum iṭṭālum kaḻuti kutiriyākātu. oru moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭiya, ēṟkeṉavē coṟperukkum vērkcol vaḷamuṅ koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum; allatu, āṅkilampōṟ putuppuṉivāḷar moḻiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum. iraṇṭu millā inti pōṉṟa intiya moḻikaḷellām āṅkilam pōl aṟiviyal moḻikaḷākap paṇpaṭuttap paṭavēṇṭu meṉṟu, aḷaviṟantu potumakkaḷ paṇattic celaviṭṭu, āṅkilatti yoḻikkap pārkkum mūṭak koḷkiyiyum muṭṭāḷ taṉattiyum, pakaṭit taṉamāyp paḻittukkāṭṭat teṉṉāliyirāmaṉ pōṉṟa tiṟamikka nakiyāṇṭi nāṭṭi lilli.
7. centamiḻum nāppaḻakkam
tāymoḻiyil tērcci peṟutaṟkē pēccup paḻakkam vēṇṭu meṉiṉ, ayaṉ moḻiyil tiṟampeṟa ataṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyic colla vēṇṭuvatilli. āṅkilaṅ kaṟkum māṇavarkku ilakkaṇaṅ kaṟpittal maṭṭum pōtātu; pēccup payiṟcikkum pōtiya vāyppaḷittal vēṇṭum.
moḻipeyar muṟi
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷi allatu kuṟiyīṭukaḷi vēṟu moḻiyil peyarkkum muṟikaḷ aintām. aviyāvaṉa:-
1. nērccol (e.kā: Pen -ötš)
2. vērpporuṭ col. (e.kā: motor - iyaṅki)
3. ciṟappiyaṟ col. ( e-kā: trin - puki vaṇṭi)
4. oppolic col 
5. mutaleḻuttuc col
ivaṟṟuḷ, mutalatu muṉṉamēyuḷḷa col, ēṉiyavellām putitāy ākkappeṟuvaṉa.
iv ai vakiyuḷ, mutal mūṉṟum tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkumā? eṉṉum muntiya kaṭṭuriyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. ēṉiyiraṇṭē iṅkuk kūṟappeṟum.
oppolic collāvatu, oliyilum poruḷilum okkumāṟu amittukkoḷḷap peṟum col.

Parler eṉpatu pēcu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum pireñcu viṉic col; ment eṉpatu oru pireñcut toḻiṟ peyarīṟu.

braccal eṉpatu breeches (kuṟuṅkāṟ caṭṭikaḷ) eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum ilattīṉ col. braga eṉpatu ataṉ icuppāṉiyat (Spanish) tiripu. bragueta eṉpatu ataṉ kuṟumipporuḷ vaṭivam (diminutive). ataṉiṉṟu mutalil bragget eṉṟum, piṉṉar bracket eṉṟum āṅkilac col tirintatu.

mutaleḻuttuc collāvatu, oru poruḷip paṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉic coṟkaḷ mutaleḻuttukkaḷi maṭṭum cērttu oru peyar allatu kuṟiyīṭu amittal.

āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷellām poruḷaḷavil peruvaḷaṅ koṇṭaṉa vēṉum, collaḷavil mika eḷiya muṟiyilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa. mēṟkūṟiya aim muṟikaḷiyum kiyāḷiṉ ellā aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum tamiḻil eḷitāy moḻi peyarttuviṭalām. tāymoḻip paṟṟoṉṟē tēvi.  (teṉmoḻi.)
moḻiyārāycci (COMPARATIVE PHILOLOGY)
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām Turanian, Semitic, Aryan eṉa mupperuṅ kavikaḷā yaṭaṅkum. ivaṟṟuḷ ovvoru kaviyum paṟpala kiḷikaḷāyp piriyum,. ovvoru kiḷiyum ovvoru moḻit tokutiyākum. ivaṟṟuḷ, turēṉiyak kaviyil tirāviṭak (Dravidian)»is¡F¤ talimiyākak karutappaṭuvatu amiḻtiṉumiṉiya tamiḻmoḻiyē.
oru marattil, ciṟu kiḷikaṭku nēr iyipu iṉṟēṉum avaṟ ṟit tāṅkum peruṅkavikaṭku aṭimarattuṭaṉ nēr iyi piruppatupōla, ulaka moḻittokutikaṭkuḷ ciṟu pirivukaṭku nēr iyipiṉṟēṉum avaṟṟiṟku mūlamāṉa perum pirivukaṭku nēriyipu tiṭṭamāyuḷḷatām. ellāk kiḷikaṭkum mūlamāṉa ōr aṭimaramiruppatupōla ellā moḻikaṭkum mūlamāṉa oru tāymoḻiyu miruttal vēṇṭum. ulakattiluḷḷa makkaḷel lām oru tāy vaḻiyiṉarātaliṉ avar vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷum orutāy vayiṟṟu vaḻiyilātal vēṇṭum.
ulakattiṉ makkaḷ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiṉaviṭam ki.mu.2000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ intumakā camuttirattil amiḻntupōṉatum taliccaṅkamiruntatumāṉa kumarināṭē. āttirēliyā, teṉṉin tiyā, teṉṉāppirikkā eṉṟa mūṉṟum ātiyil nilattālicin tiruntaṉa veṉṟu mōṉāṭṭup paṇṭitarkaḷ kaṇṭa vuṇmiyē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām. ākavē, makkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ pēciya moḻi tamiḻēyātal vēṇṭum.
ellā moḻikaḷum iyaṟki ceyaṟkiyeṉa irupālā yaṭaṅkum. ivaṟṟuḷ tamiḻ oṉṟumē iyaṟki. ēṉiyavel lām ceyaṟki. ātalāṉ, tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻi (Primitive Language) eṉṟum, ēṉiya tirimoḻi (Derivative Languages) eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ellā moḻikaṭkumuḷḷa perumpāṟ coṟkaḷip pakutip poruḷuṭaṉ iyaṟki vaṭivil vaḻaṅkuvatu tamiḻē yeṉṟu moḻinūlāl (Philology) viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ āṅkilam (English), kirēkku (Greek), ilattīṉ (Latin) eṉṟa mum moḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum teṉcoṟkaḷ āyirakkaṇak kiṉa veṉpati ainnūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭavaṟṟāl veḷḷiṭi malipōl teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭutum:

moḻiyārāycciyum moḻiyakaḻvārāycciyum oṉṟē
moḻi yeṉpatu, oru makkaḷiṉ pēcum pēccilum iyaṟṟum ilakkiyattilum āḷum coṟkaḷiṉ muḻumiyāṉa coṟ ṟokuti, coṟkaḷaṉṟi, moḻiyeṉṟu taṉi vēṟāka oṉṟu milli. ātalāl collārāycciyē moḻiyārāyccikku aṭippaṭi. collārāycci oru allatu pala coṟkaḷiṉ varalāṟupaṟṟi yirukkalām. aṅṅaṉamē, moḻiyārāycciyum oru allatu pala moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟupaṟṟi yirukkalām. moḻiyeṉpatu, colliyum oru makkaḷiṉattiṉ coṟ ṟokutiyiyum kuṟittalāl, moḻiyārāycci eṉpatu collā rāycciyik kuṟittaṟkum iṭamuṇṭu.
moḻikaḷuḷ, intiyum maliyāḷamum pōṉṟu putiyavum uḷa: kirēkkamum ilattīṉamum pōṉṟu mutiyavum uḷa. tamiḻō, mutiyavaṟṟuḷḷum mutiya mutaṉmoḻi. putumoḻikaḷum mutumoḻikaḷiṉ tiripākavē tōṉṟiyiruttalāl, collā rāycciyip poṟuttamaṭṭil, iruvaki moḻiyum oṉṟē.
āytal eṉpatu, oru poruḷi nuṇuki nōkki ataṉ uṇmit taṉmiyi uṇartal.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam  (tol. uri. 32)
ārtal niramputal. āra āytal ārāytal.
akaḻtal eṉpatu tōṇṭutal. mēṉmaṭṭattil tōṉṟātu putintu allatu maṟintu kiṭappatik kalliyeṭuttal tōṇṭutal. atupōṉṟu āḻntu ārāytalum tōṇṭutal.
o.nō: tōṇṭu - tēṇṭu - tēṭu - tēṭṭam.
nōṇṭu - nōṭu - nōṭṭam, nōṭu - nāṭu - nāṭṭam.
nōṇṭu - nēṇṭu - nēṭu - nēṭṭam.
potuvāka, coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷit teḷivākak kāṭṭakkūṭiya moḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē. ataṉāl.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (peya.1)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam terivikkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, tamiḻilum, ellāc collum kaṇṭamaṭṭil allatu kēṭṭamaṭṭil vērpporu ḷuṇarttuvaṉa alla. pala coṟkaḷi āḻn takaḻntārāyntē, avaṟṟiṉ vērccolliyum mūlapporu ḷiyum aṟital kūṭum. ataṉāl
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (uri. 96)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamē kūṟukiṉṟatu.
coṟkaḷ, oru poruṭcol eṉṟum, palporuṭcol eṉṟum, poruḷuṇarttum vakiyil iruvakip paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. palporuṭ collum, oru vērccol eṉṟum, palvērc col eṉṟum, irutiṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ātalāl, vaṭi voppumi yoṉṟē nōkkip palvērc colli oru vērc col leṉṟu mayaṅkutal tavaṟām.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, veḷ eṉṉum iyaṟcolli eṭuttuk koḷḷalām. itu veppam, veṇṇiṟam, veṟumi, viruppam mutaliya palporuḷuṇarttuvatu. avaṟṟuḷ, veppamum virup pamum, namnāṭṭut taṭpaveppa niliyum ataṉ viḷivāka eḻum makkaṭ karuttum nōkkiṉ, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muraṇpaṭ ṭaṉavākum.
namnāṭu potuvāka veppanāṭāyiruppiṉum, kōṭik kālat tiṟ kuḷirnta kuṭippiyum mārikkālattil veppak kuṭippi yum virumpukiṉṟōm. cama taṭpaveppa nāṭukaḷilum miku taṭpa nāṭukaḷilum eṉṟum kuḷirntiruppatāl, an nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷ veppatti virumpuvatu iyalpē. ataṉāl, tiyāka rācap pāṭakar pāṭṭic cevikuḷirak kēṭṭu iṉpuṟṟēṉ eṉṟu namar colvatu pōṉṟu, amerikka nāṭṭut talivarkkuc cerumaṉiyil vetuvetuppāṉa (warm) varavēṟpiruntatu eṉṟu kuḷirnāṭṭār kūṟak kēṭkiṉṟōm.
tamiḻil vemmi eṉṉum colliṟku veppam, viruppam eṉṉum iruporuḷum uḷḷaṉa.
urumamum karumamum uruppamum veppamum
aḻaṉamum kōramum aḻalum vemmi  (7:105)
eṉpatu piṅkalam.
vemmi vēṇṭal  (tol. uri. 36)
veḷ - veṭṭi = 1. veppam. aṉal veṭṭiyāṟ curuṇṭu (irāmanā. yut. 14) 2. nilakkoti (W). 3. kāmaccūṭu. kāma veṭṭiyilē matimayaṅki  (taṉippā.) veṭṭiccūṭu eṉpatu peruvaḻakku.
te. veṭṭa, ka. veṭṭe.
veḷ eṉṉum iyaṟcollaṭip piṟanta veyya, veyyōṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷ veppattip pōṉṟē viruppattiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr oru kālattil kuḷirnāṭṭilum vāḻntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu col lārāycci yillār muṭivukoḷḷa nērkiṉṟatu. kaṭal koṇṭa kumarināṭṭiṉ teṉkōṭi veppa maṇṭalattiṉ teṉṉellik kuṭpaṭṭatē.
tamiḻccoṟkaḷ perumpālum ukarac cuṭṭaṭiyiṟ piṟan tuḷḷaṉa. u- ul eṉṉum mūla aṭi, kul, cul, tul, nul, pul, mul eṉṉum āṟu vaḻiyaṭikaḷākap pirintum, tirintum, pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷip piṟappittuḷḷaṉa. vakaram ukaramutal koḷḷāmiyāl, vukara aṭiyākap piṟanta col oṉṟumilli. ātalāl, olikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷoḻinta vakara meymmutaṟ coṟkaḷellām pakara mutalaṭiyiṉiṉṟō makara mutalaṭiyiṉiṉṟōtāṉ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
veḷ eṉṉum iyaṟcol, veppap poruḷil meḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟum, viruppap poruḷil peḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟum tirintuḷḷatu.
muḷ - muḷi. muḷital = erital. muḷi - miḷi - miḷir. miḷirtal = erital. viḷaṅkutal. miḷ - miṉ = oḷi. oḷi neruppiṉ iyal. muḷ - miḷ - meḷ - veḷ - vey - veyya = veppamāṉa. 
veyyōṉ = veppamāṉavaṉ, katiravaṉ, koṭiyōṉ.
ḷakaramey yakaramākat tirital perumpāṉmi.

tiricoṟkaḷil yakaramey tokuvatu iyalpē.
