iṅṅaṉam, coṟkaḷiṉ mūlatti āḻntāyntutāṉ kāṇa voṇṇum,
tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr kumarināṭṭil naṇṇilakkōṭṭi aṭuttē vāḻntavar. nāḷ muḻutum veyilil uḻippavar karuttum, niḻalil vāḻpavar veḷuttum, iruppar. veṇ kaḷamar X karuṅkaḷamar, veḷḷāḷar X kārāḷar eṉṉum peyariṇikaḷi nōkkuka. uḻutuṇpavar veyililum, uḻuvittuṇpōr niḻalilum iṉṟum vāḻtal kāṇka.
iṉi, uṇavuc ciṟappiṉālum uṇṇā vaṟumiyālum veḷuttum karuttum pōvatum uṇṭu. veḷḷokkal X kārokkal eṉṉum peyariṇiyi nōkkuka.
tamiḻar veppamikka kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟē mēṉāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟamiyi,  V.R. irāmaccantira tīṭcitariṉ origin and Spread of the Tamils eṉṉum maṟukkoṇā ariya ārāycci varalāṟṟu nūli nōkkik kāṇka.
māntaṉ mutaṟpeṟṟōr naṇṇilakkōṭṭi yaṭutta nāṭṭilēyē vāḻntiruttal kūṭum.
mēlināṭṭu moḻiyārāycciyāḷar eṉṉeṉṉa uṇmi kāṇpiṉum, aviyellām kumarināṭṭu māntaṉ tōṟṟattiṟ kō tamiḻaṉ tōṟṟattiṟkō muraṇāka iruttal muṭiyāteṉ patiyum, moḻiyārāycciyum moḻiyakaḻvārāycciyum oṉṟēyeṉpatiyum, itaṉāl tiṭṭavaṭṭamāyt terintu koḷka.  (centamiḻc celvi akutōvar 1980)
moḻiyiṉa vuṇarvum paṭimuṟik kalappum
intiyāvil, ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭil, iṉṟuḷḷa piṟavik kulaṅkaḷ piṟanāṭukaḷil illāviṭiṉum, celvanili, paṭṭam, toḻil, matam, kaṭci, paḻaṅkuṭi eṉpaṉa paṟṟiya potuvaki yāṉa vakuppukkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. iruppiṉum, aviyellām nāṭṭumoḻi vakiyil ōriṉamāy oṉṟupaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru nāṭṭiṟ paṉmoḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ taṉittaṉip peruntoki makkaḷik koṇṭiruppiṉ, ap paṉmoḻikaḷum aṅku nāṭṭu moḻikaḷākac camaniliyaṭikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭilō, āriya vaḻiyiṉarukkum tamiḻē tāymoḻiyāyiruntum, avaratu iraṇṭakattālum avarkkaṭimippaṭṭa araciyaṟ kaṭciyāḷariṉ pētimiyālum, vēṟṟu moḻikaḷē pōṟṟappaṭṭu nāṭṭu moḻi tūṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ik kēṭṭunili evvakiyilum uṭaṉē māṟṟappaṭal vēṇṭum. allākkāl, tamiḻ vāḻavum tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟavum eḷḷaḷavum iṭamilli.
kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭākat tamiḻa ruḷḷattil ūṭṭappaṭṭa āriya nañcit tiṭumeṉṟu veḷiyēṟṟa muṭiyātātaliṉ, paṭippaṭiyākattāṉ tamiḻar kumukāyam tirunti varal vēṇṭum.
mutaṟkaṇ, orē kulattiluḷḷa akamaṇap pirivukaḷellām puṟamaṇap pirivukaḷākalām.
atiyaṭuttu, teṟkum vaṭakkum vevvēṟu peyar koṇṭuḷḷa orē kulattār orē peyar koḷḷalām. e- ṭu: nāṭārum kirāmaṇiyārum, kikkōḷarum ceṅkuntarum.
ataṉpiṉ, vēḷāṇ kuṭiyiṉiṉṟē paṭittalimip patavi paṟṟip pirinta mutaliyār kulattiṉar, piḷḷip paṭṭamuḷḷa marakkaṟi veḷḷāḷaruṭaṉ maṇavuṟavu koḷḷalām.
ataṉpiṉ, mukkulattār (kaḷḷar, maṟavar, akampaṭiyar) orē kulattārākalām.
ataṉpiṉ, uṇṭāṭṭiṟ kalantukoḷḷuṅ kulaṅkaḷ maṇa vuṟavilum kalakkalām.
ataṉpiṉ, caṟṟē ēṟṟat tāḻvuḷḷa kulaṅkaḷ, muṉpu uṇṭāṭṭiṟ kalantu piṉpu koḷvaṉi koṭuppaṉiyuñ ceytu koḷḷa lām. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyumpōtu, oru kulattāriṉ uṇavumuṟi iṉṉoru kulattārkku ēṟkāviṭiṉ, ati māṟṟiviṭa vēṇṭum.
iṟutiyil, kalviyilum nākarikattilum tuppuravilum uyarntu, otta pataviyil allatu nilimiyiluḷḷa tāḻttappaṭṭa vakuppuk kuṭumpaṅkaḷuṭaṉ, uyarnta vakuppārum iruvaki yilum uṟavāṭalām.
tāḻttappaṭṭōriṉ uyarviṟku ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkak kalvi iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ataṉāltāṉ, taṉ māṉavuṇarcciyum tuppuravum uṇṭākum. tuppuravum nākarikamum iṉṟēl tīṇṭāmi oḻiyātu. tīṇṭāmi oḻinta piṉṉum, calakkup puri vārutalum muṭi tiruttutalum pōṉṟa toḻil ceyvār vīṭṭiluṇṭal ellārkkum ēṟkātu.
tamiḻar tamiḻaruṭaṉ uṟavu kalantapiṉ, teluṅkar kaṉṉaṭar mutaliya tiraviṭaroṭum uṟavu kalakkalām. ataṉpiṉ, tamiḻp paṟṟuḷḷa inti makkaḷoṭum maṇattoṭarpu koḷḷalām.
makkaḷ tāmākat tiruntāviṭiṉ, etirkālattil uṇmiyāṉa potuvuṭimiyāṭci vantu virintu tirutti viṭum. iṉṟu potuvuṭimiyeṉṟu ulakil vaḻaṅkuvatu kūṭṭuṭimiyiṉ (Socialism) muṉinta vakiyē yaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
mōtiram vittal viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭuvārellārum irukaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, kaṭcik koruvarāka iruvaroḻiya ēṉiyarellām, kaṭci vāriyāy iru variciyāka etiretir uṭkārntu koḷvar. uṭkārāta iruvarum tattam kaṭci variciyiṉ piṉṉāl niṉṟu koṇ ṭiruppar. avaruḷ oruvar oru mōtirattik kiyil vittuk koṇṭu kuṉintu, tam variciyil ovvoruvar piṉṉālum ati vippatāka naṭittu, yārēṉum oruvar piṉṉāl vittuviṭṭu varici neṭukalum ceṉṟapiṉ nimirntu niṟpar. etir varicikkup piṉṉāl niṟpavar, mōtiram vikkappaṭṭa iṭatti iṉṉārukkup piṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭikkūṟa vēṇṭum. cariyāyc colliviṭiṉ, aṭuttamuṟi etir variciyāḷ mōtiram vittal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ muṉvittavarē vittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu āṭappeṟum. kīḻē yiruppavar āṭṭu neṭukalum uṭkārntu koṇṭē yiruppār.  (ta.nā.vi.)
mōriyap paṭiyeṭuppu
(ki.mu. 3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
tamiḻakattiṉ mēl mutaṉmutalākap paṭiyeṭukkat tuṇinta vaṭintiya (makatanāṭṭu mōriya) aracaṉ pintucāraṉ (ki.mu. 301 -273), oru perumpaṭiyit teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kaṉuppiṉāṉ. appaṭi kōcar eṉṉum oruvakip porunarit tuṇik koṇṭu, teṉkaṉṉaṭam eṉṉum koṇkāṉattiṉ kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyāṉa tuḷunāṭṭiṟ pukuntu, ati yāṇṭukoṇṭirunta naṉṉaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ maṉṉaṉi nāṭṭiviṭṭu ōṭṭi viṭṭatu. itaṉi,
aḻiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉṉaṉ
naṟumā koṉṟu nāṭṭiṟ pōkkiya
oṉṟu moḻik kōcar pōla
eṉṉum kuṟuntokip pāṭṭāl (73) aṟiyalām.
piṉṉar, kōcar teṉkiḻakkāka vantu koṅkunāṭṭup paḻiyaṉ mōkūrit tākkiṉar. avaṉ avarip puṟaṅkaṇṭu turattiṉāṉ. ataṉpiṉ kōcarukkut tuṇiyāka makatattiṉiṉṟu oru putuppaṭi vantatu. atu varumpōtu, atic cērnta tērkaḷum carakku vaṇṭikaḷum varuvataṟkut taṭiyāyirunta pāṟikaḷi yellān takarttu, pātiyic cevvip paṭuttikkoṇṭu vantatu. atu,
tuṉikā laṉṉa puṉitērk kōcar
toṉmū tālat tarumpaṇip potiyil
iṉṉici muracaṅ kaṭippikut tiraṅkat 
temmuṉi cititta ñāṉṟi mōkūr
paṇiyā miyiṟ pakitali vanta
mākeḻu tāṉi vampa mōriyar

ilaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi yaṟivāy
eṉṉum akappāṭṭup pakutiyāl (251) aṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi, appaṭi vaṭukariyum tuṇikkoṇṭu vanta teṉpatu,
muraṇmiku vaṭukar muṉṉuṟa mōriyar
teṉṟici mātiram muṉṉiya varaviṟku
viṇṇuṟa vōṅkiya paṉiyiruṅ kuṉṟat
toṇkatirt tikiri yuruḷiya kuṟitta
aṟi yiṟantu  (akam. 281)
eṉṉum māmūlar kūṟṟāl teriya varukiṉṟatu.
ākavē, vaṭukar kōcar mōriyar eṉṉum mūviṉa maṟavarik koṇṭatu appaṭi eṉpati aṟiyalām. vaṭukariyum kōcariyum puṟaṅkāṇiṉ mōriyar tāmē puṟaṅkāṭṭuvar eṉpati aṟinta cōḻaṉ iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi, taṉ nāṭṭik kākkum kaṭamiyi yuṇarntu, muṉpu vaḷaṅkeḻukōcar viḷaṅkupaṭi nūṟi (akam 205), piṉpu koṇkāṉañ ceṉṟu pāḻiyaraṇi yaḻittu orē yaṭiyāka mōriyarit tamiḻakatti ṉiṉṟu turattic ceruppāḻi yeṟinta eṉṉum virutaṭi moḻiyum peṟṟāṉ. itaṉi,
eḻāat tiṇitōṭ cōḻar perumakaṉ
viḷaṅkupukaḻ niṟutta iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi
kuṭikkaṭa ṉākaliṟ kuṟiviṉi muṭimār
cempuṟaḻ puricip pāḻi nūṟi
vampa vaṭukar pintali cavaṭṭi  (akam. 375)
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyalām. avaṉ vaṭavaṭukar vāḷōṭṭiya (puṟam. 378) ceyal caṟṟup pintiṉatā yirukkalām. vaṭa vaṭukar kaliṅkanāṭṭut teluṅkar.
kīrantiyiṉ vīṭṭuk katavit taṭṭi, ataṉāl vēṇṭātu taṉ kiyik kuṟittukkoṇṭa koṟkip pāṇṭiyaṉum, makaṉi muṟi ceyta maṉumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉum, ki.mu. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarā yiruntirukkalām.
maṉumuṟi eṉṟatu vaṭamoḻi maṉutaruma cāttira muṟiyi yaṉṟu. annūliṉ kālam ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. ataṟku muṉ tōṉṟiya āriyataruma nūlkaḷum, naṭuniliyiṉṟik kulattiṟ korumuṟi kūṟuvaṉavē. kaṉṟik koṉṟataṟkuk kaḻuvāy allatu taṇṭaṉi yeṉṉeṉṟu cōḻaṉ viṉaviyapōtu, poṟkaṉṟu ceytu pirāmaṇarkkuk koṭuttu, āniriyoṭu kāṭu ceṉṟu oru mātam puṉmēyntu varavēṇṭumeṉṟu, pirāmaṇar kūṟiṉar. ati avaṉ oppuk koḷḷātu, uyiruk kuyirē yīṭeṉṟu taṉ makaṉ mēl tērēṟṟik koṉṟāṉ. vaṭa nūlkaḷiṟ katiravaṉ kula aracaruḷ mutalvaṉākak kuṟikkap peṟum maṉu, tamiḻppeyar koṇṭa oru cōḻaṉā yiruntirut tal vēṇṭum. avaṉ kaṇṭa naṭunili muṟiyiyē, makaṉi muṟi ceyta cōḻaṉ ciṟappup peyar kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
tilliyiliruntu pāṇiṉīyattiṟku virivuri (pāṣyam) varinta patañcaliyār ki.mu. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarē. pēcu - va. - pāṣ - pāṣyam.
āriyam tamiḻakattil vērūṉṟi, nālvaraṇamum piṟappilamin tavi yeṉṟum, pirāmaṇaṉ nilattēvaṉ eṉṟum, tuṟavāl maṭṭum vīṭupēṟeṉṟum, tuṟavu pirāmaṇaṉukkē vuriya teṉ ṟum, pirāmaṇaṉ vēḷvi vaḷarppataṉālēyē ulakam naṭi peṟukiṉṟa teṉṟum, pirāmaṇaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyvataṉā lēyē ēṉi mūvaraṇattārum maṟumiyil naṉṉili yaṭiva reṉṟum, pala tīya koḷkikaḷtamiḻaruḷḷattiṟ patikkappaṭṭu varuvatu kaṇṭa tiruvaḷḷuvar, tamiḻariṉ kaṇ tiṟakkavum avari muṉṉēṟṟavum tam tirukkuṟaḷi iyaṟṟiya ruḷiṉatu ki.mu. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. eṉ tirukkuṟaḷ tamiḻ marapuriyip pārkka.
pirāmaṇarkku muṟṟum aṭimiyākit tamiḻakattip pāḻākkiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ taliciṟanta palvēḷvic cāli (yākacāli) mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti, ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉāyirun tirukkalām.
iṟiñcuka perumaniṉ ceṉṉi ciṟanta
nāṉmaṟi muṉiva rēntuki yetirē  (puṟam.6)
eṉṟu, vētamōtiya pirāmaṇari yellām muṉivar eṉṟu kūṟi, avarukkumuṉ taṉ kuṭumiyaviḻntu viḻumāṟu tali kuṉintu vaṇaṅkumpaṭi, mutukuṭumip pāṇṭiyaṉi vēṇṭiya kārikiḻār eṉṉum tamiḻat tamiḻp pulavaṉ nilimiyi nōkkumpōtu, ettuṇi yuṇavuttaṭ ṭiruppiṉum ikkālamē naṟkālam eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. iṉi,
palcāli mutukuṭumiyiṉ
nalvēḷvit tuṟipōkiya
tollāṇi nallāciriyar
puṇarkūṭ ṭuṇṭa pukaḻcāl ciṟappiṉ
nilantaru tiruviṉ neṭiyōṉ pōla  (maturik. 759 - 763)
eṉṉum aṭikaḷ, tolkāppiyam araṅkēṟiya kālattu nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉum, nāṉmaṟi muṟṟiya ataṅ kōṭṭācāṉik koṇṭu palvēḷvi ceytavaṉ tāṉō eṉṟu aiyuṟac ceykiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉāṉa karikāl vaḷavaṉ,
irunila maruṅkiṟ porunarip peṟāac
ceruveṅ kātaliṟ ṟirumā vaḷavaṉ
vāḷuṅ kuṭiyu mayirkkaṇ muracum
nāḷoṭu peyarttu naṇṇārp peṟukavim
maṇṇaka maruṅkiṉeṉ valikeḻu tōḷeṉap 
puṇṇiya ticimukam pōkiya vannāḷ
acivi lūkkattu nacipiṟak koḻiyap 
pakivilak kiyatip payaṅkeḻu maliyeṉa 
imiyava ruṟiyuñ cimiyap piṭarttalik
koṭuvari yoṟṟik koḷkiyiṟ peyarvōṟku
mānīr vēli vaccira naṉṉāṭṭuk 
kōṉiṟi koṭutta koṟṟap pantarum
makatanaṉ ṉāṭṭu vāḷvāy vēntaṉ
pakippuṟattuk koṭutta paṭṭimaṇ ṭapamum
avanti vēnta ṉuvantaṉaṉ koṭutta
nivantōṅku marapiṉ tōraṇa vāyilum
poṉṉiṉum maṇiyiṉum puṉintaṉa vāyiṉum
nuṇviṉik kammiyar kāṇā marapiṉa
tuyarnīṅku ciṟappiṉavar tollō rutavikku
mayaṉvitittuk koṭutta marapiṉa vivitām
oruṅkuṭaṉ puṇarntāṅ kuyarntō rēttum
arumpeṟaṉ marapiṉ maṇṭapam  (cilap. 5: 89-110)
niṟuvi,
kāṭukoṉṟu nāṭākki
kuḷantoṭṭu vaḷamperukki
....................................................................................
kōyiloṭu kuṭiniṟīi.  (paṭṭiṉap. 283 -6)
kāvirikkuk karikaṭṭi vēli nilam āyiraṅ kalam nel viḷiyac ceytu,
nīriṉ vanta nimirparip puraviyum
kāliṉ vanta karuṅkaṟi mūṭiyum
vaṭamalip piṟanta maṇiyum poṉṉum
kuṭamalip piṟanta āramum akilum
teṉkaṭal muttuṅ kuṇakaṭal tukirum
kaṅki vāriyum kāvirip payaṉum
īḻat tuṇavuṅ kāḻakat tākkamum
ariyavum periyavum neriya vīṇṭi  (paṭṭiṉap. 185 - 192)
paṭṭiṉum mayiriṉum parutti nūliṉum
kaṭṭu nuṇviṉik kāruka māntarum,  (cilap. 5: 16-7)
kañca kārarum cempucey kunarum
maraṅkol taccarum karuṅkik kollarum
kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñarum maṇṇīṭ ṭāḷarum
poṉcey kollarum naṉkalan tarunarum
tuṉṉa kārarum tōliṉ tuṉṉarum
kiḻiyiṉum kiṭiyiṉum toḻilpala perukkip
paḻutil ceyviṉip pālkeḻu mākkaḷum  (cilap. 5:29-34)
vēṟu pala toḻilāḷarum taḻittōṅkac ceytāṉ. āyiṉ, avaṉum āriyac cāykaṭiyil vīḻntu,
aṟamaṟak kaṇṭa neṟimā ṇaviyattu
muṟinaṟ kaṟiyunar muṉṉuṟap pukaḻnta
tūviyaṟ koḷkit tukaḷaṟu makaḷiroṭu
paruti yuruviṟ palpaṭip purici
yeruvi nukarcci yūpa neṭuntūṇ
vēta vēḷvit toḻiṉmuṭit tatu  (puṟam. 224)
mikamika varuntat takkatē.
ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa, āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉum, kaṇṇakikkup paṭimi camitta cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉum, tamiḻiṉattiṉ perumiyik kāttataṉāṟ pārāṭṭat takkavarē.
āriyap paṭi eṉṟatu āntirap pēraraciṉ (ki.mu. 567 - ki.pi.220) vaṭukap paṭiyi. vaṭanāṭṭil allatu vaṭakkil uḷḷavari yellām āriyareṉṉum vaḻakku iṭikkālattil eḻuntu viṭṭatu. āriyak kūttāṭi ṉālum kāriyattil kaṇṇā yiru. eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyil, āriyak kūtteṉṟatu vaṭukariṉ kaḻikkūttiyē.
putiya leṭuttava ṉeṉṟu ciṟiyiliṭappaṭṭa vārttikaṉi viṭutali ceytapiṉ, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ
nīrttaṉ ṟituveṉa neṭumoḻi kūṟi
aṟiyā mākkaḷiṉ muṟinili tirintaveṉ
iṟimuṟi piḻittatu poṟuttalnum kaṭaṉeṉa
maṉṉippuk kēṭṭatuṭaṉ,
taṭampuṉaṟ kaḻaṉit taṅkāl taṉṉuṭaṉ
maṭaṅkā viḷiyuḷ vayalūr nalkiyatē
aḷaviṟku miñciṉatām. ataṟkum mēl, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ vārttikaṉ kālil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkiyatu tamiḻiṉattiṟkē aḻiyāp pēriḻivām. itaṉāl aṟṟip pirāmaṇar koṭṭamum tamiḻar aṭimittaṉamum teḷivām.
kārttiki kaṇavaṉ vārttikaṉ muṉṉar
irunila maṭantikkut tirumārpu nalki
eṉṟu (cilap. 23:120 -1) iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ paṭimāṟṟaṇiyāl maṟittuk kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
kōpperuntēviyiṉ ūṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭa maṉakkalakka niliyil, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āyntupārātu kōvalaṉik koṉṟatu kaṭuṅkuṟṟa māyiṉum,
poṉcey kollaṉ ṟaṉcoṟ kēṭṭa
yāṉō varacaṉ yāṉē kaḷvaṉ
maṉpati kākkun teṉpulaṅ kāval
eṉmutaṟ piḻittatu keṭukaveṉ ṉāyuḷeṉa
maṉṉavaṉ mayaṅkivīḻn taṉaṉē teṉṉavaṉ
kōpperun tēvi kulintaṉaḷ naṭuṅkik
kaṇavaṉi yiḻantōrkkuk kāṭṭuva tilleṉ
ṟiṇiyaṭi toḻutuvīḻn taṉaḷē  (cilap. 20:75 -81)
eṉṉuñ ceyti, vaḷintakōli uṭaṉē nimirttiviṭṭatu.
itaṉāṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ ceṅkōṉmiyum veḷiyāyiṟṟu.
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, tamiḻaracari yikaḻnta kaṉaka vicayarmēl, paṉimaliyileṭutta pattiṉitteyvap paṭimik kalli ēṟṟik koṇarntatu, eṉṟum tamiḻarkkup perumi taruvatē. āyiṉum, akkallik kaṅkik kariyil nīrāṭṭiya pōtu, māṭalaṉ eṉṉum pirāmaṇaṉukkut tuliniṟit tāṉamāka 50 tulām poṉ koṭuttatum, koṭuṅkōḷūril avaṉ coṉṉavuṭaṉ vēḷvi ceytatum, avaṉatu āriya aṭimit taṉattit teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pattiṉip paṭimikkuk kalleṭukka vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟapōtu pēriyāṟṟaṅ kariyiliruntu orēnāḷil taṉ talinakarkkut tirumpiya taṉālum, nīlamaliyil taṅkiṉataṉālum, tirumpi vantakkāl neytal nila makaḷir varavēṟṟup pāṭiya taṉālum, avaṉ kālattil koṭuṅkōḷūrē talinakarāka iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. tamiḻakam maṭṭumaṉṟi intiyā muḻutum avaṉ atikārattiṟ kuṭpaṭṭiruntataṉāl, avaṉ virumpiyiruppiṉ koṅku nāṭṭuk karuvūri mīṇṭum tali nakarākak koṇṭiruttalkūṭum. āyiṉ, avaṉ muṉṉōrē ati viṭṭuviṭṭataṉālum, nāṭṭi vaḷampaṭuttum nīr vaṇikattiṟkup pūmpukār pōl oru tuṟinakarē ēṟṟatā yiruntataṉālum koṭuṅkōḷūri lēyē nilittu viṭṭāṉ. tirumāl kōvilākiya āṭakamāṭam, karuvūriṟ pōṉṟu koṭuṅkōḷūrilum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. ak kālattiṟ poṉṉiṟkup pañcamilli. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattiṟ koṅku nāṭṭup pakutiyi yāṇṭa cērar kuṭiyiṉar, takaṭūr atikamāṉ caravaṭiyiṉarē.
neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āriyappaṭiyi veṉṟiruntataṉālum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ paṭi kaṅkiyik kaṭattaṟku nūṟṟuvar kaṉṉar (cātakarṇi? cātavākaṉar?) utaviyataṉālum, ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil āntirap pēraraciṟkut tamiḻnāṭu uṭpaṭṭirunta teṉṟu koḷḷa iṭamilli.

vakkā
vakkā-paka
vakkā=kokkuvaki.
vakkāvu nāriyuṅ kokkum paṭukkavē.  (kuṟṟā. kuṟa. 93 : 2).
vakkā-vaṅkā=paṟavi vaki.
vaṅkāk kaṭanta ceṅkāṟ pēṭi (kuṟun. 151).  (va.va. 245)
vaṅku-vak, vaṅk
vaḷ-vaṇ-vaṇam-vaṇar-vaḷivu, yāḻkkōṭṭiṉ vaḷinta pakuti.
vaḷar-vaṇari=vaḷitaṭi.
vaṇam-vaṇaṅku-vāṅku-vaṅku-vaṅki=neḷi  vaḷiyal, vaḷinta katti.  vaṇaṅkutal - vaḷital.
vaṇakkam = vaḷivu.
vilvaṇakkam  (kuṟaḷ. 827)
vāṅku = vaḷivu.  vāṅkutali=vaḷital.
vāṅkukatir varakiṉ  (mullip 98).
vāṅku-vāṅkā=vaḷinta  ūtukaruvi. vaṅkam=vaḷivu, āṟṟuvaḷivu.  (va.va. 245)
vañcakam-vañcaka
vañcaṉam-vaṇcaṉa
iru moḻiyilum vaṅku eṉpatē mūlam. āyiṉ, vaṭavar vakku (vak) eṉṉum valittal vaṭiviṟ kāṭṭuvar. itiṉiṉṟē vakra, vakrīkaraṇa mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
oruvaṉi ēmāṟṟuvatu vaṭṭañ cuṟṟi avaṉi vaḷivatu pōliruttalāl, vaḷitaṟkaruttil ēmāṟṟuk karuttup piṟantatu.
circumvent eṉṉum āṅkilac colliyum nōkkuka.  (va.va. 246)
vañci
vañci-vañc
vaṅku-vañcu-vañci. o.nō: aṅku-añcu.  poṅku-poñcu.
vañci-vañcakam, vañcakam, vañcaṉam, vañcaṉi.
vañcu=vañcakam. "vañcē vallarē" (tēvā. 828:3)  (va.va.245)
vañci :  vaṅkutal vaḷital, vaṅku-vañcu-vañci. o.nō: aṅka (vaḷi)-añcu, poṅku-poñcu.
oruvaṉi vañcippatu avaṉ tappamuṭiyāvāṟu vaṭṭañcuṟṟi vaḷivatu pōliruttalāl, vaḷitaṟ karuttil vañcittaṟ karuttup piṟantatu. Circumvent eṉṉum āṅkilac colli nōkkuka.
vañci-vañcam, vañcakam, vañcaṉam, vañcaṉi.
vañci : vañc(va.), vañcakam-- vañcaka (va.), vañcaṉam-- vañcaṉa (va.).  (ti.ma : 753.)

vaṭṭam
vaṭṭam-vrutta (i.vē.).
val-vaḷ-vaṭṭu-vaṭṭam=1. vaḷivu.
villi vaṭṭappaṭa vāṅki  (tēvā. 5: 9).
2. vaḷitaṭi.
pukariṉar cūḻ vaṭṭattavi  (paripā. 15 : 61)
3. vaṭṭavaṭivam.  4. kativaṉi allatu tiṅkaḷic cuṟṟiya kōṭṭi.
5. vaṇṭiccakkaram.  6. tiriki,  7. tōṭkaṭakam (piṅ.).

iccoṟku iṉṉum palaporuḷkaḷ uḷa. mutaṉmi yāṉaviyē iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa.
vaḷ-vaḷku-vaṭku.  vaṭṭutal=vaḷital, vaṇaṅkutal.

vaṭacol teṉcol kāṇumvaḻikaḷ
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutivari vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām perumpālum vaṭacollē eṉṉum tava ṟāṉa karuttu, tamiḻaruḷḷattilum nilittiruntatu. vaṭamoḻi yilum  teṉcoṟkaḷuṇmi kaṇṭu av vuṇmiyi mutaṉ mutal veḷippaṭuttiyavar paṇṭārakar (Dr.) kuṇṭarṭṭu, kālṭu vel, pōppu eṉṉum mēliyaṟiñarāvar. ivaruḷ iṭiñar, taliciṟantavar. avarum, teṉ colli vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi, vaṭacollit teṉcolli ṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷik kūṟiṉārallar. ēṉeṉiṉ atu avaratu tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṟkut tēviyāṉataṉṟu. āyiṉum, avar teṉ coṟkuk kūṟiya viti kaḷit tuṇikkoṇṭē vaṭacoṟkum cilavaṟṟi vakuttuk koḷḷalām. avi varumāṟu.
vaṭa coṟkaḷ :
1.	mūlavaṭivil āriyaveḻuttum āriya meymmayakkamum amintiruppaṉa.
2.	paṇṭit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟakālat tamiḻil vaṭamoḻi yiṉiṉṟu vantu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
3.	tamiḻil vēriṉṟi āriyattilēyē vēr koṇṭiruppaṉa.
4.	tamiḻum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta tiraviṭamumāṉa ellā moḻi kaḷilum vaḻaṅkātu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟilmaṭṭum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
5.	mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
6.	āriyattiṟ ciṟappup poruḷ allatu ciṟappu viṉit toṭarpu koṇṭiruppaṉa.
cila teṉ coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peruvāriyāy vaḻaṅkuvatāl perumpāṉmi vaḻakku vaṭacoṉmikkuc cāṉṟākāteṉa vaṟika.
mēṟkūṟiya vitikaḷ allatu nilippāṭukaḷ oṉṟiṟku mēṟ poruntiruppiṉ, avaṟṟilē mikutikkut takkavāṟu vaṭa coṉmi tēṟṟamām. āyiṉum, avi kaṇita muṟippaṭṭa tulliya vitikaḷalla. ātalāl, paranta moḻinūṟ kalviyum neṭunāṭ collārāycciyum ciṟnata naṭuniliyumilavēl avi peritum payaṉpaṭā. uṇmiyāṉa teṉcollit teṉcolleṉṟu kūṟuvatum naṭuviṉmiyil etirikaḷ kūṟa lām. āyiṉ āḻnta ārāycciyum tūya  maṉaccāṉṟum vali yuṟuttiṉ evarukkum eḷḷaḷavum añcavēṇṭuvatiṉṟu. cilar aṟivārāycci valimiyiṉṟit tam paṭṭam patavic ciṟappāl oru muṭipiṟku varukiṉṟaṉar. atu mikat tavaṟām.
paṇṭit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālat tamiḻil iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷuḷ `pacu' eṉpatu oṉṟākum. itu vaṭacolleṉpatu veḷippaṭiyāyiṉum, orucār tamiḻp pērāciriyar itit teṉcolleṉpatāl, māṇavarum ārāycci yillātārum mayaṅkuvataṟku, iṭaṉāvatuṭaṉ, avaravar virumpiyavāṟē pala vaṭa col teṉcoleṉavum pala teṉ col vaṭacoleṉavum paṭṭu, uṇmiyāṉa ārāycci valiyaṟṟup pōyviṭukiṉṟatu. pala aṭippaṭit teṉcolli vaṭa colleṉṟu kūṟum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṟṟum valiyuṟṟuviṭukiṉṟatu.
pacuvik kuṟikkum tūyateṉcoṟkaḷ, ā (āṉ), kaṟavi. kuṭañcuṭṭu, kurāl, kō, curi, peṟṟam (irupāṟpotu) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, kaṟavi eṉpatu pāl kaṟakkappaṭuvatu. curi eṉpatu maṭi peruttatu; kuṭañcuṭṭu eṉpatu oru kuṭam pāl kaṟappatu: kurāl eṉpatu pukār (kapila) niṟantatu. peṟṟam eṉpatu mikap paruttuyarntatu: ā, kō eṉpaṉa potuppeyar. ivaṟ ṟuḷ ā eṉpatu tamiḻilum tiraviṭattilum maṭṭum vaḻaṅkum: kō eṉpatu āriyattilum vaḻaṅkum.
E.cow, A.Sax, cu, G. kub, D. and Dan. koe, Ice. ku, Sc. kye, skt. go, gus.
kōvi mēyppavaṉ allatu vaḷarppavaṉ kōvaṉ, kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kōṉāṉ. o.nō : āyaṉ - āvi vaḷarkkum iṭiyaṉ.
pacu eṉṉum vaṭacol, pācattiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum vērp poruḷatu. pācam = kayiṟu. āriya vēḷviyiṟ kaṭṭap paṭum uyirikaḷ. māntaṉ, āṉ, kutiri, veḷḷāṭu, cemmaṟi yāṭu eṉṉum ainteṉṟu atarvavētam (xi, 2, 9 etc) kūṟuvatāka, māṉiyar villiyamcu tam vaṭamoḻiyāṅkila vakarātiyiṟ kāṭṭuvar. (pak-611).
pacu eṉpatu mutaṟkālattil, potuvāka, kaṭṭappaṭṭa oru vilaṅki maṭṭum taṉippaṭavum tokutiyākavum (irupāṟ potup peyarākak) kuṟittu. piṉpu vēḷvi yuyirip peyarāka vaḻaṅki, ataṉpiṉ āviṟkuc ciṟappāka vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu.
āriya vaḻakkit taḻuviyē. pacu eṉṉum col tamiḻilum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil vilaṅku. kāḷi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.
"pacuvēṟit tirivāṉōr pavaṉ" (tēvā. 760.4) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷiyiyum, "pacuk kaḷip pōlac cellum naṭiyāl" (patiṉo. paṭṭiṉattiruvēkam. 32) eṉṉumiṭattil vilaṅki yum, pacu eṉṉum col kuṟittal kāṇka. āṉ eṉṉum tūya peṇpāṟ col, tamiḻ marapiṟku māṟāka.
āṉmukattaṉ  aṭaṟkaṇnāyakaṉ.  (kantapu. pāṉukō. 95)
eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷiyik  kuṟippatum āriya vaḻakkit taḻuviyē.
pacu eṉpatu āvi maṭṭuṅ kuṟippatu vaḻakkamākum.
vēta āriyar tamiḻarōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu civaneṟik koṇ muṭipu (civa cittāntam) kaṟṟapiṉṉarē, pācam, pacu, pacupati eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, muṟiyē. taḷi (malam), pulampaṉ (āṉmā). iṟi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil aṇiyiyaṉ muṟiyil āḷap peṟṟaṉa.
pacu eṉṉum coṟku iṉamāka, ilattīṉil, peck (cattle) bos eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, kirēkkattila bous eṉṉum collum uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉiraṇṭiyum gus eṉṉum vaṭa collōṭiṇippar mēli moḻi nūlārum akarāti yāḷarum. itu attuṇip poruttamākat tōṉṟavilli. āyiṉum kakarattiṟkum pakarattiṟkum pōlit toṭar piruppatāl, ati muṟṟum maṟattaṟkilli.
Pecu eṉṉum iyaṟ colliṉiṉṟu tirintu paṇattik kuṟikkum Pecunia (money) eṉṉum ilattiṉ peyarc collum, Pecuniary eṉṉum āṅkilap peyareccamum, celvattik kuṟikkum māṭu eṉṉum colloṭu oppunōkkaṟ kuriyaṉa.
kēṭil viḻuccelvam kalvi yoruvaṟku
māṭalla maṟṟi yavi  (kuṟaḷ. 340)
kō (āṉ) eṉṉum teṉcol mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum, pecunia eṉṉum ilattīṉ col māṭu eṉṉum teṉcollottuc celvatti yuṇarttuvatum, pērāciriyar pi.ṭi. cīnivāca aiyaṅkārum, paṇṭārakar irāmac cantira tīṭcitarum kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻaruḷ oru kūṭṭattārē mēli nāṭu ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟi yuḷ ḷamiyi uṇarttum.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, pacu eṉpatu vaṭacollēyeṉṟum, atit teṉcolleṉpatāl poyyoṭu kalanta meyyum poyyākip pala tūya teṉ collum vaṭacollākat tōṉṟu meṉṟum, ātalāl ataṉāl tīmiyē yaṉṟi naṉmiyilli yeṉṟum, pacumpullit tiṉpatu pacuveṉpatu caṟṟum poruntā teṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.

vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkun tolkāppiyat teṉcoṟkaḷ
iṟṟi muḻuttamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ muṉmiyatu tolkāppiyamāta liṉ atil vantuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷ āci, viciyar pōṉṟa aintāṟē yākum. vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkum ataṉ piṟa coṟ kaḷellām tūya teṉcollē yātal veḷḷiṭimali. āyiṉum ārāycci yillārkku aṟivippāṉ vēṇṭi avaṟṟi muṟiyē ovvoṉṟā yeṭuttu moḻinūṟ paṭi viḷakkuvām.
paṭimi
palpukaḻ niṟutta paṭimi yōṉē.  (tol. pāyiram)
mēviya ciṟappiṉ ēṉōr yaṭimiya  (tol. akat. 30)
paṭimi eṉṉuñ col paṭi eṉṉum viṉiyaṭiyākap piṟantatu. paṭi eṉpatu paṭu eṉpataṉ maṟuvaṭivam. ivviraṇṭum paṭ eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyavi.
paṭutal = paṭital i. = . vīḻtal.  ii. olittal.
vīḻtaṟporuḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyākap paṭi eṉpataṉiṉṟum pala karuttukkaḷum coṟkaḷum piṟakkum.
paṭital = 1. viḻutal : kīḻttaṅkal, nilipeṟutal, paḻakutal.
pēci muṭivātal. (e-ṭu) vili paṭintatu.
2. viḻutal.  - oruvariṉ aṭivaṇaṅkal, aṭippaṭutal, kīḻp paṭital. tāḻmiyā yiruttal.
3. viḻutal : cāyntu allatu maṭaṅki viḻutal, nilattil allatu uṭampiṟ paṭutal.
(e-ṭu) payir nilattiṟ paṭintirukkiṟatu.
caṭināyiṉ urōmam paṭintirukkiṟatu.
mayir taliyiṟ paṭintirukkiṟatu.
paṉṟiyiṉ vayiṟu nilattiṟ paṭikiṟatu.
4. viḻutal : oṉṟil oṉṟu viḻuntu ataṉ uruvam amital.
paṭi (peyar) = oṉṟiloṉṟu viḻuntataṉālāṉa uruvam, ataṉ vaṭivam, aḷavu, mātiri, vaki.
paṭi = 1 a copy (e-ṭu) paṭiyōli opposed to  mūvavōli.
mūṭciyiṟ kiḻitta vōli paṭiyōli mūla vōli.  (taṭuttāṭkoṇṭa purāṇam)
an article, uruppaṭi
2. aḷavu (potu) grade, paṭippaṭiyāy, vāyiṟpaṭi, paṭikkaṭṭu, paṭi, paṭikkācu.
niṟi - paṭikkal
mukattal - oru paṭi, paṭiyaḷakkiṟavaṉ - kākkiṟavaṉ.
paṭi = bata, allowance.
3. vitam allatu vaki.
appaṭi, ippaṭi, eppaṭi, ataṉpaṭi, coṉṉapaṭi, mēṟpaṭi.
ellāp paṭiyālum eṇṇiṉāl ivvuṭampu  (auviyār)
coṉṉapaṭi kēḷātavaṉ.
eṉakkoru paṭiyāy varukiṟatu. paṭiyāy - vaṭiyāy (pōlittiripu).
4. Form. paṭi + vu = paṭivu + am = paṭivam (pa-va. pōli) E. body; a.S. bodig; Hind. pataṉ.
paṭi - (n.) copy, duplicate, anything of equal value, exchange, answer, return, substitution.
paṭi - paṭil. cf. kuṭi - kuṭil, viṭṭi - viṭṭil.
paṭil - patil - vatil (corruption).
patil = return, answer, substitutin.
patilāḷ = substitute. patilāḷi = representative.
paṭi eṉṉum collē vaṭamoḻiyil prati eṉṟākum. vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ meymmutalkaḷi rakaram cērtteḻutuvatu peruvaḻakku.
(e-ṭu) pavaḷam - pravāḷam : tamiḻam - traviṭam
ṭa - ta cf. paṭāki - patāki. paṭi - prati
prati eṉṉum  collukku vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamilli. prati eṉṉum upacarkkam peṟṟu. pratiniti eṉṉum peyaruṇ ṭākum.
paṭi (v.imp.) = viḻu.
paṭi - vaṭi to fall down, to filter, to select -  vaṭikaṭṭutal. to cast as moulten metal- vaṭittuca ceytal. to abate, as water nīrvaṭital. to hang low, as the lobe of the ear - kātu vaṭital.
paṭi - pati (v.imp.) - to fall, to make impression on the mind, to be comprehended, to be memorised - maṉattiṟ paṭiyavilli. maṉattiṟ patiyavilli.
pati = to fall down, to settle down, to dwell. 
patital-vatital = taṅkutal, vacittal.
pati. (n.) an abode, a town.
"patipeyar viṉātal" (akapporuḷ), kāciyampati.
E. above (v.) a + bidan; = to wait.
A.S. abidan = a + bidanï bidan = to wait.
pati = bide. cf. maturi - Madura.

patipōṭu - to crouch, to transplant.
patiyam pōṭutal allatu vittal - to transplant a cluster of saplings, to graft, to heap up cowdung in a regular shape.
pati - to sink in, to print : pati + am = patam (1) kāl. (2) nili.
pati + am = pātam. foot (mutaṉili tirintu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar, ākupeyar).
cf. paṭi + am = pāṭam.

koḷ + tal = kōṭal. 
cel + tal = cēṟal.

cf. aṭi + mi = aṭimi : kuṭi + mi - kuṭimi.

vaṭamoḻi
camaṟkirutatti vaṭamoḻiyeṉṟum tamiḻit teṉmoḻi yeṉṟum toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki viṭuvatāl, muṉṉatu vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu vanta ayaṉ moḻi eṉṟum, piṉṉatu mutalil iruntē teṟkiṉkaṇ vaḻaṅki vanta nāṭṭumoḻiyeṉṟum teḷḷat teḷivāka aṟiyalām.
moḻikkuc coṉṉatu moḻiyāḷarkkum okkum cilar varalāṟṟuṇmikku māṟāka vaṭamoḻiyum muṟkālattil iruntē teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta teṉpar. avarkku vaṭamoḻi eṉṉum peyarē vāyaṭittal kāṇka. (col. 21).
1. vaṭamoḻi: eḻuttiyal
(1) vaṇṇamāla (varṇa mālā)
vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakku allatu kuṟuṅkaṇakku varṇamālā eṉappeṟum. vaṇṇam-varṇa. māli-mālā. varṇa, akṣara eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉ potup peyarkaḷ. ivaṟṟuṭ piṉṉatu aciyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.  (va.va.)
vakiyum tokiyum
uyireḻuttukkaḷ (13) :
a, ā, i, ī, u, ū, ru, rū, lu, ē, ai, ō, au.
a, a#, i, i, u, u#, r@u, r@u#, l@u, e#, ai, o, au.
aṉuvāra
visarka (pa) :
meyyeḻuttukkaḷ (33):  (va.va.)
aivarkkam (25)
k, kh, g, gh, n@

t@. t@h, d@,  d@h, n@

p, ph, b, bh, m
iṭiyiṉam (4) y, r, l, v
irippiṉam (3) c, ṣ, 
mūccoli (1) h.
pākupāṭum peyarum
uyireḻuttu ac (ac) eṉṟum vara eṉṟum pūta (bhu#ta) eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ kuṟil hrava eṉṟum neṭil tīrkka (di#rgha) e eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum.
meyyeḻuttu hal eṉṟum vyañcaṉa eṉṟum peyar peṟum . avaṟṟuḷ aivarkkamum parcā (Mutes) eṉṟum, iṭiyiṉam. antatā (antastha# : Semi-vowels) eṉṟum, irippiṉmum mūccoliyum (Aspirate) uṣmāṇ : (Sibilants) eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
aivarkkaṅkaḷum, muṟiyē, olipaṟṟik kavarkkam kavarga), cavarkkam (ca#varga), ṭavarkkam (t@avarga), tavarkkam (tavarga), pavarkkam (pavarga) eṉṟum; piṟappiṭampaṟṟik kaṇṭyā : (Gutturals), tālavyā : (Palatals), mūrtaṉyā : (Murdhanya#:- Linguals or Cerebrals) tantyā : (dantya# :- Dentals), ōṣṭyā : (Labials) eṉṟum, collappeṟum.
(2) oliyum piṟappum
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ; rukaram kuṟṟiyalukaram cērnta rakaramākum; rūkāram ataṉ neṭil allatu nīṭṭam. lukaram kuṟṟiya lukaram cērnta lakaram.
aṉuvāra eṉpatu muntiṉa vuyiri yaṭuttolikkum makara vaki.
visarkkam (visāka) eṉpatu āytap pōli; āyiṉ vari vaṭiviṉṟi uyirēṟap peṟuvatu; sakarattiṉ tiripāka varakkūṭiyatu.
k, kh ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉum p, ph ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉum vantu ari visarkkamāka olikkum olikku, muṟiyē, jihvā mulīya eṉṟum upatmāṉīya (dh) eṉṟum peyar. muṉṉataṟku miṭaṟum piṉṉataṟku itaḻum piṟappiṭamām.
cilaviṭattu, uyirkaḷum ya pa la ākiya iṭiyiṉa meykaḷum mūkkoliyuṭaṉ icikkum. aṉṟu avi aṉunācikam eṉappaṭum.
ūṣmāṇaṅkaḷuḷ : hakarattiṟ miṭaṟum, cakarattiṟku iṭi yaṇṇamum, ṣakarattiṟku muṉṉaṇṇamum, sakarattiṟkup pallaṇṇamum, piṟappiṭamām.
ai, au eṉṉum iru neṭilum mutaṟkālattil āy, āv eṉa nīṇṭolittaṉa veṉṟum, āriyam tamiḻoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ, avi tamiḻiṟpōl ay, av eṉak kuṟuki yolik kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, kūṟuvar.
tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvāṉa piṟa eḻuttukka ḷellām, tamiḻiṟpōṉṟē olikkumeṉa aṟika.
ē, o iraṇṭum tamiḻil ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ nīṭṭamāṉa taṉi yolikaḷākak (Simple vowels) koḷḷappeṟum; camaṟkirutat til tavaṟākap puṇarolikaḷākak (Diphthongs) koḷḷap peṟu kiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku ammoḻiyil ekara okara miṉmiyum kuṇacan tiyumē kāraṇam.
a,ā+i,ī - ē. e-ṭu : mahā + intira(ṉ) = mahēntira (ṉ)
a, ā+u, ū=o.  e-ṭu : kula + uttuṅkaṉ = kulōttuṅkaṉ
(3) eḻuttuc cāriyi
kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu civaṇi
nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyi eṉpatu tol kāppiyam (134)
uyireḻuttiṉ utaviyiṉṟit tāmāka volikkāta mey yeḻuttukkaḷi olippittaṟ poruṭṭum, tāmāka volik kum uyireḻuttukkaḷiyum eḷitāka olippittaṟ poruṭṭum, cila tuṇiyolikaḷip paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlār amittuḷḷaṉar. avi cāriyi eṉappeṟum.
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ kuṟiṟkuk karamum neṭiṟkuk kāramum cāriyiyām. aikāra aukāraṅkaṭkuk kāṉ eṉpatu ciṟappuc cāriyi. itik kuṟiṟkum meykkum payaṉpaṭuttiyatu piṟkālat tatākat terikiṉṟatu.
meyyeḻuttukkaṭku avvum akaramum cāriyi.
meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum. (tol. 46)
e-ṭu:  valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.  (tol. 19)
vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu. (kṣa. 81).
vaṭamoḻi meykaḷum tamiḻ muṟippaṭiyē akaramoṭu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. uyirmey kaṭkuḷ kuṟiṟkuk karamum neṭiṟku meyc cāriyiyoṭu kūṭiya uyirneṭiṟ cāriyiyum cāriyiyām.
e-ṭu : kakaram (ka), kakara ākāram (kā).
āytattiṟku a-kēṉam eṉpatu cāriyiyām.
e-ṭu. aḵkēṉam. itu tolkāppiyattiṟ collappeṟa villi.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻeḻuttuc cāriyi yoḻuṅku niṟivāka amintuḷḷatu.
vaṭamoḻiyār karam, kāram eṉṉum iraṇṭiyē tamiḻi ṉiṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. atōṭu kuṟiṟku iraṇṭiyum vēṟu pāṭiṉṟi āḷvar. itaṉāṟ cāriyi yamippiṉ tamiḻ mūlam teḷivākat terikiṉṟatu. karam, kāram eṉpaṉa vaṭamoḻiyil kara, kāra eṉṟu īṟukeṭṭu niṟkum. cāriyi eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭum vaṭa moḻiyililli.
rakāra ḻakāram kuṟṟoṟ ṟakā.  (tol. 49).
uū kāram navavoṭu navilā.  (tol. 74)
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟic ceyyuḷ tiripil aṭakkutal vēṇṭum.
karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻut tilavē.  (tol.. 135) 
eṉṟu vilakkiyapiṉ,
aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum. (tol. 137)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyarē muraṇpaṭak kūṟuvatāl,
varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟum kuṟṟeḻut tuṭiya.  (tol. 136).
eṉṟuavar kuṟittiruppatu kumarināṭṭu vaḻakkoṭu porun tiyatāyt tōṉṟavilli.
(4) muṟi
mēliyāriya moḻikaṭkuḷ oṉṟiṟkāvatu muṟi eṉṉum eḻuttilakkaṇam iṉṟumilli. vēta āriyar eḻuttum ilakkiyamumiṉṟi intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar. vēta moḻikkum vētattiṟkum eḻutākkiḷavi eṉṟu peyar. vētattik kuṟikkum cruti (kēḷvi) eṉṉum peyarē āriyarkku eḻuttiṉmiyi uṇarttum.
intiyāvil maṭṭumiṉṟi ulakattilēyē mutaṉmutal neṭuṅ kaṇakku vakuttatum ataṟku muṟiyamittatum tamiḻarē.
vēta āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpukoṇṭa piṉṉarē, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻikkuk kiranta vaṇṇamāliyi amittuk koṇṭaṉar.
uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum, uyiruḷ ā ī ū muṉṉum ē ō piṉṉum, ovvōr uyirilum kuṟil muṉṉum neṭil piṉṉum, ēyiṉ piṉ aiyum ōviṉ piṉ auvum, āyta motta visarkkam uyirkkum meykkum iṭiyum, meyyuḷ vali vaki muṉṉum meli piṉṉumākak kacaṭatapa muṟiyilum, avaṟṟiṟkup piṉ iṭiyiṉam yaralava muṟiyilum, amintiruppatu, vaṭa moḻi vaṇṇamāli tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyi muṟṟun taḻuviya teṉpati, ettuṇit teḷḷat teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṉṟatu! iḵtoṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyiṉ piṉmiyik kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
uyir variciyuṭ cerukappaṭṭa ciṟappeḻuttukkaḷi nōkki ṉum, muṟṟukaratti yaṭuttuk kuṟṟukaramum, rakarak kuṟṟukarattiṉ piṉ lakarak kuṟṟukaramum vikkappeṟṟiruppatu, ettuṇit tamiḻ muṟiyoṭṭiyatu!
(5) aḷapu
aḷavu eṉpatu eḻuttoliyiṉ māttiri allatu aḷavu. aḷapu, māttiri eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu tamiḻc ciṟappuc col; piṉṉatu teṉmoḻikkum vaṭa moḻikkum potuc col.
meykku ariyum kuṟiṟku oṉṟum neṭiṟku iraṇṭum māttiri eṉpatu irumoḻikkum potu. vaṭamoḻiyil plutam eṉṉum aḷapeṭiyiyum uyir allatu uyirmey vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟākac cērttu, ataṟku mummāttiri eṉa vakuttuḷḷaṉar.
aḷapeṭi tamiḻilum uṇṭu. āyiṉ ati oḻuṅkāṉa uyir vakiyākak koṇṭilar. ēṉeṉiṉ, atu ulakiyal allatu iyaṟkiyaḷapeṭiyil mummāttiri mutal pattu allatu paṉṉiru māttirivari nīṇṭaḷapeṭukkum. ceyaṟkiyākiya ceyyuḷiyal aḷapeṭi kuṟukiyolippiṉum, arukiyum tiṭṭamāṉa māttiri varampiṉyumē nikaḻum.
aḷapeṭiyiyum ōr uyir vakiyākak koṇṭatu vaṭa moḻiyilakkaṇattiṉ piṉmiyiyē kāṭṭum.
(6) vaṭivam
vaṭamoḻikku mutaṉmutal eḻuttu ēṟpaṭṭatu tamiḻ nāṭṭil tāṉ. atu tamiḻ ēṭṭeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tirinta kiranta veḻuttu. ataṉ kālam tōrā. ki.mu. 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
aintiram eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi viyākaraṇam tolkāppiyat tiṟku muntiya nūlātalāl, vaṭamoḻik kirantaveḻuttu ataṟku ōriru nūṟṟāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
kirantam eṉpatu nūl. vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkuc conta vaḻakku moḻiyiṉmiyāl, nūliṟku maṭṭum payaṉpayaṭuttappeṟṟa eḻuttik kirantāṭcaram eṉṟaṉar.
iṉṟuḷḷa tēvanākari ki.pi. 4-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ karuk koṇṭu 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil muḻuniṟivaṭintatu. atiyum uṟṟu nōkkiṉ, ataṟkum kirantaveḻuttiṟkumuḷḷa nuṇṇiya vaṭivoppumi pulaṉākum.
tēvamoḻi yeṉṉum vaṭamoḻikku nakaraṅkaḷil āḷap peṟṟa eḻuttu tēvanākari. tēvar nakaraṅkaḷil āḷappeṟṟatu tēva nākari eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. avar tēvareṉṟu nāṇātu kuṟippatu pirāmaṇari.
eḻuttu		vaṭivam
aṉunācikam
jihvāmūlīya	-	(k, kh ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ)
upatmāṉīya	-	(p, ph ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ)
tamiḻk kiyeḻuttil cila kūṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi āḷvatu pōl, vaṭamoḻi acceḻuttil ērāḷamāṉa kūṭṭeḻuttuk kaḷi amittirukkiṉṟaṉar. avi cantiyakṣaram eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu : kṣa-kṣa, crī-srī
ivi kirantam. iṅṅaṉam tēvanākariyil ērāḷamākavuṇṭu.
meyyik kuṟikkak kirantattil mēṟpuḷḷiyum tēvanākariyiṟ kīḻiḻuppum iṭṭatum, iruvakiyeḻuttilum uyirmeykkut taṉi vaṭivamittum, muṟṟum tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyē.
(7) puṇarcci
eḻuttup puṇarcci, coṟpuṇarcci eṉap puṇarcci iru vakippaṭum.
tamiḻilakkaṇatti mutaṉ mutal āyntu kaṇṭu nūliyaṟṟiyavar taliciṟanta muṉivar eṉpatu,
viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (1594) viḷaṅkum.
muṉivar meypporuḷuṇarvu mutirntavarātaliṉ, ulakiluḷḷa ellāp poruḷ kaḷum uyir, uyirillatu (mey), uyiruḷḷatu (uyirmey) eṉa mūvakiyāka irukkakkaṇṭu, avaṟṟi yotta mūvaki eḻuttolikaṭkum avaṟṟiṉ peyariyē uvamiyāku peyarāka iṭṭu, uyirmeykkut taṉi vaṭivum amittaṉar.
itaṉāl, uyirmeyyoliyi uyirum meyyumāka mutaṉ mutaṟ pakuttavar tamiḻar eṉpatum, uyirmeyyilēyē eḻuttup puṇarcci tōṉṟiviṭṭateṉpatum, teḷivām.
coṟpuṇarcci ellā moḻikaḷilumuṇṭu. āyiṉ, iyaṉ moḻiyilēyē puṇarcci oḻuṅkākavum puṇarccoṟkaḷ eḷitāyp pakukkak kūṭiyaṉavākavumirukkum. tirimoḻikaḷil taṉiccollum kūṭṭuc collum perumpālum tirintiruppa tālum, tiripup puṇarcci nikaḻavēṇṭiya viṭattum iyalpup puṇarcciyē perumpāṉmiyāy  nikaḻvatālum, tiripup puṇarcci nikaḻntaviṭattum atu varivaṭiviṟ kāṭṭap peṟāmi yālum, avaṟṟiṟ puṇarcciyilakkaṇam vakukkap peritum iṭamilli.
camaṟkirutam tirimoḻiyāyiṉum, toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭamiyāṉum, aintiliru pakutikkuk kuṟiyātu tamiḻāyiruttalāṉum. tamiḻit taḻuviyē ilakkaṇañ ceyyap peṟṟamiyāṉum, puṇarcciyilakkaṇam ataṟkamintateṉka. āyiṉum, tirimoḻiyātaliṉ, tamiḻp puṇarccipōṟ perumpālum oḻuṅkupaṭātu ettuṇiyō vilakkukkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷateṉa aṟika.
vaṭamoḻip puṇarcciyum tamiḻp puṇarcci pōṉṟē uyirīṟum meyyīṟum paṟṟi iruvakippaṭum. uyirīṟṟup puṇarcciyi acsanti allatu varasanti eṉṟum, meyyīṟṟup puṇarcciyi halsanti eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
uyirīṟṟup puṇarcciyil, kuṇam, virutti eṉṉum iruvaki uyirttiripukaḷ vaṭamoḻikkuc ciṟappāṉavi. iṉṉiṉṉa ciṟṟuyirkaṭku (Simple Vowels) iṉṉiṉṉa vuyirkaḷ allatu acikaḷ kuṇam eṉṟum, iṉṉiṉṉa vuyirkaḷ allatu acikaḷ virutti eṉṟum, vakukkakppaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟik kīḻvaruṅkaṭṭamippiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
iyaluyir	a	i,ī	u,ū	ru,rū	lu
kuṇavuyir	a	ē	ō	ar	al
viruttiyuyir	ā	ai	au	ār	āl
tīrkkasanti eṉṉum mūvaki neṭiṟpuṇarcci, kuḷāmpal (kuḷa āmpal), putūr (putu ūr) eṉṉum marūup puṇarcciyot taviyē.
tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉṉum muppuṇarccit tiripukaḷum, ākama(m), ātēca(m), lōpa(m)eṉamoḻipeyarkkappaṭṭuḷa.
eṇpeyar muṟipiṟap puruvam māttiri
mutalī ṟiṭinili pōli yoṉṟā
patampuṇar peṉappaṉ ṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē.
eṉa naṉṉūlār (57) kūṟum paṉṉiruvaki eḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kaḷuḷ, mutal īṟu iṭinili eṉṉum mūṉṟum tirimoḻi yākiya vaṭamoḻikkilli.
(8) vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttup perukkam
kirēkkak kuṟuṅkaṇakku (24)
A (a and a#), B,G (g and n(), D, E, Z, E#, TH, I (i and i), K, L, M, N, X, O, P, R, S ( s and z), T, U (u and u#)))), PH, KH, PS, O.
ivaṟṟuḷ TH, X (ks), PH, KH, PS eṉṉum aintē puṇar meykaḷ; X (ks) vaṭamoḻiyil kṣa (ks) āka vaḷarntuḷḷatu.
ilattīṉ kuṟuṅkaṇakku (21)
A, B, C, D, E, F, Z, H, I (J), K, L, M, N, O, P, Q, R, S, T, V (U), X
ivaṟṟuḷ X (ks) oṉṟē puṇarmey. atuvum mūccoliyaṟṟatu. C=G. J=Y.
tīyūttāṉiyak kuṟuṅkaṇakku (āṅkilam)
A, B, C, D, E, F, G, H, I, J, K, M, N, O, P, Q, R, S, T, U, V, W, X, Y, Z.
 ivaṟṟuḷ X (ks) oṉṟē puṇarmey.
kirēkka arivari piṉīciyariṭattiṉiṉṟum, ilattīṉ arivari kirēkkariṭattiṉiṉṟum,  peṟappaṭṭaviyākum.
iṉṟu airōppā muḻutum vaḻaṅkum āriyamoḻi arivarikaḷ, ilattīṉ arivariyit taḻuviyaviyē.
āṅkilam piṟkāla moḻiyātalāl, cila piṟkāla volikaḷ iṇivarikaḷāl (Diagraphs) kuṟikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu : ch=¢r, sh=õ.
camaṟkirutam āriyamum tiraviṭamuṅ kalanta moḻiyātaliṉ, pala mutirolikaḷik koṇṭiruppatuṭaṉ, valliṉ aivaki yuḷḷum poli (Voiced) yoliyuṭaṉum poliyā (Voiceless) voliyuṭaṉum mūccoliyi oḻuṅkākac cērttu avaṟṟiṟkut taṉi vaṭivam amittuḷḷatu.
kh, gh; ch, jh; t@h, dh; th, dh; ph, bh eṉṉum pattuk kūṭṭu mey kaṭkum, vaṭamoḻi yil taṉi vaṭivam amintiruttal kāṇka. innilimi vēṟem moḻiyilumilli.
(9) vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyiṉ piṉmi
vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyiṉ piṉmi piṉvaruñ cāṉṟu kaḷāl tuṇiyappeṟum.
(1) vaṭamoḻiyiṉ piṉmi.
(2) āriya vētam paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu eḻutāk kiḷaviyāyiruntami.
(3) tamiḻeḻuttip piṉpaṟṟiya kirantam tēvanākarikku muṟpaṭṭami.
(4) oli vaṭivu, māttiri, cāriyi, varivaṭivu, muṟi mutaliya vaṟṟil iyaṉṟavari tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyiruttal.
(5) olipperukkam.
(6) kūṭṭu meykaṭkut taṉi vaṭivu koṇṭuḷḷami.
aivarkkaṅkaḷum kavarkkam cavarkkam (e) eṉa mutaṉmeyyā lēyē peyarpeṟṟi ruppatāl, ammutaṉmeyyiṉ tiripē piṟpaṭṭa mūṉṟum eṉpatu peṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, tamiḻppoliyā volic coṟkaḷ tiraviṭattiṟ poli voliccoṟ kaḷākavum, āṅkilap poliyāvolic coṟkaḷ ilattīṉ kirēkkattiṟ polivolic coṟ kaḷākavum, tirintiruttaliyum nōkkit teḷika.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyivarukka mutaṉmeykaḷum tamiḻ valli ṉattiṉ valitta vaṭivē yeṉpatiyum kaṇṭaṟika.
(7) mēliyāriyampōṟ kuṟuṅkaṇakku maṭṭuṅ koṇṭirātu tamiḻpōl neṭuṅkaṇakkuṅ koṇṭiruttal.  (va.va.)
(10) vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyiṉ oḻuṅkiṉmi
(1) ru, rū, lu eṉṉum uyirmeykaḷ uyiriṉattiṟ cērkkap paṭṭiruttal
(2) tēvanākariyiṟ cila eḻuttukkaṭku oṉṟōṭoṉṟi mayakkaṟkēṟṟavāṟu vaṭivamintiruttal.
(3) makaravoliyākiya aṉuvārakkuṟi melliṉap potuvā yiruttal.
(11) vaṭamoḻik kuṟuṅkaṇakku nūṟpākkaḷ

	ippatiṉāl nūṟpākkaṭkum makēcuvara cūttiraṅkaḷ allatu civacūttiraṅkaḷ eṉṟu peyar. ivi pāṇiṉīyattoṭu potu vākac cērttuk kūṟappaṭiṉum, civaperumāṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa viyeṉṟum, avar tuṭiyip patiṉāṉmuṟi iyakka ippati ṉāl olittokutikaḷum tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi yāḷar kūṟuvar. vaṭamoḻiyiyē tēvamoḻi yeṉpār iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟuvatil viyappoṉṟumilli. āyiṉum tuṇicca lāṉa ēmāṟṟē.
aṣṭātyāyī eṉṉum pāṇiṉiyilakkaṇattiṉ mūlap pāṭattil ivi cērkkap peṟāmiyālum,  pāṇiṉīya nūṟ pākkaḷ pōl atikāraviyal eṇ peṟāmiyālum, naṉṉūṟ potup pāyira nūṟpākkaḷpōl muṉṉarē palarāṟ ceyyap paṭṭu vaḻivaḻi tirunti vantaviyāyumirukkalām.
innūṟpākkaḷiṉ iṟutiyeḻuttukkaṭku it eṉṟu peyar. oru nūṟpāviṉ mutaleḻuttiyum ataṉpiṉ varum oritti yum cērppiṉ pratyākāra eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭākum. akkuṟiyīṭu. ataṉ mutaleḻuttiyum, ataṉ īreḻuttiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ittallā ellā nūṟpā eḻuttukkaḷiyum kuṟikka utavum.
immuṟiyil, aṇ eṉpatu akarattiyum akara ikara vukaraṅ kaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum. ac eṉpatu ellā uyirkaḷiyum, hal eṉpatu ellā meykaḷiyum, al eṉpatu ellā uyir kaḷiyum meykaḷiyum kuṟikkum.
itaṉāl, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉāl nūṟpākkaṭkum pirattiyākāra cūttiraṅkaḷ eṉṟum peyar.
2. vaṭamoḻi: colliyal
ellā moḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇavakic coṟkaḷ peyarviṉi iṭi eṉa mūṉṟē. ivi cikṣi, niruktam eṉṉum toṭakka vaṭa nūlkaḷil, nāma (m), ākyāt (m), nipāt (m) eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. pāṇiṉi ivaṟṟi muṟiyē cupantam, tiṅantam avyaya(m) eṉak kuṟittār.
upasarkkam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu nipātattuḷ aṭaṅkum.
āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉpē, nāmam eṉṉuñ col mēliyāriyattil namon, nomem eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyatākat terivatālum, tiyūttāṉiyattil viṉiyākavum vaḻaṅkivaruvata ṉālum, oru kāl atu mēliyāriyac collāka iruppiṉum irukkalām. ākyāta (kh) eṉṉumcol, col eṉṟē poruḷ paṭuvatu. itu poruḷilum āṭciyilum verb (L. verbum) eṉṉum āṅkilac colliyottatu. kiyā=col. verb=brhš. nipāta eṉpatu nipat eṉṉam kūṭṭu mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirintatu. ni=kīḻ. pat=viḻu. nipāta=neṟi tiṟampiyatu, oḻuṅkaṟṟu. paṭi (ta.) - pat.
vēṟṟumi yurupēṟṟa peyarum kālaveṇṇiṭaṅ kāṭṭum īṟēṟṟa viṉiyum patam eṉappaṭum.
(11) peyarccol (supanta)
tātu eṉappaṭum mutaṉiliyoṭu, kirut eṉappaṭum peyaraṭi yiṭiyoṭṭum tattitaṉ eṉappaṭum vaḻinili yiṭi yoṭṭum cērnta vaṭiviṟkup pirakiruti allatu pirātipatikam eṉṟu peyar. ippirātipatikattuṭaṉ vēṟṟumi yurupu cēriṉ peyarppatamām. atu cupantam eṉappaṭum. cup (sup) eṉpatu vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷiyellām potuvākak kuṟikkum pāṇiṉīyak kuṟiyīṭu. antam īṟu.
kirut īṟu kirutantam eṉṟum. tattita īṟu tattitāntam eṉṟum collappaṭum. iviyiraṇṭiṟkum piratyam eṉpatu vaṭa moḻip potuppeyar.
pirātipatikattilum peyarppatattilum krut, tattitaṉ ākiya iruvakiyiṭi yoṭṭum cērntumirukkalām; avviraṇṭi loṉṟu taṉittuyirukkalām.
pāl (liṅka)
vaṭamoḻiyil tiṇiyilli; pālmaṭṭum uṇṭu. atu pulliṅkam (āṇpāl), tirīliṅkam (peṇpāl), napumsaka liṅkam (alippāl), eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
vaṭamoḻip pālvakuppu iyaṟkiyāṉa poruḷiyalpu paṟṟiya taṉṟu; perumpālum ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkaṇa muṟi paṟṟiyatu. cila peyarkaḷē uṇmiyāṉa pālkāṭṭum.
e-ṭu : putakam (ā. gh.), cilā (pe.pā.) = kal.
maṉiviyik kuṟikkum tāram (ā. pā) pāriyā (pe. gh.), kaḷattiram (a. pā.) eṉṉum muccoṟkaḷum, pāl vēṟupaṭum.
eṇ (vacaṉa)
vaṭamoḻiyil, ēkavacaṉam (orumi), tvivacaṉam (irumi), pahu vacaṉam (paṉmi) eṉa eṇ mūvakippaṭum.
mēliyāriyattic cērnta tiyūttāṉiyattilum ilattīṉilum irumiyeṇ illi; camaṟkirutattiṟku mika neruṅkiya kirēkkattil tāṉ uṇṭu.
iṭam (puruṣa)
iṭam tamiḻiṟ pōṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyilum mūṉṟām.
avi uttama puruṣa (taṉmi), matyam puruṣa (muṉṉili), pratamapuruṣa (paṭarki) eṉappaṭum.
vēṟṟumi (vipakti)
kirēkka ilakkaṇattil, eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi viḷivēṟṟumi, ceyporuḷ vēṟṟumi, kiḻami vēṟṟumi, koṭi vēṟ ṟumi eṉṉum aivēṟṟumikaḷum, ilattīṉ ilakkaṇattil eḻuvāyvēṟṟumi, kiḻamivēṟṟumi, koṭi vēṟṟumi, ceyporuḷ vēṟṟumi, viḷivēṟṟumi, nīkkavēṟṟumi, eṉṉum aṟuvēṟṟumikaḷumē, kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. mutaṟkāla āṅkilattil eḻuvāy, viḷi, ceyporuḷ, kiḻami, koṭi, karuvi eṉṉum aṟuvēṟṟumikaḷ iruntatākac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. āyiṉ mēliyāriya moḻi oṉṟilāvatu eṇ vēṟṟumi collappaṭavilli.
vaṭamoḻiyilō tamiḻiṟpōl eṇvēṟṟumi kāṭṭap peṟuvatuṭaṉ, avaṟṟiṉ variciyum peyarum poruḷum muṟṟum tamiḻi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.

(2) viṉiccol (tiṅanta)
tātu eṉṉum mutaṉiliyoṭu kālaveṇṇiṭaṅ kāṭṭum īṟu cēriṉ viṉippa tamām. atu tiṅantam eṉappaṭum. tiṅ eṉpatu viṉiyīṟukaḷiyellām potuppaṭak kuṟikkum pāṇiṉiyak kuṟiyīṭu.
camaṟkiruta viṉikaḷ, āṟukālaṅkaḷum (Tenses) nāṉku paṭicu kaḷum (Moods) mūṉṟu uṟavukaḷum (Voices) koḷḷum. kālam kāla eṉṟum, paṭicu artta eṉṟum, uṟavu prayōka eṉṟum, peyar peṟum.
āṟukālaṅkaḷum nāṉku paṭicukaḷum lakkaraṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil ellā viṉi vakikaṭkum iṭukuṟiyāṉa kuṟiyīṭuṇṭu.

camaṟkiruta viṉimuṟṟīṟukaḷ, taṟporuṭṭuviṉi (ātmaṉē pata) īṟukaḷ eṉṟum, maṟporuṭṭuviṉi (paramipata) īṟukaḷ eṉṟum, iruvakippaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila viṉikaḷ taṟporuṭṭu viṉiyīṟukaḷiyum, cila viṉikaḷ maṟporuṭṭu viṉiyīṟu kaḷiyum, cila viṉikaḷ iraṇṭiyum ēṟkum. muṉṉoṭṭuc cērkkiyāl, ta. po. viṉi ma. po. viṉiyākavum, ma. po. viṉi ta. po. viṉiyākavum māṟuvatuṇṭu.

e-ṭu : 
tēvatatta : yajatē (t@)= tēvatattaṉ taṉakku vēṭkiṟāṉ.
tēvatatta : yajati (t@) = tēvatattaṉ piṟaṉukku vēṭkiṟāṉ. vēṭṭal vēḷvi ceytal.
ellā viṉikaḷum pattu lakkaraṅkaḷilum mūviṭattum mūveṇṇilum puṭipeyarum.
nikaḻkālam, iṉṟalā iṟantakālam, ēvalviṉi, āṟṟal viṉi ākiya nālilakkaraṅkaḷilum, viṉikaḷ cila vēṟupāṭaṭi kiṉṟaṉa. avvēṟupāṭu vikaraṇa(m) eṉappaṭum. vēṟupāṭaṭi yum lakkaraṅkaḷ sārvatātuka eṉṟum, vēṟu pāṭaṭiyā lakkaraṅkaḷ artta tātuka eṉṟum, collappaṭum.
vikaraṇampaṟṟi viṉikaḷ pattuk kaṇaṅkaḷāka vakukkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ippattuk kaṇaṅkaḷum mīṇṭum iru tokuti kaḷākap pakukkappaṭṭuḷa. mutalām iraṇṭām mūṉṟām pattāṅ kaṇaṅkaḷ mutal tokutiyum, ēṉiya iraṇṭām tokutiyum ākum. mutal tokuti viṉiyaṭikaḷ akarattil iṟṟu eṉṟun tiriyātu niṟkum; iraṇṭān tokuti viṉiyaṭikaḷ akarattil iṟātu tirintuvarum.
vaṭamoḻic ceyappāṭṭiṟantakāla viṉiyeccavīṟākiya `ta', tamiḻiṟantakāla viṉiyecca vīṟākiya tuvvi orupuṭi yottatu.
vaṭamoḻi etirmaṟiyēva laciyāka varum `mā', va;tatu eṉṉum teluṅkuc colliṉ tiripākat terikiṉṟatu. kūṭātu eṉṉum etirmaṟiyāṟṟalviṉi vilakkup poruḷiyum uṇarttum. nī pōkakkūṭātu=nī pōkātē, nī pōkavēṇṭām.
ollum=kūṭum.  ollātu=kūṭātu.
ollātu-(te.) ottu (vēṇṭām)-vattu-(i.) mat.
vētakālac cūracēṉiyilum mattu eṉṟē iruntirukkalām.
mattu-mat-mā(va.) Gk.me#.
mā pi : (bh) = añcātē.
vaṭamoḻiyil, tvitvaliṭ eṉṉum cēymi yiṟantakāla viṉi yum saṉ eṉṉum ārva viṉiyum mutaṉili yiraṭṭittal, cila tamiḻ iṟantakāla viṉikaḷ mutaṉiliyiṟuti vali iraṭṭip patiyottatu. kirēkka moḻiyilum ittakiya iraṭṭip puṇṭu.
vaṭamoḻi tirimoḻiyātaliṉ, ataṉ viṉikaṭku vērc coṟkaḷ ammoḻiyililli. avi perumpālum iyaṉ moḻiyākiya tamiḻiltāṉ uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, vaṭamoḻi tēva moḻiyeṉṉum ēmāṟṟiṟkēṟpa vaṭamoḻi viṉic coṟ kaḷiṉ mutaṉilikaḷiyum (Themes) aṭikaḷiyum (Stems) vēr kaḷākak (Roots) kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. mēṉāṭṭārum, tamiḻi varalāṟṟu muṟippaṭi ārāyāmiyāl, vaṭamoḻiyi iyaṉmoḻiyeṉṟum, āriyat tāymoḻiyeṉṟum mayaṅki, vaṭavar kāṭṭiyuḷḷa pōli vērccoṟkaḷi uṇmiyāṉa vērccoṟkaḷeṉṟē nampiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
vaṭavar kāṭṭiyuḷḷa tātukkaḷ (vērccoṟkaḷ) ēṟattāḻa 1750. avi eṅṅaṉam tavaṟāṉavi eṉpatu, iṅku aintātukkaḷāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟum.

cuṭṭum viṉāvum
cuṭṭum viṉāvum, āriyamum cēmiyamumāṉa piṟamoḻikaḷilel lām coṟkaḷākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻilō avi coṟ kaḷāka maṭṭumiṉṟic coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. avveḻuttukkaḷ uṇmiyil ōreḻuttuc coṟkaḷē.
neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi.
kuṟṟeḻut tintum moḻiniṟi pilavē.
āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
ai uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu.
āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉāa.
evvayiṟ peyarum veḷippaṭat tōṉṟi.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (43, 44, 301, 31, 32, 552) kūṟuvatālum, cuṭṭum viṉāvum iṉṟum taṉi neṭilākat tiraviṭattilum vaṭatiraviṭak kāṉ muḷiyākiya intiyilum vaḻaṅkuvatālum, kuṟil neṭil palukka veḷitā yiruppataṉālum, āṇṭu īṇṭu yāṇṭu (ēṇṭu) eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭku mutal kuṟukiya vaṭiviṉ miyālum, cuṭṭu viṉāveḻuttukkaḷ mutaṟkālattil nīṇṭē yiruntu piṉṉark kuṟukiyami aṟiyappaṭum.
cuṭṭaṭikaḷum viṉāvaṭikaḷum taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷā yiruppata ṉāl, avaṟṟi iṭic colleṉṟu koṇṭaṉar muṉṉōr. avi taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷāyiṉum, poruḷuṇarttuva tāṟ col lāyum palaveḻuttuc coṟpōṉṟē  poruḷ  nirampiyum, iruppati aṟika.
āvūr, avvūr, antavūr eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṉṟē. a = anta 
(that) That eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟpōṉṟē, a, anta eṉpaṉavum cuṭṭup peyareccam (Demonstrative Adjective) eṉa aṟika. peyarit taḻuvuvatellām peyareccamē. atu terinili viṉiyākavu mirukkalām; kuṟippu viṉiyākavu mirukkalām; piṟavāṟum irukkalām.
iyalpāṉa cuṭṭukkaḷ cēymi, aṇmi, muṉmi eṉa mūṉṟē. ākavē, avaṟṟik kuṟikkum olikaḷ allatu eḻuttukkaḷum ā (a). ī. (i), ū (u) eṉṉum mūṉṟē.
ivi kumarikkaṇṭattil tamiḻt tōṟṟak kālattil kic cuṭṭukkaḷukkup pakaramāka (patilāka) vāyccuṭṭukkaḷākat tōṉṟiya  olikaḷ, immūvolikaḷaṉṟi vēṟevvolikaḷum ammūviṭattiyum cuṭṭāmiyi olittuk kāṇka.
muccuṭṭum mutaṉmutal tamiḻil tōṉṟiyataṉālēyē, avi tamiḻilum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa tiraviṭattilum iṉṟum taṉiyeḻuttu vaṭiviliruppatuṭaṉ, mūviṭattiyum oḻuṅkākac cuṭṭavum ceykiṉṟaṉa. iḵtoṉṟē tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlam eṉṉum uṇmiyik kāṭṭavum nāṭṭavum pōtiya cāṉṟām.
vaṭamoḻi tirimoḻi yātaliṉ, atiṟcuṭṭeḻuttillā tiruppatoṭu, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ iṭamāṟiyuñ cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu : atra=iṅkē. atya=iṉṟikku. atunā=ippōtu. cila akarac cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ cēymi yaṇmiyākiya īriṭattiyuñ cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu : ata=atu, itu.
cila ikarac cuṭṭukkaḷ ēkāramākat tirintuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu : ētat=itu, ēvam=ippaṭi.
cila cuṭṭukkaḷiṉ cārpu meyyāṉa takaram sakaramākat tirintuḷḷatu.
e-ṭu : tat (d) = avaṉ, avaḷ, atu.  (va.va)

3. vaṭamoḻi: toṭariyal
(1) tokic col (samāsa)
vētamoḻiyiṉ mūlamākiya mēliyāriya muṟippaṭi camaṟkirutac coṟṟoṭaramippu oru maruṅku vēṟupaṭṭi ruppiṉum, vēta moḻiyiṉ vaṭatiraviṭa allatu pirākirutak kalappiṉālum, camaṟ kirutattiṉ tamiḻt toṭarpiṉālum, atu oru maruṅku tamiḻiyum taḻuviyuḷḷatu.
camācam eṉṉum camaṟkirutak kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ allatu tokic coṟkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu aivakippaṭum.
(1) tuvantuvam (tvantva)  ummit toki
(2) taṟpuruṭaṉ (tatpuruṣa)  vēṟṟumit toki
(3)karumatārayaṉ (karumatāraya) paṇputtoki (irupeyaroṭṭu)
(4) pakuvirīki (pakuvrīhi) aṉmoḻit toki.
(5) avviyayīpavām (avyīpāva) iṭic colliyal toki allatu viṉiyeccaviyal toki.
samāsa eṉṉum colli, sam + a2 eṉṟu pakuppar. sam=kūṭa. a=eṟi. samāsa=(irucolli) uṭaṉiṭṭatu.
karumatārayaṉip pala ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ taṟpuruṭaṉil aṭakkik  kūṟum. atu poruntātu. eṇṇup paṇput tokiyit taṉiyāyp pirittut tviku eṉṟu peyar koṭuppar.
potuvāka nōkkiṉ, vaṭamoḻic camācap pakuppiṉum tamiḻt toki toṭarp pakuppu mikac ciṟantatu.
aṭukkut toṭar (vīpsā)
tamiḻiṟ  pōṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyilum coṟkaḷ aṭukkivaralām.
e-ṭu : vrukṣm vrukṣm siñcati=maram maramāyt taṇṇīr ūṟṟukiṟāṉ.
(2) coṟṟoṭar (vākya)
vaṭamoḻic coṟṟoṭarc colvarici perumpālum tamiḻ muṟiyi ottatē.
eḻuvāy muṉṉum ceyappaṭuporuḷ iṭiyum payaṉili. piṉṉum varuvatē iyalpāṉa muṟi.
pālaka : krantam paṭati=piyaṉ pottakattip paṭikkiṟāṉ. [ th ïnk ghyfh th M«u« ¡UàzªJ.=mtdhtJ intap piyaṉkaḷāvatu māmpaḻatti eṭuttuk koḷḷaṭṭum.
coṟṟoṭariṉ mūvuṟuppum aṭimoḻiyoṭum varum. aṭimoḻi potuvāka aṭikoḷikku muṉniṟkum.
e-ṭu : rūpavati trī = aḻakāṉa peṇ.
apyakāryasatam krutvā parttavyā=nūṟu tīviṉikaḷic ceytum kākkappaṭavēṇṭum.
iru, uṇṭu eṉṉum iṇippu viṉiyiṉṟip peyarum payaṉiliyāka varuvatuṇṭu.
e-ṭu : simha : cvāpatarāja : = arimā vilaṅkaracu.
ēṣa mē niccaya : = itu eṉ tīrmāṉam.
iṉi, coṟṟoṭariṉ mūvuṟuppukkaḷum, ētēṉum oru payaṉ nōkki, poruḷ māṟā vakiyil mēṟkuṟitta muṟi māṟiyum varum.
maṇīm cōrayati tēna : = maṇiyit tiruṭukiṟāṉ tiruṭaṉ. iṉi, kūṭṭuk kiḷaviyap payilvu, neṭuntoṭar yāppu, cārpuk kiḷaviyaṅkaṭkup pakaramāka eccaviṉi varal, muṟṟuviṉik kīṭāka iṟantakāla viṉiyecca āṭci, nēral kūṟṟiṉmi mutaliya kūṟukaḷilum, vaṭamoḻic coṟṟoṭaramippu tamiḻit taḻuviyatē.
vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇam eḻuttu, col, coṟṟoṭar eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu muṭikiṟatu. ceyyuḷilakkaṇam canta eṉṟum, aṇi yilakkaṇam alaṅkāra cātiram eṉṟum vēṟu nūlkaḷpōl vevvēṟākak kūṟappaṭum.
tamiḻil vaṭamoḻiyillāp poruḷilakkaṇa muṇmiyālum, paṇṭi yilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivi liruntamiyā lum, colliyaṭutta moḻiyaṟuppu poruḷeṉṟu koṇṭu, ataṉuḷḷē ceyyuḷiyum uṭampiṉ iyaṟkiyaḻaku pōṟ ceyyuḷilamin tirukkum aṇiyiyum aṭakki viṭṭaṉar, meyp poruḷiyal muṟṟat tuṟipōya mētaku paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlōr.
naṉṉūl, ciṉṉūl mutaliya teṉṉūlum eḻuttuñ collum coṟṟoṭarumē kūṟukiṉṟaṉavēyeṉiṉ, avi vaṭanūṉ muṟi taḻuviya piṉṉūleṉṟum, tolkāppiyampōl niṟi vāṉa piṇṭa nūlākātu kuṟivāṉa tuṇṭa nūlē yeṉṟum, kūṟi viṭukka.
vaṭamoḻi eṉpatu orukālum pēcappaṭāta nūṉmoḻiyē yātaliṉ,  ataṉ varalāṟeṉpatu uṇmiyil ilakkiya varalāṟē.  (va.va.)
vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum vēṟupāṭu
1.	eḻuttukkaḷiṉ toki (uṇmiyāṉapaṭi) vaṭa moḻiyil 42 : teṉmoḻiyil 30.
2.	vaṭamoḻiyolikaḷ valiyaṉa; teṉmoḻiyolikaḷ meliyaṉa.
3.	mūccolikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil maṭṭumuṇṭu.
4.	kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ teṉmoḻiyil illi.
5.	vaṭamoḻiyil uyirkaḷ (tīrkkam, kuṇam, viruttiyeṉa mūvakiyil) neṭilāka māṟip puṇarum; teṉmoḻiyil uṭaṉpaṭumey peṟṟup puṇarum.
6.	tamiḻil moḻi mutal iṭi kaṭi varāta eḻuttukaḷellām, vaṭamoḻiyil moḻi mutaliṭi kaṭi varum.
7.	iṭukuṟippeyar tamiḻukkilli.
8.	muṉṉiliyi uḷappaṭuttum taṉmip paṉmip peyar vaṭamoḻiyililli.
9.	uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉṟa pākupāṭu vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
10.	pālkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil āṇpāl, peṇpāl, alippāl eṉa mūṉṟu; avi īṟu paṟṟiyaṉa; tamiḻil āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉap pāl aintu; avi poruḷum eṇṇum paṟṟiyaṉa.
11.	irumi eṉṉum eṇ tamiḻil illi.
12.	mutal vēṟṟumikku urupu vaṭamoḻiyi luṇṭu; teṉmoḻiyil illi.
13.	kuṟippuviṉi vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
14.	vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peyareccamum peyar pōla vēṟṟumiyēṟkum.
15.	vaṭamoḻiyil varum muṉṉoṭṭuc (Prefix) coṟkaḷ tamiḻil piṉṉoṭṭuc (Suffix) coṟkaḷāyirukkum.
16.	taḻuvuñ collum nilimoḻiyum  vaṭamoḻiyil varu moḻiyā yiruppatuṇṭu. tamiḻil vaḻuvamitiyāyum aruki yumē aṅṅaṉam varum.
17.	viṉittoki vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
18.	tamiḻil mika mukkiyamākak karutappaṭum poruḷilakkaṇam vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
19.	veṇpā, āciriyappā mutaliya pākkaḷum ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu kaḷum iṉaṅkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
20.	iyal ici nāṭakameṉat tamiḻi mūṉṟākap pakuppatu pōla vaṭamoḻiyip pakuppatilli.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻi mēṉāṭṭariya moḻikaḷic cērntateṉ patik kīḻvarum coṟkaḷāl uṇarka.

6. `iru' eṉṉum viṉiccol

mēṉāṭṭāriya moḻikaṭkum vaṭamoḻikkum coṟkaḷil oppumi yiruppatupōlavē, ilakkaṇa neṟimuṟikaḷilum oppumi yirukkiṉṟatu.
pārppaṉar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vanta putitil, tamiḻic cariyāyp pēcamuṭiyavilli. ippōtuḷḷa mēṉāṭṭāriṉ tamiḻp pēccup pōṉṟē, akkālap pārppaṉar pēccum nakippi viḷivittatu. ataṉāl, tolkāppiya meyppāṭṭiyal 
(1-ām cū.) uriyil, āriyar kūṟum tamiḻi nakic cuvikkuk kāṭṭāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. kapilar, nacci ṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya tamiḻaṟiñar palar āriyap pārppaṉa rēṉum, avar oru ciṟu tokiyiṉarē yāvar. perumpāṟpaṭṭa pārppaṉar putitāy vantavarāyum tamiḻic cariyākak kallātavarāyum iruntamiyiṉ, tamiḻic cevviyāyap pēcamuṭivilli. mēṉāṭṭāruḷ peki, kālṭuvel, pōp, uviṉlō mutaliya pala ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñariruntatiyum, piṟarukku avarippōl tamiḻic cevviyāyp pēca muṭiyāmiyiyum mēṟkūṟiyatuṭaṉ oppiṭṭuk kāṇka.
tamiḻarkkum, vaṭamoḻi ataṉ ceyaṟkiyoli mikutipaṟṟi naṉṟāyk kaṟka varāmiyiṉ, "pārppāṉ tamiḻum vēḷāḷaṉ kirantamum vaḻavaḻa" eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu. itaṉālum, pārppaṉarum tamiḻarum vēṟāṉavar eṉpati yaṟiyalām.
āriyaṉ kaṇṭāy tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭāy.  (tēvā. 844:5)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatum ik karuttuppaṟṟiyē.
kulavaḻakku : pārppaṉar ippōtu naṉṟāyt tamiḻaṟintavarākak, karutappaṭiṉum, vaṭamoḻippaṟṟuk kāraṇamāka, iyaṉṟavari vaṭacoṟkaḷik kalantu maṇippavaḷa naṭiyiṟ pēcuvatē vaḻakkam, cāppāṭu, kāraṇamāka, aṭimāṉam, taṇṇīr, tiru viḻā, kuṭiyiruppu, kaliyāṇam mutaliya coṟkaḷukkup patilāka, muṟiyē pōjaṉam, viyājamāka, pōkkiyam, tīrttam, utcavam, vācam, vivākam mutaliya coṟkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarka ḷiṭattiṉiṉṟē tamiḻarum pala vaṭacoṟkaḷik kaṟṟut tam pēccil vēṇṭātu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilac coṟ kaḷit tamiḻiṟ kalantu pēcuvatu uyarveṉṟu tavaṟāy ippōtu eṇṇappaṭuvatu pōlavē, vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷik kalantu pēcuvatum muṟkālattil uyarvā yeṇṇappaṭṭatu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam pārppaṉat talimiyē.
ammāmi, ammāñci (ammāṉcēy), attimpēr (attiyaṉpar) mutaliya muṟippeyar vaṭivaṅkaḷum, cāsaṉam (paṭṭayam) tīrttam mutaliya poruṭpeyarkaḷum, avā pōyppaṭṭā, nēkku, pōṭṭuṇṭu, colṟēḷ (avarkaḷ pōyviṭṭārkaḷ, eṉakku, pōṭṭukkoṇṭu, colkiṟīrkaḷ) eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa kocci vaḻakkum, āṭavar peṇṭiruḷ mūttāriyum aṭī eṉṟa viḷippatum, pārppaṉat tamiḻp pēcciṟku oru taṉittaṉmi yūṭṭuvaṉavākum.
pārppaṉar cila taṉiccoṟkaḷāl taṅkaḷiyum taṅkaḷ poruḷkaḷiyum pirittuk kāṭṭuvatumuṇṭu.
u-m : 
maṅkalam, caturvēti maṅkalam	-	ūr
akkirākāram	-	kuṭiyiruppu
piramasrī	-	aṭimoḻi

piramarāyaṉ	-	aracappaṭṭam

paṭṭavirutti	-	vētaṅkaṟṟa pirā
		maṇaṉukku viṭṭa
		iṟiyili nilam
pārppaṉar tamiḻppaṟṟiṉmi, taṉittamiḻi virumpātati ṉālum, yūṉivarciṭṭi, kūl piṉal eṉṟu āṅkilac coṟ kaḷi moḻipeyarkkātu vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālum aṟiyappaṭum.
(5) uṟiyuḷ : 
pārppaṉar eṅkiruntālum tamiḻaroṭu kalavātu taṉittē uṟikiṉṟaṉar. akkirakārattil iṭam allatu vacatiyillāta poḻutē tamiḻar naṭuvil vantu kuṭiyiruppar. aṅṅaṉamiruppa varum perumpālum āṅkilam kaṟṟavarāyum aluvalāḷa rāyumē yiruppar. akkirakārattil ekkāraṇattiyiṭṭum tamiḻarukkuk kuṭiyirukka iṭantaruvatilli. palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ cuṭukāṭu kūṭap pārppaṉarukkut taṉiyāka vuḷatu.
(6) oḻukkam :
pārppaṉar tamiḻa muṟippaṭi, viruntōmpal, naṭuvunilimi mutaliya aṟaṅkaḷ iṉmiyāl illaṟattārumallar; kol lāmi, tuṟavu mutaliya aṟaṅkaḷiṉmiyil tuṟavaṟat tārumallar.
iruntōmpi yilvāḻva tellām viruntōmpi
vēḷāṇmi ceytaṟ poruṭṭu.  (kuṟaḷ. 81)
oruvar tam kulattārukku maṭṭuma viruntu ceyvatu virun tōmpalākātu.
vēḷviyil uyirik kolvatum koliyē.
ellārkkum potuvāṉa aṟaccālikaḷiṟkūṭap pārppaṉar uṇṭapiṟakē tamiḻar uṇṇavēṇṭu meṉṟa ēṟpāṭu iṉṟum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām Turanian, Semitic, Aryan eṉa mup perum pirivukaḷā yaṭaṅkum. avaṟṟuḷ āriyak kaviyil Teutonic kiḷikkut talimiyāṉatu vaṭamoḻi yeṉṉum camaṟkirutam; ēṉiya German, Dutch, English mutaliyaṉa.
mēṉāṭṭu moḻiyārāyccip pulavar, ilakkaṇa oṟṟumi yālum ilakkiyac coṟkaḷiṉ  oṟṟumiyālum vaṭamoḻiyic cerumaṉ moḻikku iṉamākak kaṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, intiya āriyarum ceru māṉiyarum oru kālattil orē vakup piṉarāy orēyiṭattil (mēlāciyā) vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum. carittira nūlkaḷellām perumpālum kiṟittu vukkup piṟpaṭṭaṉavātaliṉ, avarkku muṟpaṭṭa varalāṟukaḷiyel lām uṇartaṟku moḻinūlē ciṟanta karuviyākkiṉṟatu. oru vakuppāri iṉṉāreṉa uṇartaṟku avaratu pēcciṉum ciṟanta karuvi piṟitoṉṟilli.
nilattiṟ kiṭantami kālkāṭṭuṅ kāṭṭuṅ
kulattiṟ piṟantārvāyc col.  (kuṟaḷ. 959)
ākavē, intiya āriyar cerumāṉiyarkku iṉameṉṟum avar pēciya moḻiyum cerumaṉukku iṉameṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum. ati avviru moḻikaṭkum iṉṟiyamiyāta cila potuc coṟkaḷāṟ kāṭṭutum. iṅkilīṣ cerumaṉ moḻiyiṉiṉṟum piṟantatātaliṉ, kīḻvarum iṅkilīṣ coṟkaḷellām cerumaṉeṉṟē teḷika. (Jutes, Saxons, Angles eṉṟa mūvakic cerumāṉiya vakuppiṉarē ki.mu. 449-l cerumaṉiyiṉiṉṟum pirittāṉiyāvukkup pōy iṅkilīṣ moḻiyi vaḻaṅkiṉavar.)

iṉi ilakkaṇa oṟṟumikaḷāvaṉa :
1. mey moḻi mutalātal
2. orumi irumi paṉmi eṉa eṇ mūṉṟātal
3. peyareccaṅkaḷ pāl eṇ vēṟṟumikaḷik kāṭṭal
4. ilakkaṇap pāl (grammatical gender)
5. viṉimuṟṟukaḷ pāl kāṭṭāmi
6. payaṉili viṉiccollāyē yiruttal
ivi pōṉṟavi iṉṉum ettuṇiyō uḷa. mukkiya māṉavi maṭṭum iṅku utāraṇamākak kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āriyam eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu mutaṉmutal āriya moḻikaḷ ellā vaṟṟiṟkum potuvāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. piṉpu Teutonic vakuppukku maṭṭuma vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉpu intiya āriya moḻiyākiya camaṟkirutattiṟkē ciṟappāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. intiya āriyar nāl vakuppārāyiṉum, avarkaḷukkuḷ pirāmaṇar maṭṭum ik kuṟiyīṭṭit tamakkum tam moḻikkum vari yaṟuttukkoṇṭaṉar.
āriya vakuppu moḻikaḷuḷ Latin, Greek eṉṟa iraṇṭum muṟpaṭṭavi. Teutonic moḻikaḷ avaṟṟiṟku piṟpaṭṭavi. airōppiya aracukaṭkellām muntiya kirēkka carittiram ki.mu. 3000ām āṇṭiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ākavē, camaṟkiruta āriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉatum atē kālamāyttā ṉirukkavēṇṭum.
ulakil mutaṉmutal uṇṭāṉatu tamiḻ. tamiḻar (ki.mu. 8000) kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟum vaṭakkē pōyc ciṉṉa āciyāvil kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpatu pāpilōṉiyak kuki veṭṭālum kuḻi veṭṭālum teriya varukiṉṟatu. aṅkētāṉ tamiḻ cēmiyak (Semitic) kiḷiyākat tirintatu. epirēyam (Hebrew), arapi (Arabic) mutaliyaṉa cēmiyak kiḷi moḻikaḷ. avaṟṟiyaṭuttu Latin, Greek mutaliya āriyak kiḷi moḻikaḷ piṟantaṉa.  oru tāy moḻiyiṉiṉṟum oru kiḷimoḻi uṇṭāyiruppiṉ ataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta mutaṟ coṟkaḷellām tāymoḻiyā yirukkum. ēṉiyavellām ataṟkac contamāṉa ākkac coṟkaḷē. avaṟṟuḷ oru pakuti piṟamoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉ koṇṭa ticiccoṟkaḷāyirukkum. oru moḻiyiṉ ākkac coṟ perukkattiṟkum curukkattiṟkum kāraṇam am moḻiyi vaḻaṅkum makkaḷaṟivē yākum.
āriyak kiḷikaḷil oṉṟākiya Teutonic eṉpatu Greko-Latin eṉṟa peruṅkiḷiyiṉiṉṟum kiḷittapoḻutē (atāvatu camaṟ kiruta āriyar intiyāvukku varumuṉṉamē) atil nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kalantiruntaṉa. āriyarkaḷ intiyāvukku vantu tamiḻarōṭu (ki.mu.2500) kalantapiṉ eṇṇiṟanta teṉ coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalantaṉa. āriyar varumuṉṉum vanta poḻutum vaṭintiyāvil vaḻaṅkivantavi yellām koṭun tamiḻ moḻikaḷē.
āriya varukikku muṉ camaṟkirutattil allatu Teutonic moḻi kaḷiṟ kalantirunta teṉcoṟkaḷiṟ cila:

camaṟkirutattiṟku mutaṟkāviyam irāmāyaṇam. irāmar  iruntatu  iṭiccaṅka kālam. ākavē, tolkāppiyamum akattiyamum irāmāyaṇattiṟku muṟpaṭṭaṉavām. vaṭa moḻikku mutal ilakkaṇam pāṇiṉīyam. pāṇiṉi pāratattiṟ kum piṟpaṭṭavar. pāratakālam 1000 B.C.; irāmāyaṇa kālamō 2000 B.C.; ākavē, pāṇiṉīyam tolkāppi yattiṟkuk kuṟintatu āyiram āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. āriya varukikku muṉṉamē tamiḻar muttamiḻilum palkali kaḷilum tērntiruntaṉar. akattiyam vaḻinūlē yaṉṟi mutaṉulākātu. 
iṉi, vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷāvaṉa:
matam
mati + am = matam.
tēy + u = tēyvu - tēvu - tē - tī.
tēyvu + am - tēyvam - teyvam Gk. Theos.
tēyvam - tēvam - tēvaṉ.
teyvam eṉpatu mutalāvatu neruppiyum piṉpu civattiyuṅ kuṟikkum. (tiruvācaka virivuri kāṇka)
civa + m = civam - civaṉ - civi.
uru + tiram = uruttiram - uruttiram - rutraṉ = ciṉaṉ. 
uruttal = ciṉattal, tiram toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
ari + am = aram - araṉ.
ō - ōm - ōṅkāram or ōkāram.
ōveṉpatu mūla volikkuṟippu. ol, oli, ōlam, oci, ōti mutaliya ōcipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷellām ōkara ōkāra mutaṉ moḻikaḷātal kāṇka. rī eṉpatu rīṅkāramāṉāṟ pōla ō eṉpatu ōṅkāramāyiṟṟu.
uru + tiram + ākkam = uruttirākkam - ruttirākṣam (urutti rākka viḷakkaṅ kāṇka.)
vi + pūti = vipūti. puḻuti - pūti; vi, upacarkkam.
āṇavam : āḷ - āṇ - āṇmi - āṇavam.
māy + ai = māyi : ai. - to. pe.vi.
kāmi + am = kāmiyam, kām + iyam eṉṟum pirikkalām.
ṇiyam oru toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
kām + am = kāmam.
kām + ar = kāmar.
kumaraṉ, kumari - kommi (tiraṭci) eṉṉum paṇpiṉaṭiyākap piṟantavi. cf. virgin Gk. orago - to swell. cēy (zeus), murukaṉ, vēlaṉ eṉpaṉa kumaraṟku maṟupeyarkaḷ.
caṇṭiki - caḷ- caḷḷi - caṇṭi - caṇṭaṉ - caṇṭi - caṇṭiki.
kāḷi : kaḷ - kaḷḷam - kāḷam - kāḷi. - kaḷam - kaḷaṅkam = karumpu.
ciṉṉa : ciṉa + am = ciṉam - ciṉaṉ.
viṣṇu : viṇ - viṇṭu. viṇṇippōl nīlamāyum eṅkum niṟintumiruppavaṉ.
māyōṉ : mā - māy - māyōṉ. mā = karuppu.
kiruṣṇaṉ : kaḷ - kaḷḷaṉ - kaṇṇaṉ.
māyōṉ, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ kiruṣṇāvatārattiṟ putitāy uṇṭāṉavi yalla. ataṟku muntiyē vaḻaṅkiṉavi. "māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟi yulakamum" eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu kāṇka.
araṅkaṉ :- araṅkam - araṅkaṉ. araṅkam = āṟṟiṭikkuṟi.
irāmaṉ :- irāmam - irāmaṉ.
irāmam eṉpatu tirumaṇkāppu. irāmam = irēki vari. irāmak karumpu eṉṉum vaḻakku nōkkuka. vaṭanāṭṭil itit tavaṟāka nāmam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. irāmar oru viṇava paktar. irāmarukku muṉpē māl vaḻipāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭi liruntatu.
iraṇṭu, alampu, ilakku, ulāttu mutaliya coṟkaḷ uyirmutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkuvatupōla araṅkaṉ irāmaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷum uyir mutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nārāyaṇaṉ: nāram + ayaṉaṉ.
varuṇaṉ : vār +  aṇaṉ - vāraṇam - vāraṇaṉ - varuṇaṉ.
vār + i = vāri. vār + iti = vāriti.
ātmā : āṉ + mā - āṉmā. āṉ - pacu. mā perumip poruḷ vikuti. māṉ eṉpatu īṟu kuṟintu niṉṟateṉiṉum okkum. Gk. animos. vaṭamoḻiyil ātamā carīrattik kuṟikkum.
naraṉ : nari + aṉ = naraṉ = narippavaṉ.
pati : patikkut talivaṉ : iṭavāku peyar. pati = nakar. vīṭu.
pacu : pācattāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu pacu.
pācam : pacumi - pacci - pāci - pācam, pācam. pācam pōṟ piṇikkuṅ  kayiṟu: kayiṟupōṟ piṇikkum malam.
pācam eṉpataṟku āci yeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉum poruntum.
narakam : narakal - narakam.
cf. dungeon, from dung 
kāyam - ākāyam. kāyam = karuppu. `kāyap peyarvayiṉ' eṉṟu tolkāppiyattuḷ varutal kāṇka.
pūtam : potu - potukku - pontu - pūtam.
ulakam : ula + ku = ulaku. ulaku + am = ulakam.
ulattal = aḻital.
nīr + am = nīram - nāram - nār + am.
cali + am = calam. cali + aṉam = calaṉam. cali + ati = calati. calittal = acital. calippu eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkiyum acivu eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiyum oppu nōkkuka.
kālam - kāl + am = kālam. cami + am = camiyam. (viti mutaṉilit toḻilākupeyar.)
vāṉanūl
cūriyaṉ - cur - curaṉ - cūraṉ - cūriyaṉ. Gk. sol. cur eṉpatu oru vemmik kuṟippu.
cur + am = curam = kāyccal. pālinilam.
mati - kālatti matittaṟ kiṭamāṉatu.
mati + am = mātam cf. Moon. māṉi + am = māṉam.
matiyāl uṇṭāvatu mattam. cf. L. lunacy, from luna. 
tāraki - tār pōlvaṉa.
putaṉ : puttaṉ - putaṉ. putti - utti.
caṉi : cal  - calliyam - caṉi.
kōḷkaḷ ēḻēyaṉṟi oṉpatākā. āyiṉ nāḷkaḷum ēḻaṉṟi oṉpatāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟika.
irāci = (race) kūṭṭam.
mēṣam : mēḻakam - mēḻam - mēṣam.
riṣapam : viṭi - viṭali - viṭapam - iṭapam.
ā + viṭi = āviṭi. viṭittiruppatu viṭi.
cf. kuṇṭāyiruppatu kuṇṭi.
kōḻiviṭi eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkik kāṇka.
kumpam : kum - kumi - kumpam. kumpal = kuvital.
kumpu + iṭu = kumpiṭu. kumpu + al = kumpal.
kumpu + am = kumpam.
kumpu + aṉi = kumpaṉi (company).
kumpu - kūmpu. kumi - kuvi. kumpu - kuppu. kumi + ḻ = kumiḻ.
kuppu + ai = kuppi. kuppu + al = kuppal.
mīṉam : miṉ - mīṉ - mīṉam.
kaṉṉi: kaṉ + i = kaṉṉi. kaṉṉutal = paḻuttal.
cf. maturity, from mature - to ripen.
tulām: tāl - tālam - tuli - tulām.
ciṅkam: ciṅku + am = ciṅkam, aḻippatu.
ciṅkal = kuṟital, aḻital.
kaṟkaṭakam:- kal + kaṭakam = kaṟkaṭakam, kalnaṇṭu
vaṭavi: vaṭam + ai = vaṭavi, vaṭakkuḷḷa neruppu
c.f. Aurora Borealis, the northern light.
aurora - fire; borealis - northern.
vaṭavi + mukam = vaṭavimukam. itip peṭavā mukam eṉat tirittup peṭṭik kutiri eṉṟu poruḷurippar.
vaṭavik kaṉalip piḻinteṭuttu  -vīrarākavar
akkaṭaliṉmītu vaṭavaṉal niṟkaviliyō  -tāyumāṉavar
pūkōḷam
kōḷam: kuḻu - kōḷam - kōli.
maṇṭalam: maṇ + talam = maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam.
pūmi vaṭṭamāyiruppatu tamiḻarkkut terintiruntatu.
iṭi yeṉṉum uṟuppuppeyar amcāriyi peṟṟu iṭamā ṉāṟ pōla, tali yeṉṉum uṟuppuppeyar amcāriyi peṟṟut talamāyiṟṟu. iṭappeyarkaḷ perumpālum ciṉippeyarkaḷākavē yirukkum.
kaṇkāl puṟamakam uḷḷuḻi kīḻmēl
piṉcār ayalpuṭi tēvaki eṉāa
muṉṉiṭi kaṭitali valamiṭam eṉāa
aṉṉa piṟavum ataṉpāla eṉmaṉār.  (tol.col.vēṟ.20)
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam. maṇṭalittal - vaṭṭamiṭutal.
tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilam = tiṅkaḷ vaṭṭam.
kaṇṭam : kaṇ - kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam.
tīvu: tīr - u = tīrvu - tīvu. tarip pākattiṉiṉṟum tīrttiruppatu. itit tvīyam eṉṟu tirittu iru pakkattil nīrāl cūḻap paṭṭiruppateṉṟu poruḷurippar.
ilakkaṇam :
ilakku + aṇam = ilakkaṇam - lakṣaṇam.
ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam - lakṣiyam.
ilakkittal = eḻututal (cīvaka. 180)
cf. grammer from Gk. gramma, a letter, Gk. grapho, to write literature from letter.
karam, kāram, kāṉ, ēṉam - tamiḻ eḻuttuc cāriyikaḷ.
āytam : āy + tam = āytam. āytal = ārital.
māttiri: mā + tiri = māttiri. māttal = aḷattal. mā + tiram = māttiram.
mā eṉpatu ōr eṇṇaḷavukkum oru parappaḷavukkum peyar.
pakuti : paku + ti = pakuti - pāti. paku - vaku.
paku + am = pakkam = pakṣam  - pākam. pakkam - pāṅku - pāṅkaṉ.
paku + al = pakkal - pakal - pāl.
paku + ai = paki; paku - pā; pāttīṭu - pātīṭu.
pā + ti = pātti. pakuti - pirakruti.
mūlam: muḷ - muḷi - mūlam.
vikuti : viku + ti = vikuti. viku - vī. vītal = muṭital.
vikuti - vikruti.
cūttiram: cū + tiram = cūttiram, carukkam.
vaṇṇam: paṇ - paṇṇam - vaṇṇam - vaṇṇakam.
cantam: - olikkuṟippup paṟṟiyatu. cantaṭi eṉpatu vaḻakku.
paṇṇatti: paṇ + atti = paṇṇatti.
urupu:  uru + pu = urupu; uru + u = uruvu. uruvu + am = uruvam. uruttal, tōṉṟutal.
uru x aru. cf. uṟu x aṟu. uruvam from rūpam.
kāraṇam: karu - karuvi - karaṇam kāraṇam - kāriyam.
karu - karuppu - karuppam. mēkam cūlkoṇṭa piṉ karuttiruttal kāṇka.
ētu: ētu eṉṟu viṉavuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruppatu.
vf. the why; the how.
ampōtaraṅkam: ampu + taraṅkam.
am - ampu; taraṅku + am = taraṅkam, kariyik kuttuvatu.
uvami: uva + mi - uvami. uvattal = virupputal. uvama vurupukaḷ perumpālum viruppu veṟuppup poruḷkaḷilēyē varum. uvami - upamāṉam.
e-ṭu: nāṭa = virumpa. pukala - pōla. pukalal - virumpal.
vf. like, similar, to be pleased with.
uruvakam: uru + akam = uruvakam. oṉṟi maṟṟōr uruvākac collutal. uruvakam - rūpakam.
ici, nāṭakam
curam: curi + am = curam. curi = tuḷi. nākattiṟkup piriyamāṉatu nākacuram.
toṉi: toḷ - toḷi - toṇṭi - toṉi.
irākam: arākam - irākam. kalippā vuṟuppu.
tāṇṭavam: tāḷ - tāṇṭu - tāṇṭavam.
naṭam - naṭa - naṭi - naṭam - naṭṭam.
naṭi + am - naṭam.
naṭi + akam = nāṭakam.
maṇṭalam = mandoline. curavīṇi - saraphina.

aracu
ari - aram - aravu - aracu - aracaṉ, Gk. archon.
aricaṉ - ariyaṉ -  Roy, Royal.
maraṅkaṭkuḷ aracu pōṉṟiruppatu aracamaram.
cēraṉ: cāral - cēral - cēraṉ - cēralaṉ - kēraḷaṉ.
cōḻaṉ: cōḷam - cōḻam - cōḻaṉ.
kaḷḷarveṭṭi (makkā)c cōḷam cōḻanāṭṭiltāṉ ciṟappāy viḷikiṟatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ: pāṇṭi + aṉ = pāṇṭiyaṉ, vīraṉ.
pāṇṭi = vaṭṭam. pāṇṭil = erutu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ kāḷi pōṉṟavaṉ.
pāṇṭavar campantappaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟu Caldwell  kūṟiyiruppatu tavaṟu. pāṇṭiyaṉ peyar pāratattiṟku 1000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa vālmīki irāmāyaṇattiṟ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
cāmam: camam - cāmam.
pētam: pēti + am = pētam.
tāṉam: tā + aṉam = tāṉam.
taṇṭam: taṇṭi - taṇṭu - taṇṭam.
taṇṭi + aṉi = taṇṭaṉi. taṇṭi - taṭi.
taṭittal - paruttal. taṭital = veṭṭutal.
mutal taṇṭaṉi taṭiyālaṭippatā yiruntatu.
ciṉippeyarkaḷ
mukam: mukar - muka + m = mukam. mukattal. muka - mō.
mukar - nukar. mukam - nukam. mukam - mūkku - mūñci - mukaṭu.
muka + pu = mukappu. muka + vi. mukavi.
mukam mutalāvatu mūkkiṉ peyarāyiruntu piṉpu tāṉiyāku peyarāy mukattiṟkāyiṟṟu. vaṭamoḻiyil vāyi maṭṭum kuṟikkum.
irattam: arattam - irattam. arakku, alattakam iratti eṉpavi cenniṟattik kuṟikkum.
pittu + am = pittam - pittiyam.
karu + pu = karuppu. karuppu + am - karuppam.
moḻi - muḻam - muṭṭu - muṭṭi - muṣṭi pati + am = pātam.
ciṉip peyarkaḷ
vaṭivam = paṭi - paṭivu - vaṭivam, body, piratimā.
paṭi - vaṭi - vaṭivu - vaṭivam.
vaṭṭam: vaḷ + tam = vaṭṭam. vaḷ + i = vaḷi.
vaḷ + ti = vaṭṭi - vaṇṭi vaḷ + am = vaḷḷam.
vaḷ + tu = vaṭṭu - vaṇṭu = vaḷiyal.
vaḷ + ti = vaṭṭi - vaṭi. vaḷi +am = vaḷiyam.
vaḷ + ai = vaḷi. vaḷi + al - vaḷiyal.
vaḷiyam vaṭamoḻiyil valayam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. vaṭṭam viruttattiṉ tiripaṉṟu.
ticip peyarkaḷ
tikku + ai = tiki. tikittaṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruppatu.
tiki - tici.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭam - kuṇam - kuṇakku.
kuṭam: kuṭi - kuṭam. katiravaṉ vaḷiyumiṭam - kuṭakku.
uttaram: u + taram = uttaram. u = mēl, uyarvu.
teṟku + aṇam - teṟkaṇam - tekkaṇam - takkaṇam. tek kaṇam - Deccan. cf. koṅku + aṇam = koṅkaṇam.
eṇṇup peyarkaḷ
ilakkam = eṇ. ip potup peyarē nūṟāyirattukkuc ciṟappup peyarāyiṟṟu. lakh - lakṣam.
eṭṭu - aṣṭam.
kōṭi - kōṭṭi. kōṭi = kaṭici yeṇ.
nāṉā - nāl - nālā - nāṉā.
palvakic coṟkaḷ
tamiḻam - trāviṭam.
vēṭṭi: veṭṭuvatu vēṭṭi. vēṭṭi - vēṣṭi.
cf.  tuṇippatu tuṇi; aṟuppatu aṟuvi.
muṭṭi - muṭṭiyāṟ ceyyum pōr. muṭṭi - muṣṭi.
vf. wrestle from wrist.
kākam - kākkā - kākki kākam.
mayil - mayūram. kūki - kūkam.
kali - kal + ai. paṭi + am - pāṭam.
cuṟṟuru - cūtram. kali + yāṇam = kaliyāṇam.
kalittal = taḻittal. yāṇam = putumi. putuvaruvāy.
paḻam - palam. paḻu + am = paḻam. aṭavi - aṭarntiruppatu.
pari + am = pāram - pāri. tari + am = tāram.
tarukku + am = tarukkam - mikuttuk kāṭṭutal.
kuṭi - kuṭil - kuṭilam - kuṭīram. cāy + ai = cāyi.
i - itam = ippaṭi. pavaḷam - pravāḷam. nīl + am = nīlam.
iṉi, akkā, attā, āṇi, ammā, ampā, aṭavi, āḷi, kaṭu, kāvēri, kucam, kūccam, kuṭi, kūṉ, kuḷam, kōṭṭi, kaṭṭil, cām, cā, paṭṭaṇam, poṉ, paḷḷi, veḷ mutaliyaṉa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya teṉcoṟ kaḷām. iṉṉum ivi pōṉṟa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻivāyp paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷatellām vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟu moḻi nūluṇarātār cilar koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. mūla mutaṉmoḻi yākiya tamiḻ tavira vēṟu enta moḻiyāvatu piṟacoṟka ḷillātatilli. oru colli iṉṉa moḻiyeṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakutip poruḷiyum ataṉiṉṟum tirinta palvēṟu coṟkaḷiyum pārkkavēṇṭum. avi enta moḻi yiluḷḷaṉavō anta moḻikkē atu uriyatām. viṉic collāyiṉ puṭipeyarcci (Conjugation) vēṇṭum. peyarc collāyiṉ peyarilakkaṇam (Declension) vēṇṭum kal eṉṉum viṉi kaṟṟāṉ, kaṟṟāḷ, kaṟṟār, kaṟṟatu, kaṟṟa, kaṟka, kaṟpittāṉ, kaṟkappaṭṭatu, kaṟṟilaṉ, kali eṉap pala vaṭivukaḷilum puṭipeyara vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyilō atu kalā (kali) eṉat toḻilākupeyarāy maṭṭum niṟkum. iḵtōr utāraṇam.
cila vaṭamoḻip paṟṟuṭiyār muṉivar eṉṉum collukku muṉi eṉpatu pakutiyeṉ ṟaṟiyāmal, mavuṉam ceypavar muṉivar eṉṟu vaṭamoḻivaḻi yurippar. `aiyaṉ' eṉṉum tamiḻ muṟip peyari āriyaṉ eṉpatiṉ citivākak kūṟuvar. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉic coṟkaḷukkum avar vaṭamoḻiyik kaṟpippatuṇṭu.
oru vakuppārāvatu piṟa vakuppāruṭaṉ iyipiṉṟit tāmāy ulakattil vantavarallar. aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ tavira oru moḻiyā vatu piṟamoḻiyiṉ kalappiṉṟit tāṉāy vantataṉṟu eṉṟu teḷika.  ("centamiḻc celvi" kaṭakam 1931)
vaṭamoḻit teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām oru perun tāymoḻiyi ṉiṉṟum utittavi yeṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūl (History), kulanūl (Ethnology), moḻinūl (Philology) mutaliya pala nūlkaḷālum valiyuṟuttappaṭum. āyiṉum, ticiccol vakiyil oru vakuppār putiyaṉa vāy ākkik koṇṭaṉavum tāymoḻiyi ṉiṉṟum putiyaṉavāyt tirittukkoṇṭaṉavu māṉa iruvakic coṟkaḷ ovvoru moḻikkum ciṟappākum. iv viruvakic coṟkaḷum oru kiḷimoḻikkuriya ataṉ tāymoḻiyic cārum; oru tāymoḻik kuriyavi oru perun tāy moḻiyic cārum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi nōkkat tamiḻ oru tāymoḻi yum, āriya moḻikaḷi nōkkat tamiḻ oru perun tāymoḻiyu mākum.
eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭamiyālum, āriyac coṟkaḷil pala urut teriyātavāṟu attuṇit tirintuḷḷati ṉālum, pala vaṭacoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ciṟappuc coṟkaḷākavē tōṉṟāniṟkum. ēṉic coṟkaḷi itu pōtuḷḷa teṉ coṟ ṟuṇikoṇṭē teṉcolleṉak kāṭṭal moḻi nūlāṉ muṭṭiṉṟi muṭivatākum. iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷāyiṉ iv varampiṟ kuṭpaṭā.
oru colli oru moḻik kuriyateṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakuti (stem) allatu mūlam (root) emmoḻiyiluḷḷa teṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum.
oru colliṉ pakuti allatu mūlam palamoḻiyi liruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil, pakuti iyaṟkiyāṉateṉṟum, poruḷ ciṟanta teṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
pala moḻiyilum, pakuti vaṭivilum poruḷilum ottiruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil ataṉ tiricoṟkaḷuḷa veṉṟum, aviyum pala moḻiyiliruppiṉ, etil avi kaḻipalaveṉṟum kāṇṭal vēṇṭum.
ettuṇiyō teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉṟu paṉ moḻip pēraṟiñaruṅkūṭap pakarntu varukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu:
akaṅkāram. akam + kāram. akam - avvulaka, vāṉulaku. mōkṣam. akaraccuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟantatu: cēymiyiluḷḷa vāṉulakattik kuṟippatu; ampar eṉṉum coṟpōla. a - ampar - amparam. ikarac cuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟanta ikam, impar eṉṉum  coṟkaḷ aṇmic cuṭṭāy ivvulakattik kuṟittal kāṇka.
viṇṇulakiyum maṇṇulakiyum cēymi aṇmipaṟṟi avvulakam ivvulakam eṉpatu marapu.
aruḷillārk kavvulaka milli poruḷillārk
kivvulaka millāki yāṅku  (kuṟaḷ. 247)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum.
vīṭu eṉṉum muttip peyar illatti uṇarttiṉāṟpōla. akam eṉṉum peyarum illatti uṇarttum vīṭu = viṭutali: viṭuvatu vīṭu. piṟavit tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutali ceyvatu pēriṉpa vīṭu. veyiṉ maḻit tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutali ceyvatu ivvulaka vīṭu.
il eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyar  ēḻām vēṟṟumi urupāy uṭpākattik kuṟittāṟ pōla, akam eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyarum uṭpākattiyum uḷḷattiyum kuṟikkum `akameṉ kiḷavi' eṉat taṉittamiḻ ilakkaṇamākiya tolkāppiyat tuḷḷum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
kāram: kari eṉṉum viṉiyaṭiyāp piṟanta toḻiṟpeyar. kari + am = kāram: mutaṉili tirintu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
cf: paṭi + am = pāṭam. tavi + am = tāvam.
karittal mikutal. kāram mikuti.
kari eṉpatu kaṭi eṉṉum uriccolliṉ tiripu.
ṭa-ra, pōli. o.nō: paṭavar  - varavar. mukaṭi - mukari.
uppukkarikkum, uppuk kaṭukkum eṉpaṉa oruporuḷpaṟṟiya ulaka vaḻakku. kaṭi eṉṉum uriccoṟ poruḷkaḷuḷ mikutiyum oṉṟu.
kāram eṉṉum col mutalāvatu mikutiyi uṇarttip piṉpu uṟippu mikutiyi uṇarttum.
kari eṉṉum collē ati eṉṉum upacarkkam (prefix) peṟṟu atikari eṉa niṟkum. atikarippatu atikāram.
akam karippatu akaṅkāram. akaṅkāram - āṅkāram.
ati eṉpatu oru mikutip poruḷupacarkkam (intensive prefix).
atikāratti (āṇi mikutiyi or āṟṟaṉ mikutiyi) uṭiyavaṉ atikāri.
oru nūliṉ perumpākam atikāram.
akam karippatu akaṅkāram. akaṅkāram - āṅkāram.
akaṇṭam: a + kaṇṭam.  a. etirmaṟi upacarkkam: al eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. al eṉṉum etirmaṟic collē un eṉṟu āriyamoḻikaḷil tiriyum. il eṉpatu il, in, im, eṉṟu tiriyum.
kaṇṭam: kaṇ - kaṇu - kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam.
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭi. cf: tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi. taṇṭu - taṇṭi - taṭi.
kaṇpōṉṟu iruppatu kaṇu.
kaṇukkaṇuvāy naṟukkuvatu kaṇṭu.
kaṇṭu eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu kaṇṭi eṉṉum viṉiyuṇ ṭākum. kaṇṭittal kaṇukkaṇuvāy allatu tuṇṭutuṇṭāy veṭṭutalum, aṅṅaṉam veṭṭutalpōṟ kaṭumoḻi kūṟalum.
kaṇṭippatu kaṇṭam. kaṇṭi + am = kaṇṭam. cf. tuṇṭi + am = tuṇṭam. taṇṭi + am = taṇṭam.
kaṇṭi eṉṉum col iṭik kuṟintu kaṭi eṉṟum niṟkum.  cf. taṇṭi - taṭi
kaṇṭam eṉpataṉ nīṭṭal kāṇṭam. cf. karaṇam - kāraṇam. kaṇṭam = tuṇṭu, pākam, pirivu.
kaṇṭam eṉpatu catippākam nilappākaṅkaḷiyum, kāṇṭam (canto) eṉpatu nūṟpākaṅkaḷiyum kuṟikkum.
Canto eṉṉum āṅkilac colli cano (to sing) eṉṉum ilattīṉ   viṉiyaṭip piṟappākak koḷvatu ciṟantataṉṟu.
akati: a - al eṉpataṉ kaṭikkuṟi.
kati - celavu. kaṭa eṉpataṉ tiripu.
katittal - cellutal. kati + am = katam.
irākkatam - irāc celavu.
irākkataṉ - irācceṉṟu uṇavu tērpavaṉ.
irākkataṉ - rākṣasaṉ (Transliteration)
 nici = iravu: caram - acivu, celavu.
akanti : akam - akanti, akattiṉ mikuti.
akam = maṉam. cf. kuṭam - kuṭanti  (kumpakōṇam). uṭam - uṭanti. cf. uḷḷam = ūkkam. uḷḷattiṉ mikuti.
akarāti: akaram + āti. akaram - karam cāriyi.
āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭumuṉṉum, tamiḻil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaḻinūlkaḷē akattiyamum tolkāppiyamum. karam, kāram mutaliya eḻuttuc  cāriyikaḷ mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaṭ killi. āti potuccol.
akāraṇam: a + kāraṇam.
karaṇam - karaṇam. karu + aṇam = karaṇam.
karuttal - mēkam karuttalik kuṟikkum.
karu - kār. mēkam cūl koḷḷumpōtu karuttiruttiliṉ makkaḷiṉ cūlum karu. karuppam eṉappaṭum.
mēkam karuttu maḻipoḻivatu ulaka viṉikaṭkellām kāraṇamā yiruttaliṉ karu eṉpatu kāraṇattiyum kuṟikkum.
vāṉiṉ ṟulakam vaḻaṅki varutalāṉ.  (kuṟaḷ.11)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum.
karaṇam, karuvi eṉpaṉa karu eṉṉum oruviṉit toḻiṟ peyarkaḷ. tirikaraṇam = mukkaruvi. antakkaraṇam - uṭkaruvi. karaṇam aṇam vikuti. karuvi - vi vikuti. karaṇattiṉ nīṭṭam karaṇam. karuvi mēkattiṉ tokutiyiyum uṇarttum; e-ṭu: `karuvi māmaḻi', `karuvi tokuti' - tolkāppiyam. karu - kār. kār + aṇam = kāraṇam. kār + iyam = kāriyam. iyam toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
akālam. a + kālam.
kālam: kāl+am=kālam. am cāriyi. kāl uṭampiṉ kāṟpākamākiya uṟuppu.
kāl = 1. leg 
2. tūṇ, kāl pōlvatu
3. canal - kālpōl nīṇṭōṭuvatu
4. vamcam - kālpōlat toṭarvatu
5.  kāṟṟu - kālpōlac ceṉṟu vīcuvatu
6. time - kālpōlac ceṉṟu vīcuvatu
aṅkuṭṭam. am + kuṭṭam.
am = aḻaku. kuṭṭam - kuṭṭiyāṉa viral.
acaram. a + caram.
cari + am = caram. toḻiṟpeyar.
carital = acital, cāytal.
cali - cari; pōli eṉiṉum okkum.
caram aciyum poruḷik kuṟikkumpōtu toḻilāku peyar.
acalam. a + calam = acalam = mali. aciyātatu.
cali + am = calam. toḻiṟpeyar.
calittal - acittal.
calam - nīr. calippatu. toḻilāku peyar. 
calam eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭil nīrpporuḷkaḷil oṉṟākiya cīḻukku vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
calam eṉṉum teṉcolliyē jalam eṉat tirittaṉar āriyarum tamiḻarum.
`jeṭṭiyār kaṭikku jeṉṟu jēlam jīṉi vāṅki vā' eṉṟu iṉṟikkum tamiḻmakkaḷ teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭacoṟpōlat tirittu vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. calippu eṉpatu kaḷippu eṉṟa poruḷpaṭṭāṟ pōla, ataṉ pariyāyac collākiya acivu eṉpatum kaḷippu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.
iḷivē iḻavē acivē vaṟumiyeṉa
viḷivil koḷki aḻuki nāṉkē.  (tol. 199)

añcali. añcal-añcali.

atikam. ati-ati eṉṉum colliṉ tiripu.

atimaturam. ati + maturam = atimaturam, oru carakku.

vilaṅkukaḷilum, ciṟappāyp paṟavikaḷilum iḷamiyāyp paruttiruppavaṟṟi viṭi eṉpatu vaḻakku. viṭi - viṭit tiruppatu mutaṉilit toḻilākupeyar.
viṭittal-paruttal.

cf. vālam-vālipam.
viṭapam eṉpatu mutaṉmey nīṅki iṭapam eṉṟu niṟkum.
cf. cami - ami. vaḷi-aḷi.
viṭi eṉpatu viṭali eṉṟum tiriyum. viṭi eṉṉum peyarum ataṉ tiripukaḷum pala vilaṅkiṟkum potuvēṉum, ciṟappāka māṭṭiṟkāḷaṅkaṉṟiyē kuṟippatu nūṉmarapu. tōṭuṭiyaceviyaṉ viṭiyēṟi. kāḷi eṉṉum peyar uvamiyāku peyarāy ōr iḷiñaṉi allatu vīraṉi uṇarttiṉāṟpōla. ataṉ pariyāyap peyarākiya viṭi eṉpa tum ataṉ tiripukaḷum iḷiñari allatu vīrari uṇarttum.
pālimakkaḷāṉa maṟavariṉ talivaṉ viṭali eṉappaṭuvatu kāṇka.
ōr iḷiñaṉi viṭalippiyaṉ eṉpatum, iḷaṅkōḻi kaḷic cēvalviṭi kōḻiviṭi eṉpatum iṉṟum teṉ ṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
viṭali eṉṉum kaṉṟuppeyar ilattīṉ moḻiyil vitula eṉṟu tiriyum.
